package org.example.smashhub.auth.controller;

import com.nimbusds.jose.JOSEException;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.AuthRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.ForgotPasswordRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.IntrospectRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.LogoutRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.RefreshTokenRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.ResendOtpRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.ResetPasswordRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.VerifyAccountRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.response.AuthResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.response.IntrospectResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.AuthService;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.JwtService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.user.dto.response.UserResponse;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.text.ParseException;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/auth")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class AuthController {

    AuthService authService;
    JwtService jwtService;

    @PostMapping("/login")
    ApiResponse<AuthResponse> login(@RequestBody @Valid AuthRequest request) {
        return ApiResponse.<AuthResponse>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .result(authService.authenticate(request))
                .message("login successfully")
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/introspect")
    ApiResponse<IntrospectResponse> introspect(@RequestBody IntrospectRequest request) throws ParseException, JOSEException {
        return ApiResponse.<IntrospectResponse>builder()
                .result(jwtService.introspect(request))
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/logout")
    ApiResponse<Void> logout(@RequestBody LogoutRequest request) throws ParseException, JOSEException {
        authService.logout(request);
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .message("logout successfully")
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/refresh-token")
    ApiResponse<AuthResponse> refreshToken(@RequestBody @Valid RefreshTokenRequest refreshTokenRequest)
            throws ParseException, JOSEException {
        return ApiResponse.<AuthResponse>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .result(authService.refreshToken(refreshTokenRequest))
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/verify-account")
    ApiResponse<UserResponse> verifyAccount(@RequestBody @Valid VerifyAccountRequest request) {
        return ApiResponse.<UserResponse>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .result(authService.verifyAccount(request.getEmail(), request.getOtp()))
                .message("Account verified successfully")
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/resend-otp")
    ApiResponse<Void> resendOtp(@RequestBody @Valid ResendOtpRequest request) {
        authService.resendOtp(request.getEmail());
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .message("OTP has been resent to your email")
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/forgot-password")
    ApiResponse<Void> forgotPassword(@RequestBody @Valid ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        authService.forgotPassword(request);
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .message("OTP has been sent to your email")
                .build();
    }

    @PostMapping("/reset-password")
    ApiResponse<Void> resetPassword(@RequestBody @Valid ResetPasswordRequest request) {
        authService.resetPassword(request);
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder()
                .code(1000)
                .message("Password reset successfully")
                .build();
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import lombok.*;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;

@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE)
public class AuthRequest {
    @NotBlank(message = "email must not be blank")
    @Email(message = "email must be a valid email address")
    String email;

    @NotBlank(message = "password must not be blank")
    String password;
}

package org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import lombok.*;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;

@Data
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@Builder
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE)
public class ResetPasswordRequest {
    @NotBlank(message = "email must not be blank")
    @Email(message = "email must be a valid email address")
    String email;

    @NotBlank(message = "otp must not be blank")
    @Pattern(regexp = "^[0-9]{6}$", message = "otp must be 6 digits")
    String otp;

    @NotBlank(message = "newPassword must not be blank")
    String newPassword;

    @NotBlank(message = "confirmNewPassword must not be blank")
    String confirmNewPassword;
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\auth\service\impl\AuthServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.auth.service.impl;

import com.nimbusds.jose.JOSEException;
import com.nimbusds.jwt.SignedJWT;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.AuthRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.ForgotPasswordRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.LogoutRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.RefreshTokenRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.ResetPasswordRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.response.AuthResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.AuthService;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.JwtService;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.LoginAttemptService;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.OtpService;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.TokenBlacklistService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OtpPurpose;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.Status;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.notification.service.EmailService;
import org.example.smashhub.user.dto.request.ChangePasswordRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.user.dto.response.UserResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.user.entity.User;
import org.example.smashhub.user.mapper.UserMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.user.repository.UserRepository;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.text.ParseException;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Date;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class AuthServiceImpl implements AuthService {

    UserRepository userRepository;
    UserMapper userMapper;
    OtpService otpService;
    EmailService emailService;
    PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    JwtService jwtService;
    LoginAttemptService loginAttemptService;
    TokenBlacklistService tokenBlacklistService;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public AuthResponse authenticate(AuthRequest request) {
        String email = request.getEmail();

        if (loginAttemptService.isLocked(email)) {
            long remainingSeconds = loginAttemptService.getLockoutSecondsRemaining(email);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ACCOUNT_TEMPORARILY_LOCKED,
                    String.format(ErrorCode.ACCOUNT_TEMPORARILY_LOCKED.getMessage(), remainingSeconds));
        }

        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.USER_NOT_EXISTED));

        if (user.getStatus() == Status.LOCKED)
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ACCOUNT_LOCKED);

        boolean authenticated = passwordEncoder.matches(request.getPassword(), user.getPassword());

        if (!authenticated) {
            boolean justLocked = loginAttemptService.registerFailure(email);
            if (justLocked) {
                long remainingSeconds = loginAttemptService.getLockoutSecondsRemaining(email);
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_ATTEMPT_EXCEEDED,
                        String.format(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_ATTEMPT_EXCEEDED.getMessage(), remainingSeconds));
            }
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.INCORRECT_PASSWORD);
        }

        loginAttemptService.reset(email);

        String token = jwtService.generateToken(user);
        return AuthResponse.builder()
                .token(token)
                .authenticated(true)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void logout(LogoutRequest request) throws ParseException, JOSEException {
        try {
            SignedJWT signedJWT = jwtService.verifyToken(request.getToken());
            String jti = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getJWTID();
            Date expiryTime = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getExpirationTime();
            tokenBlacklistService.blacklist(jti, expiryTime);
        } catch (AppException e) {
            log.info("Token already expired, logout skipped.");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public AuthResponse refreshToken(RefreshTokenRequest request) throws ParseException, JOSEException {
        SignedJWT signedJWT = SignedJWT.parse(request.getToken());
        String jti = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getJWTID();
        Date expiryTime = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getExpirationTime();

        tokenBlacklistService.blacklist(jti, expiryTime);
        String username = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getSubject();
        User user = userRepository.findByUsername(username)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.USER_NOT_EXISTED));

        return AuthResponse.builder()
                .authenticated(true)
                .token(jwtService.generateToken(user))
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    //@Transactional
    public void sendVerificationOtp(User user) {
        String otp = otpService.generateOtp(OtpPurpose.REGISTER, user.getEmail());
        emailService.sendOtpEmail(user.getEmail(), otp);
        otpService.markCooldown(OtpPurpose.REGISTER, user.getEmail());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void resendOtp(String email) {
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_NOT_EXISTED));

        if (user.getStatus() == Status.ACTIVE)
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_ALREADY_VERIFIED);

        if (otpService.isInCooldown(OtpPurpose.REGISTER, email))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.OTP_RESEND_TOO_SOON);

        sendVerificationOtp(user);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public UserResponse verifyAccount(String email, String otp) {
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_NOT_EXISTED));

        if (user.getStatus() == Status.ACTIVE)
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_ALREADY_VERIFIED);

        if (!otpService.isValid(OtpPurpose.REGISTER, email, otp))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.OTP_INVALID);
        user.setStatus(Status.ACTIVE);
        user.setEmailVerifiedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        otpService.invalidate(OtpPurpose.REGISTER, email);
        return userMapper.toUserResponse(userRepository.save(user));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void forgotPassword(ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        String email = request.getEmail();
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_NOT_EXISTED));

        if (otpService.isInCooldown(OtpPurpose.RESET_PASSWORD, email))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.OTP_RESEND_TOO_SOON);

        String otp = otpService.generateOtp(OtpPurpose.RESET_PASSWORD, email);
        emailService.sendPasswordResetOtpEmail(email, otp);
        otpService.markCooldown(OtpPurpose.RESET_PASSWORD, email);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void resetPassword(ResetPasswordRequest request) {
        if (!request.getNewPassword().equals(request.getConfirmNewPassword()))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_CONFIRM_NOT_MATCH);

        String email = request.getEmail();
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_NOT_EXISTED));

        if (!otpService.isValid(OtpPurpose.RESET_PASSWORD, email, request.getOtp()))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.OTP_INVALID);

        user.setPassword(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getNewPassword()));
        otpService.invalidate(OtpPurpose.RESET_PASSWORD, email);
        userRepository.save(user);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void changePassword(String email, ChangePasswordRequest request) {
        if (!request.getNewPassword().equals(request.getConfirmNewPassword()))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_CONFIRM_NOT_MATCH);

        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.EMAIL_NOT_EXISTED));

        if (!passwordEncoder.matches(request.getCurrentPassword(), user.getPassword()))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.INCORRECT_PASSWORD);

        if (passwordEncoder.matches(request.getNewPassword(), user.getPassword()))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PASSWORD_SAME_AS_OLD);

        user.setPassword(passwordEncoder.encode(request.getNewPassword()));
        userRepository.save(user);
    }
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\auth\service\impl\JwtServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.auth.service.impl;

import com.nimbusds.jose.JOSEException;
import com.nimbusds.jose.JWSAlgorithm;
import com.nimbusds.jose.JWSHeader;
import com.nimbusds.jose.JWSObject;
import com.nimbusds.jose.JWSVerifier;
import com.nimbusds.jose.Payload;
import com.nimbusds.jose.crypto.MACSigner;
import com.nimbusds.jose.crypto.MACVerifier;
import com.nimbusds.jwt.JWTClaimsSet;
import com.nimbusds.jwt.SignedJWT;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.experimental.NonFinal;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.IntrospectRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.response.IntrospectResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.JwtService;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.TokenBlacklistService;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.user.entity.User;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.text.ParseException;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.StringJoiner;
import java.util.UUID;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class JwtServiceImpl implements JwtService {

    TokenBlacklistService tokenBlacklistService;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${jwt.signerKey}")
    protected String SIGNER_KEY;

    @Override
    public String generateToken(User user) {
        JWSHeader header = new JWSHeader(JWSAlgorithm.HS256);
        JWTClaimsSet jwtClaimsSet = new JWTClaimsSet.Builder()
                .subject(user.getUsername())
                .issuer("cms.com")
                .issueTime(new Date())
                .expirationTime(new Date(
                        Instant.now().plus(1, ChronoUnit.HOURS).toEpochMilli()
                ))
                .jwtID(UUID.randomUUID().toString())
                .claim("scope", buildScope(user))
                .build();

        Payload payload = new Payload(jwtClaimsSet.toJSONObject());

        JWSObject jwsObject = new JWSObject(header, payload);

        try {
            jwsObject.sign(new MACSigner(SIGNER_KEY.getBytes()));
            return jwsObject.serialize();
        } catch (JOSEException e) {
            log.error("Cannot create token", e);
            throw new RuntimeException(e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void invalidateToken(String token) {
        try {
            SignedJWT signedJWT = SignedJWT.parse(token);
            String jit = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getJWTID();
            Date expiryTime = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getExpirationTime();
            tokenBlacklistService.blacklist(jit, expiryTime);
        } catch (ParseException e) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.INVALID_TOKEN);
        }
    }

    private String buildScope(User user) {
        StringJoiner stringJoiner = new StringJoiner(" ");

        if (user.getRole() != null)
            stringJoiner.add("ROLE_" + user.getRole().getName());
        return stringJoiner.toString();
    }

    @Override
    public IntrospectResponse introspect(IntrospectRequest request) throws JOSEException, ParseException {
        String token = request.getToken();
        boolean isValid = true;
        try {
            verifyToken(token);
        } catch (AppException e) {
            isValid = false;
        }
        return IntrospectResponse.builder()
                .valid(isValid)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public SignedJWT verifyToken(String token) throws ParseException, JOSEException {
        SignedJWT signedJWT = SignedJWT.parse(token);
        JWSVerifier verifier = new MACVerifier(SIGNER_KEY.getBytes());
        Date expiryTime = signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getExpirationTime();
        boolean verified = signedJWT.verify(verifier);

        if (!verified || !expiryTime.after(new Date())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHENTICATED);
        }
        if (tokenBlacklistService.isBlacklisted(signedJWT.getJWTClaimsSet().getJWTID()))
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHENTICATED);
        return signedJWT;
    }
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\auth\service\impl\LoginAttemptServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.auth.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.experimental.NonFinal;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.LoginAttemptService;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.time.Duration;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class LoginAttemptServiceImpl implements LoginAttemptService {

    RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${app.login.max-failed-attempts:5}")
    int maxFailedAttempts;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${app.login.failure-window-minutes:15}")
    long failureWindowMinutes;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${app.login.lockout-minutes:15}")
    long lockoutMinutes;

    private static final String FAIL_PREFIX = "login:fail:";
    private static final String LOCKOUT_PREFIX = "login:lockout:";

    @Override
    public boolean isLocked(String username) {
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(redisTemplate.hasKey(LOCKOUT_PREFIX + username));
    }

    @Override
    public long getLockoutSecondsRemaining(String username) {
        Long ttl = redisTemplate.getExpire(LOCKOUT_PREFIX + username);
        return ttl == null ? 0 : Math.max(ttl, 0);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean registerFailure(String username) {
        String key = FAIL_PREFIX + username;
        Long attempts = redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key);

        if (attempts != null && attempts == 1L) {
            redisTemplate.expire(key, Duration.ofMinutes(failureWindowMinutes));
        }

        if (attempts != null && attempts >= maxFailedAttempts) {
            lock(username);
            redisTemplate.delete(key);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void lock(String username) {
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(LOCKOUT_PREFIX + username, "1", Duration.ofMinutes(lockoutMinutes));
    }

    @Override
    public void reset(String username) {
        redisTemplate.delete(FAIL_PREFIX + username);
    }
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\auth\service\impl\OtpServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.auth.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.experimental.NonFinal;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.OtpService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OtpPurpose;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Duration;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class OtpServiceImpl implements OtpService {

    RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${app.otp.expiration-minutes:5}")
    long otpExpirationMinutes;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${app.otp.resend-cooldown-seconds:60}")
    long resendCooldownSeconds;

    private static final String OTP_PREFIX = "otp:verify:";
    private static final String COOLDOWN_PREFIX = "otp:cooldown:";
    private static final SecureRandom RANDOM = new SecureRandom();

    @Override
    public String generateOtp(OtpPurpose purpose, String email) {
        String otp = String.valueOf(100000 + RANDOM.nextInt(900000));
        redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                .set(otpKey(purpose, email), otp, Duration.ofMinutes(otpExpirationMinutes));
        log.info("Generated OTP for purpose={}, email={}, expires in {} minute(s)", purpose, email, otpExpirationMinutes);
        return otp;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isValid(OtpPurpose purpose, String email, String otp) {
        String stored = redisTemplate.opsForValue().get(otpKey(purpose, email));
        return stored != null && stored.equals(otp);
    }

    @Override
    public void invalidate(OtpPurpose purpose, String email) {
        redisTemplate.delete(otpKey(purpose, email));
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInCooldown(OtpPurpose purpose, String email) {
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(redisTemplate.hasKey(cooldownKey(purpose, email)));
    }

    @Override
    public void markCooldown(OtpPurpose purpose, String email) {
        redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(cooldownKey(purpose, email), "1", Duration.ofSeconds(resendCooldownSeconds));
    }

    private String otpKey(OtpPurpose purpose, String email) {
        return OTP_PREFIX + purpose.name().toLowerCase() + ":" + email;
    }

    private String cooldownKey(OtpPurpose purpose, String email) {
        return COOLDOWN_PREFIX + purpose.name().toLowerCase() + ":" + email;
    }
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\auth\service\impl\TokenBlacklistServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.auth.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.TokenBlacklistService;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.Date;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class TokenBlacklistServiceImpl implements TokenBlacklistService {

    RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    public static final String PREFIX = "token:blacklist:";

    @Override
    public void blacklist(String jti, Date expiryTime) {
        long ttlMillis = expiryTime.getTime() - System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (ttlMillis > 0) {
            redisTemplate.opsForValue()
                    .set(PREFIX + jti, "1", ttlMillis, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isBlacklisted(String jti) {
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(redisTemplate.hasKey(PREFIX + jti));
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.cart.controller;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.AddCartItemRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.MergeCartRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.UpdateCartItemRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.response.CartMutationResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.service.CartService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.AnonymousAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.DeleteMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

/**
 * Cart API â€” user (JWT) hoáº·c guest ({@code X-Cart-Token}).
 * <p>
 * Guest: láº§n Ä‘áº§u add khÃ´ng gá»­i token â†’ server táº¡o UUID, tráº£ header {@code X-Cart-Token}
 * vÃ  body {@code result.cartToken}. Client lÆ°u láº¡i gá»­i cÃ¡c request sau.
 * <p>
 * Sau login: {@code POST /cart/merge} kÃ¨m JWT + optional {@code X-Cart-Token} Ä‘á»ƒ gá»™p Redis â†’ DB.
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/cart")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class CartController {

    public static final String CART_TOKEN_HEADER = "X-Cart-Token";

    CartService cartService;

    private String currentUsernameOrNull() {
        Authentication auth = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        if (auth == null || !auth.isAuthenticated() || auth instanceof AnonymousAuthenticationToken) {
            return null;
        }
        String name = auth.getName();
        if (name == null || name.isBlank() || "anonymousUser".equals(name)) {
            return null;
        }
        return name;
    }

    private ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> ok(CartMutationResponse result, String message) {
        ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse> body = ApiResponse.<CartMutationResponse>builder()
                .result(result)
                .message(message)
                .build();
        ResponseEntity.BodyBuilder builder = ResponseEntity.ok();
        if (result.getCartToken() != null && !result.getCartToken().isBlank()) {
            builder.header(CART_TOKEN_HEADER, result.getCartToken());
        }
        return builder.body(body);
    }

    private ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> ok(CartMutationResponse result) {
        return ok(result, null);
    }

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> getCart(
            @RequestHeader(value = CART_TOKEN_HEADER, required = false) String cartToken) {
        return ok(cartService.getCart(currentUsernameOrNull(), cartToken));
    }

    @PostMapping("/items")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> addItem(
            @RequestHeader(value = CART_TOKEN_HEADER, required = false) String cartToken,
            @RequestBody @Valid AddCartItemRequest request) {
        return ok(cartService.addItem(currentUsernameOrNull(), cartToken, request), "Item added to cart");
    }

    @PutMapping("/items/{variantId}")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> updateItem(
            @PathVariable Long variantId,
            @RequestHeader(value = CART_TOKEN_HEADER, required = false) String cartToken,
            @RequestBody @Valid UpdateCartItemRequest request) {
        return ok(cartService.updateItem(currentUsernameOrNull(), cartToken, variantId, request),
                "Cart item updated");
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/items/{variantId}")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> removeItem(
            @PathVariable Long variantId,
            @RequestHeader(value = CART_TOKEN_HEADER, required = false) String cartToken) {
        return ok(cartService.removeItem(currentUsernameOrNull(), cartToken, variantId), "Cart item removed");
    }

    @DeleteMapping
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> clearCart(
            @RequestHeader(value = CART_TOKEN_HEADER, required = false) String cartToken) {
        return ok(cartService.clearCart(currentUsernameOrNull(), cartToken), "Cart cleared");
    }

    /**
     * Chá»‰ user Ä‘Ã£ login. Gá»™p guest Redis (X-Cart-Token) vÃ /hoáº·c body.items vÃ o DB, xÃ³a Redis.
     */
    @PostMapping("/merge")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<CartMutationResponse>> mergeCart(
            @RequestHeader(value = CART_TOKEN_HEADER, required = false) String cartToken,
            @RequestBody(required = false) @Valid MergeCartRequest request) {
        String username = currentUsernameOrNull();
        // merge báº¯t buá»™c login â€” náº¿u null service sáº½ UNAUTHENTICATED
        return ok(cartService.mergeCart(username, cartToken, request), "Cart merged successfully");
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\cart\guest\GuestCartStore.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.cart.guest;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.experimental.NonFinal;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.AddCartItemRequest;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Giá» khÃ¡ch trÃªn Redis.
 * <pre>
 * Key:  cart:guest:{token}
 * Type: HASH  field=variantId  value=quantity
 * TTL:  app.cart.guest-ttl-days (máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh 14 ngÃ y), refresh má»—i láº§n ghi.
 * </pre>
 */
@Component
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class GuestCartStore {

    private static final String KEY_PREFIX = "cart:guest:";

    StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${app.cart.guest-ttl-days:14}")
    long ttlDays;

    public String newToken() {
        return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
    }
    
    public boolean exists(String token) {
        if (blank(token)) {
            return false;
        }
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(redisTemplate.hasKey(key(token)));
    }

    public List<GuestLine> get(String token) {
        if (blank(token)) {
            return List.of();
        }
        Map<Object, Object> entries = redisTemplate.opsForHash().entries(key(token));
        if (entries == null || entries.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }
        List<GuestLine> lines = new ArrayList<>(entries.size());
        for (Map.Entry<Object, Object> e : entries.entrySet()) {
            try {
                Long variantId = Long.valueOf(String.valueOf(e.getKey()));
                int qty = Integer.parseInt(String.valueOf(e.getValue()));
                if (qty > 0) {
                    lines.add(new GuestLine(variantId, qty));
                }
            } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
                log.warn("Skip corrupt guest cart entry token={} field={}", token, e.getKey());
            }
        }
        return lines;
    }

    public void put(String token, Long variantId, int quantity) {
        if (blank(token) || variantId == null) {
            return;
        }
        String k = key(token);
        if (quantity <= 0) {
            redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(k, String.valueOf(variantId));
        } else {
            redisTemplate.opsForHash().put(k, String.valueOf(variantId), String.valueOf(quantity));
        }
        touchTtl(k);
    }

    public void saveAll(String token, List<GuestLine> lines) {
        if (blank(token)) {
            return;
        }
        String k = key(token);
        redisTemplate.delete(k);
        List<GuestLine> normalized = normalize(lines);
        if (normalized.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Map<String, String> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (GuestLine line : normalized) {
            map.put(String.valueOf(line.variantId()), String.valueOf(line.quantity()));
        }
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().putAll(k, map);
        touchTtl(k);
    }

    public void remove(String token, Long variantId) {
        if (blank(token) || variantId == null) {
            return;
        }
        String k = key(token);
        redisTemplate.opsForHash().delete(k, String.valueOf(variantId));
        touchTtl(k);
    }

    public void clear(String token) {
        if (!blank(token)) {
            redisTemplate.delete(key(token));
        }
    }

    public static List<GuestLine> normalize(List<GuestLine> raw) {
        Map<Long, Integer> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        if (raw != null) {
            for (GuestLine line : raw) {
                if (line == null || line.variantId() == null || line.quantity() == null || line.quantity() <= 0) {
                    continue;
                }
                map.merge(line.variantId(), line.quantity(), Integer::sum);
            }
        }
        List<GuestLine> out = new ArrayList<>(map.size());
        map.forEach((id, qty) -> out.add(new GuestLine(id, qty)));
        return out;
    }

    public static List<GuestLine> fromRequests(List<AddCartItemRequest> items) {
        if (items == null) {
            return List.of();
        }
        List<GuestLine> raw = new ArrayList<>(items.size());
        for (AddCartItemRequest item : items) {
            if (item != null) {
                raw.add(new GuestLine(item.getVariantId(), item.getQuantity()));
            }
        }
        return normalize(raw);
    }

    private void touchTtl(String k) {
        redisTemplate.expire(k, Duration.ofDays(Math.max(ttlDays, 1)));
    }

    private static boolean blank(String s) {
        return s == null || s.isBlank();
    }

    private static String key(String token) {
        return KEY_PREFIX + token;
    }

    public record GuestLine(Long variantId, Integer quantity) {}
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\cart\repository\CartItemRepository.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.cart.repository;

import org.example.smashhub.cart.entity.CartItem;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@Repository
public interface CartItemRepository extends JpaRepository<CartItem, Long> {

    @Query("""
            SELECT c FROM CartItem c
            JOIN FETCH c.variant v
            JOIN FETCH v.color col
            JOIN FETCH col.product p
            WHERE c.user.id = :userId
            ORDER BY c.updateDate DESC
            """)
    List<CartItem> findByUserIdWithDetails(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    Optional<CartItem> findByUserIdAndVariantId(Long userId, Long variantId);

    boolean existsByUserIdAndVariantId(Long userId, Long variantId);

    @Modifying(clearAutomatically = true, flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("DELETE FROM CartItem c WHERE c.user.id = :userId")
    int deleteAllByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    @Modifying(clearAutomatically = true, flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("DELETE FROM CartItem c WHERE c.user.id = :userId AND c.variant.id = :variantId")
    int deleteByUserIdAndVariantId(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("variantId") Long variantId);

    long countByUserId(Long userId);

    @Query("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(c.quantity), 0) FROM CartItem c WHERE c.user.id = :userId")
    long sumQuantityByUserId(@Param("userId") Long userId);
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\cart\service\impl\CartServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.cart.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.AddCartItemRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.MergeCartRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.request.UpdateCartItemRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.response.CartItemResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.response.CartMutationResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.dto.response.CartResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.entity.CartItem;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.guest.GuestCartStore;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.guest.GuestCartStore.GuestLine;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.repository.CartItemRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.cart.service.CartService;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Product;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductColor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductImage;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductVariant;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductImageRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductVariantRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ImagePriority;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.InventoryStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.VariantStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.user.entity.User;
import org.example.smashhub.user.repository.UserRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class CartServiceImpl implements CartService {

    private static final int MAX_LINE_QUANTITY = 99;

    CartItemRepository cartItemRepository;
    ProductVariantRepository variantRepository;
    ProductImageRepository productImageRepository;
    UserRepository userRepository;
    GuestCartStore guestCartStore;

    // =========================================================================
    // PUBLIC
    // =========================================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public CartMutationResponse getCart(String username, String cartToken) {
        if (isUser(username)) {
            return userResult(username, loadUserCart(username));
        }
        String token = ensureGuestToken(cartToken);
        return guestResult(token, buildCartFromGuestLines(guestCartStore.get(token)));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartMutationResponse addItem(String username, String cartToken, AddCartItemRequest request) {
        ProductVariant variant = requirePurchasableVariant(request.getVariantId());
        int requested = request.getQuantity();

        if (isUser(username)) {
            User user = requireUser(username);
            Optional<CartItem> existing =
                    cartItemRepository.findByUserIdAndVariantId(user.getId(), variant.getId());
            if (existing.isPresent()) {
                CartItem item = existing.get();
                item.setQuantity(clampQuantity(item.getQuantity() + requested, variant.getStock()));
                cartItemRepository.save(item);
            } else {
                cartItemRepository.save(CartItem.builder()
                        .user(user)
                        .variant(variant)
                        .quantity(clampQuantity(requested, variant.getStock()))
                        .build());
            }
            return userResult(username, loadUserCart(username));
        }

        String token = ensureGuestToken(cartToken);
        Map<Long, Integer> map = toMap(guestCartStore.get(token));
        int current = map.getOrDefault(variant.getId(), 0);
        map.put(variant.getId(), clampQuantity(current + requested, variant.getStock()));
        guestCartStore.saveAll(token, toLines(map));
        return guestResult(token, buildCartFromGuestLines(guestCartStore.get(token)));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartMutationResponse updateItem(String username, String cartToken, Long variantId,
                                           UpdateCartItemRequest request) {
        if (isUser(username)) {
            User user = requireUser(username);
            CartItem item = cartItemRepository.findByUserIdAndVariantId(user.getId(), variantId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND));
            ProductVariant variant = item.getVariant();
            ensureVariantStillValid(variant);
            item.setQuantity(clampQuantity(request.getQuantity(), variant.getStock()));
            cartItemRepository.save(item);
            return userResult(username, loadUserCart(username));
        }

        String token = requireGuestToken(cartToken);
        Map<Long, Integer> map = toMap(guestCartStore.get(token));
        if (!map.containsKey(variantId)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        ProductVariant variant = requirePurchasableVariant(variantId);
        map.put(variantId, clampQuantity(request.getQuantity(), variant.getStock()));
        guestCartStore.saveAll(token, toLines(map));
        return guestResult(token, buildCartFromGuestLines(guestCartStore.get(token)));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartMutationResponse removeItem(String username, String cartToken, Long variantId) {
        if (isUser(username)) {
            User user = requireUser(username);
            if (cartItemRepository.deleteByUserIdAndVariantId(user.getId(), variantId) == 0) {
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND);
            }
            return userResult(username, loadUserCart(username));
        }

        String token = requireGuestToken(cartToken);
        Map<Long, Integer> map = toMap(guestCartStore.get(token));
        if (!map.containsKey(variantId)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_ITEM_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        guestCartStore.remove(token, variantId);
        return guestResult(token, buildCartFromGuestLines(guestCartStore.get(token)));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartMutationResponse clearCart(String username, String cartToken) {
        if (isUser(username)) {
            User user = requireUser(username);
            cartItemRepository.deleteAllByUserId(user.getId());
            return userResult(username, emptyCart());
        }
        String token = ensureGuestToken(cartToken);
        guestCartStore.clear(token);
        return guestResult(token, emptyCart());
    }

    /**
     * Gá»™p giá» khÃ¡ch â†’ user DB rá»“i xÃ³a Redis.
     * Nguá»“n guest: Redis (cartToken) náº¿u cÃ³; khÃ´ng thÃ¬ request.items.
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CartMutationResponse mergeCart(String username, String cartToken, MergeCartRequest request) {
        if (!isUser(username)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHENTICATED);
        }
        User user = requireUser(username);

        List<GuestLine> guestLines = List.of();
        if (cartToken != null && !cartToken.isBlank() && guestCartStore.exists(cartToken)) {
            guestLines = guestCartStore.get(cartToken);
        } else if (request != null && request.getItems() != null && !request.getItems().isEmpty()) {
            guestLines = GuestCartStore.fromRequests(request.getItems());
        }

        if (guestLines.isEmpty()) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_MERGE_EMPTY);
        }

        for (GuestLine line : guestLines) {
            Optional<ProductVariant> variantOpt = variantRepository.findById(line.variantId());
            if (variantOpt.isEmpty()) {
                log.warn("Merge: skip unknown variantId={}", line.variantId());
                continue;
            }
            ProductVariant variant = variantOpt.get();
            if (!isPurchasable(variant) || variant.getStock() == null || variant.getStock() <= 0) {
                log.warn("Merge: skip non-purchasable variantId={}", line.variantId());
                continue;
            }

            Optional<CartItem> existing =
                    cartItemRepository.findByUserIdAndVariantId(user.getId(), variant.getId());
            if (existing.isPresent()) {
                CartItem item = existing.get();
                item.setQuantity(clampQuantity(item.getQuantity() + line.quantity(), variant.getStock()));
                cartItemRepository.save(item);
            } else {
                cartItemRepository.save(CartItem.builder()
                        .user(user)
                        .variant(variant)
                        .quantity(clampQuantity(line.quantity(), variant.getStock()))
                        .build());
            }
        }

        if (cartToken != null && !cartToken.isBlank()) {
            guestCartStore.clear(cartToken);
        }

        return userResult(username, loadUserCart(username));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // USER / GUEST helpers
    // =========================================================================

    private boolean isUser(String username) {
        return username != null && !username.isBlank()
                && !"anonymousUser".equals(username);
    }

    private CartResponse loadUserCart(String username) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        return toCartResponseFromEntities(cartItemRepository.findByUserIdWithDetails(user.getId()));
    }

    private String ensureGuestToken(String cartToken) {
        if (cartToken != null && !cartToken.isBlank()) {
            return cartToken.trim();
        }
        return guestCartStore.newToken();
    }

    private String requireGuestToken(String cartToken) {
        if (cartToken == null || cartToken.isBlank()) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_TOKEN_REQUIRED);
        }
        return cartToken.trim();
    }

    private CartMutationResponse userResult(String username, CartResponse cart) {
        return CartMutationResponse.builder()
                .cart(cart)
                .cartToken(null)
                .build();
    }

    private CartMutationResponse guestResult(String token, CartResponse cart) {
        return CartMutationResponse.builder()
                .cart(cart)
                .cartToken(token)
                .build();
    }

    private CartResponse buildCartFromGuestLines(List<GuestLine> lines) {
        if (lines == null || lines.isEmpty()) {
            return emptyCart();
        }
        List<CartItemResponse> items = new ArrayList<>();
        BigDecimal subtotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        int totalQuantity = 0;
        int issueCount = 0;

        for (GuestLine line : lines) {
            Optional<ProductVariant> opt = variantRepository.findById(line.variantId());
            if (opt.isEmpty()) {
                issueCount++;
                continue;
            }
            ProductVariant variant = opt.get();
            // cáº§n color + product; trong TX Hibernate load lazy OK
            CartItemResponse row = toCartItemResponseFromVariant(variant, line.quantity(), null);
            items.add(row);
            totalQuantity += row.getQuantity() == null ? 0 : row.getQuantity();
            if (row.getLineTotal() != null && !row.isUnavailable()) {
                subtotal = subtotal.add(row.getLineTotal());
            }
            if (row.isUnavailable() || row.isStockInsufficient()) {
                issueCount++;
            }
        }

        return CartResponse.builder()
                .items(items)
                .totalItems(items.size())
                .totalQuantity(totalQuantity)
                .subtotal(subtotal)
                .issueCount(issueCount)
                .build();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Domain validation
    // =========================================================================

    private User requireUser(String username) {
        return userRepository.findByUsername(username)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.USER_NOT_EXISTED));
    }

    private ProductVariant requirePurchasableVariant(Long variantId) {
        ProductVariant variant = variantRepository.findById(variantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND));
        if (!isPurchasable(variant)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INACTIVE);
        }
        if (variant.getStock() == null || variant.getStock() <= 0
                || variant.getInventoryStatus() == InventoryStatus.OUT_OF_STOCK) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INSUFFICIENT_STOCK);
        }
        return variant;
    }

    private void ensureVariantStillValid(ProductVariant variant) {
        if (variant.getActive() != VariantStatus.ACTIVE) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INACTIVE);
        }
        if (variant.getColor().getProduct().getProductStatus() != ProductStatus.ACTIVE) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INACTIVE);
        }
    }

    private boolean isPurchasable(ProductVariant variant) {
        if (variant.getActive() != VariantStatus.ACTIVE) {
            return false;
        }
        ProductColor color = variant.getColor();
        if (color == null || color.getProduct() == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return color.getProduct().getProductStatus() == ProductStatus.ACTIVE;
    }

    private int clampQuantity(int quantity, Integer stock) {
        if (quantity < 1) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_QUANTITY_INVALID);
        }
        int maxByStock = stock == null ? 0 : Math.max(stock, 0);
        if (maxByStock <= 0) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INSUFFICIENT_STOCK);
        }
        int clamped = Math.min(quantity, Math.min(maxByStock, MAX_LINE_QUANTITY));
        if (clamped < 1) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INSUFFICIENT_STOCK);
        }
        return clamped;
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Mapping
    // =========================================================================

    private CartResponse emptyCart() {
        return CartResponse.builder()
                .items(List.of())
                .totalItems(0)
                .totalQuantity(0)
                .subtotal(BigDecimal.ZERO)
                .issueCount(0)
                .build();
    }

    private CartResponse toCartResponseFromEntities(List<CartItem> items) {
        if (items == null || items.isEmpty()) {
            return emptyCart();
        }
        List<CartItemResponse> lines = new ArrayList<>(items.size());
        BigDecimal subtotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        int totalQuantity = 0;
        int issueCount = 0;
        for (CartItem item : items) {
            CartItemResponse line = toCartItemResponseFromVariant(
                    item.getVariant(), item.getQuantity(), item.getId());
            lines.add(line);
            totalQuantity += line.getQuantity() == null ? 0 : line.getQuantity();
            if (line.getLineTotal() != null && !line.isUnavailable()) {
                subtotal = subtotal.add(line.getLineTotal());
            }
            if (line.isUnavailable() || line.isStockInsufficient()) {
                issueCount++;
            }
        }
        return CartResponse.builder()
                .items(lines)
                .totalItems(lines.size())
                .totalQuantity(totalQuantity)
                .subtotal(subtotal)
                .issueCount(issueCount)
                .build();
    }

    private CartItemResponse toCartItemResponseFromVariant(ProductVariant variant, int quantity, Long cartItemId) {
        ProductColor color = variant.getColor();
        Product product = color.getProduct();

        boolean unavailable = variant.getActive() != VariantStatus.ACTIVE
                || product.getProductStatus() != ProductStatus.ACTIVE;
        int stock = variant.getStock() == null ? 0 : variant.getStock();
        boolean stockInsufficient = !unavailable && quantity > stock;

        BigDecimal price = product.getPrice();
        BigDecimal salePrice = product.getSalePrice();
        BigDecimal effective = org.example.smashhub.catalog.util.PriceCalculator.effectivePrice(price, salePrice);
        BigDecimal lineTotal = effective.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(quantity));

        return CartItemResponse.builder()
                .id(cartItemId)
                .variantId(variant.getId())
                .sku(variant.getSku())
                .size(variant.getSize())
                .quantity(quantity)
                .availableStock(stock)
                .variantStatus(variant.getActive())
                .inventoryStatus(variant.getInventoryStatus())
                .productId(product.getId())
                .productName(product.getName())
                .productSlug(product.getSlug())
                .productStatus(product.getProductStatus())
                .unitPrice(price)
                .salePrice(salePrice)
                .effectivePrice(effective)
                .lineTotal(lineTotal)
                .colorId(color.getId())
                .colorName(color.getColorName())
                .hexCode(color.getHexCode())
                .thumbnailUrl(resolveThumbnail(color.getId()))
                .stockInsufficient(stockInsufficient)
                .unavailable(unavailable)
                .build();
    }

    private String resolveThumbnail(Long colorId) {
        if (colorId == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            List<ProductImage> images = productImageRepository.findByColorIdOrderByOrderIndexAsc(colorId);
            if (images == null || images.isEmpty()) {
                return null;
            }
            return images.stream()
                    .filter(img -> img.getIsMain() == ImagePriority.MAIN)
                    .map(ProductImage::getUrl)
                    .findFirst()
                    .orElseGet(() -> images.stream()
                            .min(Comparator.comparing(
                                    img -> img.getOrderIndex() == null ? Integer.MAX_VALUE : img.getOrderIndex()))
                            .map(ProductImage::getUrl)
                            .orElse(null));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.debug("Thumbnail resolve failed colorId={}: {}", colorId, e.toString());
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static Map<Long, Integer> toMap(List<GuestLine> lines) {
        Map<Long, Integer> map = new HashMap<>();
        if (lines != null) {
            for (GuestLine line : lines) {
                map.put(line.variantId(), line.quantity());
            }
        }
        return map;
    }

    private static List<GuestLine> toLines(Map<Long, Integer> map) {
        List<GuestLine> lines = new ArrayList<>(map.size());
        map.forEach((id, qty) -> lines.add(new GuestLine(id, qty)));
        return lines;
    }
}

/**
 * Cart há»— trá»£ 2 cháº¿ Ä‘á»™:
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>User</b> (JWT): username != null â†’ persist DB {@code cart_items}</li>
 *   <li><b>Guest</b> (X-Cart-Token): lÆ°u Redis {@code cart:guest:{token}}</li>
 * </ul>
 * {@code mergeCart} chá»‰ dÃ¹ng khi Ä‘Ã£ login: gá»™p guest Redis (hoáº·c payload) vÃ o DB rá»“i xÃ³a Redis.
 */
public interface CartService {

    CartMutationResponse getCart(String username, String cartToken);

    CartMutationResponse addItem(String username, String cartToken, AddCartItemRequest request);

    CartMutationResponse updateItem(String username, String cartToken, Long variantId, UpdateCartItemRequest request);

    CartMutationResponse removeItem(String username, String cartToken, Long variantId);

    CartMutationResponse clearCart(String username, String cartToken);

    /**
     * User Ä‘Ã£ login: gá»™p giá» khÃ¡ch vÃ o DB.
     * Æ¯u tiÃªn {@code cartToken} (Redis); náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ¬ dÃ¹ng {@code request.items}.
     * Sau merge xÃ³a Redis guest key.
     */
    CartMutationResponse mergeCart(String username, String cartToken, MergeCartRequest request);
}

package org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.BrandResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.CategoryResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.ProductResponse;
import org.springframework.cache.Cache;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.CachingConfigurer;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.EnableCaching;
import org.springframework.cache.interceptor.CacheErrorHandler;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.data.redis.cache.RedisCacheConfiguration;
import org.springframework.data.redis.cache.RedisCacheManager;
import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisConnectionFactory;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.RedisSerializationContext.SerializationPair;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.RedisSerializer;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.SerializationException;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.StringRedisSerializer;
import tools.jackson.core.JacksonException;
import tools.jackson.databind.DeserializationFeature;
import tools.jackson.databind.JavaType;
import tools.jackson.databind.json.JsonMapper;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh cache Redis cho module catalog.
 *
 * <p>Má»—i cache chá»‰ chá»©a Ä‘Ãºng má»™t kiá»ƒu giÃ¡ trá»‹ nÃªn má»—i cache cÃ³ serializer riÃªng theo kiá»ƒu Ä‘Ã³
 * ({@link TypedJsonRedisSerializer}), khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng default typing: JSON khÃ´ng chá»©a tÃªn class,
 * nÃªn khÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c cáº¥u hÃ¬nh serializer cÅ© hay class ná»™i bá»™ cá»§a JDK (List.of, Stream.toList...).
 *
 * <p><b>Khi Ä‘á»•i cáº¥u trÃºc DTO Ä‘Æ°á»£c cache (Ä‘á»•i tÃªn/kiá»ƒu field, khÃ´ng pháº£i thÃªm field):</b> tÄƒng {@link #KEY_PREFIX}
 * (v1 -> v2). Entry cÅ© bá»‹ bá» qua vÃ  tá»± háº¿t háº¡n theo TTL, khÃ´ng cáº§n flush Redis báº±ng tay.
 */
@Configuration
@EnableCaching
public class CacheConfig implements CachingConfigurer {

    /** Tiá»n tá»‘ version cá»§a má»i key cache catalog: "catalog:v1::brands::all". */
    static final String KEY_PREFIX = "catalog:v1::";

    // Pháº£i trÃ¹ng vá»›i cacheNames trong @Cacheable / CatalogCacheEvictor.
    private static final String CACHE_BRANDS = "brands";
    private static final String CACHE_BRAND = "brand";
    private static final String CACHE_CATEGORY_TREE = "categoryTree";
    private static final String CACHE_CATEGORY = "category";
    private static final String CACHE_PRODUCT = "product";
    private static final String CACHE_PRODUCT_BY_SLUG = "productBySlug";

    private static final Duration TTL_REFERENCE_DATA = Duration.ofMinutes(30);
    // Product chá»©a tá»“n kho nÃªn TTL ngáº¯n hÆ¡n: dÃ¹ sÃ³t evict thÃ¬ dá»¯ liá»‡u cÅ© cÅ©ng chá»‰ tá»“n táº¡i vÃ i phÃºt.
    private static final Duration TTL_PRODUCT = Duration.ofMinutes(5);

    @Bean
    public RedisCacheManager cacheManager(RedisConnectionFactory connectionFactory) {
        RedisCacheConfiguration base = RedisCacheConfiguration.defaultCacheConfig()
                .prefixCacheNameWith(KEY_PREFIX)
                .disableCachingNullValues()
                .serializeKeysWith(SerializationPair.fromSerializer(new StringRedisSerializer()));

        Map<String, RedisCacheConfiguration> perCache = new HashMap<>();
        perCache.put(CACHE_BRAND, typed(base, TTL_REFERENCE_DATA, TypedJsonRedisSerializer.of(BrandResponse.class)));
        perCache.put(CACHE_BRANDS, typed(base, TTL_REFERENCE_DATA, TypedJsonRedisSerializer.listOf(BrandResponse.class)));
        perCache.put(CACHE_CATEGORY, typed(base, TTL_REFERENCE_DATA, TypedJsonRedisSerializer.of(CategoryResponse.class)));
        perCache.put(CACHE_CATEGORY_TREE, typed(base, TTL_REFERENCE_DATA, TypedJsonRedisSerializer.listOf(CategoryResponse.class)));
        perCache.put(CACHE_PRODUCT, typed(base, TTL_PRODUCT, TypedJsonRedisSerializer.of(ProductResponse.class)));
        perCache.put(CACHE_PRODUCT_BY_SLUG, typed(base, TTL_PRODUCT, TypedJsonRedisSerializer.of(ProductResponse.class)));

        return RedisCacheManager.builder(connectionFactory)
                .cacheDefaults(base)
                .withInitialCacheConfigurations(perCache)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public CacheErrorHandler errorHandler() {
        return new CatalogCacheErrorHandler();
    }

    private static RedisCacheConfiguration typed(RedisCacheConfiguration base, Duration ttl, RedisSerializer<?> valueSerializer) {
        return base.entryTtl(ttl).serializeValuesWith(SerializationPair.fromSerializer(valueSerializer));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Serializer JSON cÃ³ kiá»ƒu cá»‘ Ä‘á»‹nh cho má»™t cache. KhÃ´ng ghi type id vÃ o JSON; kiá»ƒu Ä‘á»c ra do code quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh
     * (List luÃ´n lÃ  ArrayList); bá» qua field láº¡ nÃªn thÃªm field vÃ o DTO khÃ´ng lÃ m há»ng entry cÅ©.
     */
    static final class TypedJsonRedisSerializer<T> implements RedisSerializer<T> {

        // JsonMapper cá»§a Jackson 3 lÃ  immutable vÃ  thread-safe; java.time Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ sáºµn trong databind.
        private static final JsonMapper MAPPER = JsonMapper.builder()
                .disable(DeserializationFeature.FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES)
                .build();

        private static final byte[] EMPTY = new byte[0];

        private final JavaType type;

        private TypedJsonRedisSerializer(JavaType type) {
            this.type = type;
        }

        /** Cache chá»©a má»™t object, vÃ­ dá»¥ BrandResponse. */
        static <T> TypedJsonRedisSerializer<T> of(Class<T> valueType) {
            return new TypedJsonRedisSerializer<>(MAPPER.constructType(valueType));
        }

        /** Cache chá»©a List<E>, vÃ­ dá»¥ List<BrandResponse>. Äá»c ra luÃ´n lÃ  ArrayList. */
        static <E> TypedJsonRedisSerializer<List<E>> listOf(Class<E> elementType) {
            JavaType listType = MAPPER.getTypeFactory().constructCollectionType(ArrayList.class, elementType);
            return new TypedJsonRedisSerializer<>(listType);
        }

        @Override
        public byte[] serialize(T value) throws SerializationException {
            if (value == null) {
                return EMPTY;
            }
            try {
                return MAPPER.writeValueAsBytes(value);
            } catch (JacksonException e) {
                throw new SerializationException("Could not write JSON for cache value: " + e.getOriginalMessage(), e);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public T deserialize(byte[] bytes) throws SerializationException {
            if (bytes == null || bytes.length == 0) {
                return null;
            }
            try {
                return MAPPER.readValue(bytes, type);
            } catch (JacksonException e) {
                throw new SerializationException("Could not read JSON for cache value as " + type + ": "
                        + e.getOriginalMessage(), e);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Cache chá»‰ lÃ  lá»›p tÄƒng tá»‘c, lá»—i cache khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÃ m há»ng request.
     * Äá»c lá»—i (entry há»ng / sai Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng / Redis cháº­p chá»n): cáº£nh bÃ¡o, xoÃ¡ entry Ä‘Ã³, coi nhÆ° cache miss Ä‘á»ƒ service
     * Ä‘á»c DB rá»“i ghi láº¡i entry Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng. Ghi / xoÃ¡ / clear lá»—i: chá»‰ cáº£nh bÃ¡o, dá»¯ liá»‡u cÅ© tá»± háº¿t háº¡n theo TTL.
     */
    @Slf4j
    static final class CatalogCacheErrorHandler implements CacheErrorHandler {

        @Override
        public void handleCacheGetError(RuntimeException exception, Cache cache, Object key) {
            log.warn("Cache GET failed (cache='{}', key='{}'), treating as miss: {}",
                    cache.getName(), key, exception.toString());
            try {
                cache.evict(key);
            } catch (RuntimeException evictFailure) {
                log.warn("Could not evict broken entry (cache='{}', key='{}'): {}",
                        cache.getName(), key, evictFailure.toString());
            }
        }

        @Override
        public void handleCachePutError(RuntimeException exception, Cache cache, Object key, Object value) {
            log.warn("Cache PUT failed (cache='{}', key='{}'): {}", cache.getName(), key, exception.toString());
        }

        @Override
        public void handleCacheEvictError(RuntimeException exception, Cache cache, Object key) {
            log.warn("Cache EVICT failed (cache='{}', key='{}'): {}", cache.getName(), key, exception.toString());
        }

        @Override
        public void handleCacheClearError(RuntimeException exception, Cache cache) {
            log.warn("Cache CLEAR failed (cache='{}'): {}", cache.getName(), exception.toString());
        }
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\cache\CatalogCacheEvictor.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.cache.Cache;
import org.springframework.cache.CacheManager;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class CatalogCacheEvictor {
    CacheManager cacheManager;

    /** XÃ³a cache cá»§a 1 sáº£n pháº©m (theo id) vÃ  toÃ n bá»™ cache theo slug. Cháº¡y SAU commit. */
    public void evictProduct(Long productId) {
        runAfterCommit(() -> {
            evictKey("product", productId);
            clear("productBySlug");
        });
    }

    /**
     * Danh má»¥c thay Ä‘á»•i (Ä‘á»•i tÃªn/slug/cha) -> tÃªn danh má»¥c nhÃºng trong ProductResponse bá»‹ cÅ©,
     * nÃªn pháº£i xÃ³a luÃ´n cache sáº£n pháº©m.
     */
    public void evictCategories() {
        runAfterCommit(() -> {
            clear("categoryTree");
            clear("category");
            clear("product");
            clear("productBySlug");
        });
    }

    /**
     * Cache brand chá»©a productCount nÃªn cÅ©ng pháº£i xÃ³a má»—i khi sáº£n pháº©m Ä‘Æ°á»£c táº¡o/sá»­a/xÃ³a.
     */
    public void evictBrands() {
        runAfterCommit(() -> {
            clear("brands");
            clear("brand");
        });
    }

    // Lá»—i Redis khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÃ m há»ng request ghi DB Ä‘Ã£ commit -> chá»‰ log cáº£nh bÃ¡o (cache sáº½ tá»± háº¿t háº¡n theo TTL).
    private void evictKey(String cacheName, Object key) {
        try {
            Cache cache = cacheManager.getCache(cacheName);
            if (cache != null && key != null) {
                cache.evict(key);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("Could not evict key {} from cache {}", key, cacheName, e);
        }
    }

    private void clear(String cacheName) {
        try {
            Cache cache = cacheManager.getCache(cacheName);
            if (cache != null) {
                cache.clear();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("Could not clear cache {}", cacheName, e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Cháº¡y SAU khi transaction DB commit thÃ nh cÃ´ng (khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction thÃ¬ cháº¡y ngay).
     * XÃ³a cache ngay trong transaction thÃ¬ request khÃ¡c cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c DB cÅ© rá»“i náº¡p láº¡i cache cÅ©.
     */
    private void runAfterCommit(Runnable action) {
        if (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
            TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
                @Override
                public void afterCommit() {
                    action.run();
                }
            });
        } else {
            action.run();
        }
    }
}

    private static final String ADMIN_ONLY = "hasRole('ADMIN')";

            Set.of("id", "name", "price", "salePrice", "createDate", "updateDate");

    ProductAttributeService attributeService;

    @GetMapping
    public ApiResponse<PageResponse<ProductSummaryResponse>> search(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String keyword,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long categoryId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long brandId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) ProductType type,
            @RequestParam(required = false) ProductStatus status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) BigDecimal minPrice,
            @RequestParam(required = false) BigDecimal maxPrice,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean onSale,
            @RequestParam(required = false) List<String> attr,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "12") int size,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "createDate") String sortBy,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "DESC") Sort.Direction sortDirection) {

        ProductSearchRequest request = ProductSearchRequest.builder()
                .keyword(keyword)
                .categoryId(categoryId)
                .brandId(brandId)
                .type(type)
                .minPrice(minPrice)
                .maxPrice(maxPrice)
                .onSale(onSale)
                .attributes(parseAttributeFilters(attr))
                .status(isStaff() ? status : ProductStatus.ACTIVE)
                .build();

        String safeSortBy = ALLOWED_SORT_FIELDS.contains(sortBy) ? sortBy : "createDate";
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(
                Math.max(page, 0),
                Math.min(Math.max(size, 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE),
                Sort.by(sortDirection, safeSortBy));

        return ApiResponse.<PageResponse<ProductSummaryResponse>>builder()
                .result(productService.searchProducts(request, pageable))
                .build();

    /** Sá»­a áº£nh kÃ¨m file má»›i (multipart/form-data). KhÃ´ng gá»­i file thÃ¬ giá»¯ áº£nh cÅ©, chá»‰ sá»­a metadata. */
    @PutMapping(value = "/{productId}/images/{imageId}", consumes = MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA_VALUE)

            @PathVariable Long imageId,

            @RequestPart(value = "file", required = false) MultipartFile file) {
        return ApiResponse.<ProductImageResponse>builder()
                .result(mediaService.updateImage(productId, imageId, request, file))
                .build();
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{productId}/images/{imageId}")
    @PreAuthorize(CAN_MANAGE)
    public ApiResponse<Void> deleteImage(@PathVariable Long productId, @PathVariable Long imageId) {
        mediaService.deleteImage(productId, imageId);
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder().message("Image deleted").build();
    }

    // ============================== VARIANT ==============================
    // deductStock / restoreStock cá»‘ Ã½ KHÃ”NG cÃ³ endpoint: chá»‰ OrderService gá»i ná»™i bá»™.

    @PostMapping("/{productId}/variants")
    @PreAuthorize(CAN_MANAGE)
    public ApiResponse<ProductVariantResponse> addVariant(@PathVariable Long productId,
                                                          @RequestBody @Valid ProductVariantRequest request) {
        return ApiResponse.<ProductVariantResponse>builder()
                .result(inventoryService.addVariant(productId, request))
                .build();
    }

    @PutMapping("/{productId}/variants/{variantId}")
    @PreAuthorize(CAN_MANAGE)
    public ApiResponse<ProductVariantResponse> updateVariant(@PathVariable Long productId,
                                                             @PathVariable Long variantId,
                                                             @RequestBody @Valid ProductVariantRequest request) {
        return ApiResponse.<ProductVariantResponse>builder()
                .result(inventoryService.updateVariant(productId, variantId, request))
                .build();
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{productId}/variants/{variantId}")
    @PreAuthorize(CAN_MANAGE)
    public ApiResponse<Void> deleteVariant(@PathVariable Long productId, @PathVariable Long variantId) {
        inventoryService.deleteVariant(productId, variantId);
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder().message("Variant deactivated").build();
    }

    // ============================== HELPERS ==============================

    /** NgÆ°á»i Ä‘ang gá»i cÃ³ pháº£i ADMIN/STAFF khÃ´ng (anonymous -> false). */
    private boolean isStaff() {
        Authentication auth = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        if (auth == null) {
            return false;
        }
        return auth.getAuthorities().stream()
                .map(GrantedAuthority::getAuthority)
                .anyMatch(a -> "ROLE_ADMIN".equals(a) || "ROLE_STAFF".equals(a));
    }


    private ProductResponse hideIfNotPublic(ProductResponse product) {
        if (!isStaff() && product.getProductStatus() != ProductStatus.ACTIVE) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return product;
    }
    private List<ProductAttributeFilter> parseAttributeFilters(List<String> raw) {
        List<ProductAttributeFilter> filters = new ArrayList<>();
        if (raw == null) {
            return filters;
        }
        for (String item : raw) {
            int idx = item == null ? -1 : item.indexOf(':');
            if (idx > 0 && idx < item.length() - 1) {
                filters.add(ProductAttributeFilter.builder()
                        .attrName(item.substring(0, idx))
                        .attrValue(item.substring(idx + 1))
                        .build());
            }
        }
        return filters;
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductType;
import org.example.smashhub.common.persistence.BaseEntity;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Data
@Entity
@Table(name = "product")
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE)
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@ToString(callSuper = true, exclude = {"category", "brand"})
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class Product extends BaseEntity {

    @Column(nullable = false)
    String name;

    @Column(nullable = false, unique = true)
    String slug;

    @Column(nullable = false, columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    String description;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "type", nullable = false, length = 50)
    ProductType type;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "category_id", nullable = false)
    Category category;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "brand_id", nullable = false)
    Brand brand;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "product_status", nullable = false)
    ProductStatus productStatus;

    @Column(nullable = false,precision = 10, scale = 2)
    BigDecimal price;

    @Column(name = "sale_price", precision = 19, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal salePrice;


}

package org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.FetchType;
import jakarta.persistence.GeneratedValue;
import jakarta.persistence.GenerationType;
import jakarta.persistence.Id;
import jakarta.persistence.JoinColumn;
import jakarta.persistence.ManyToOne;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;
import lombok.ToString;

/**
 * EAV pattern - thuá»™c tÃ­nh ká»¹ thuáº­t riÃªng theo tá»«ng loáº¡i sáº£n pháº©m.
 * vd: weight=4U, grip_size=G5 (vá»£t) | shoe_tech=Power Cushion (giÃ y) | material=Polyester (Ã¡o)
 * KhÃ´ng káº¿ thá»«a BaseEntity vÃ¬ báº£ng gá»‘c khÃ´ng cÃ³ create_date/update_date.
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@ToString(exclude = {"product"})
@EqualsAndHashCode
@Entity
@Table(name = "product_attributes")
public class ProductAttribute {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "product_id", nullable = false)
    private Product product;

    @Column(name = "attr_name", nullable = false)
    private String attrName;

    @Column(name = "attr_value", nullable = false)
    private String attrValue;
}
