@Mapper(componentModel = "spring",
        nullValuePropertyMappingStrategy = NullValuePropertyMappingStrategy.IGNORE,
        uses = {BrandMapper.class, CategoryMapper.class})
public interface ProductMapper {

    @Mapping(target = "category", ignore = true)
    @Mapping(target = "brand", ignore = true)
    Product toProduct(ProductRequest request);

    @Mapping(target = "category", ignore = true)
    @Mapping(target = "brand", ignore = true)
    void updateProduct(@MappingTarget Product product, ProductRequest request);

    /**
     * Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i chi tiáº¿t má»™t Product Entity sang DTO tráº£ vá» cho Client (ProductResponse).
     * - target = "brand", source = "brand": DÃ¹ng BrandMapper (khai bÃ¡o trong uses) Ä‘á»ƒ map entity Brand sang BrandResponse.
     * - target = "category", source = "category": TÆ°Æ¡ng tá»±, dÃ¹ng CategoryMapper Ä‘á»ƒ map sang CategoryResponse.
     * - target = "attributes", ignore = true: Bá» qua danh sÃ¡ch thuá»™c tÃ­nh (VD: kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c, trá»ng lÆ°á»£ng...),
     *   thÆ°á»ng do Service map thá»§ cÃ´ng hoáº·c lazy loading Ä‘á»ƒ tá»‘i Æ°u query.
     * - target = "colors", ignore = true: Bá» qua danh sÃ¡ch mÃ u sáº¯c/biáº¿n thá»ƒ liÃªn quan Ä‘á»ƒ xá»­ lÃ½ riÃªng biá»‡t.
     */
    @Mapping(target = "brand", source = "brand")
    @Mapping(target = "category", source = "category")
    @Mapping(target = "attributes", ignore = true)
    @Mapping(target = "colors", ignore = true)
    ProductResponse toProductResponse(Product product);

    /**
     * Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i Product Entity sang ProductSummaryResponse (báº£n tÃ³m táº¯t nháº¹, thÆ°á»ng dÃ¹ng cho trang danh sÃ¡ch/tÃ¬m kiáº¿m).
     * - source = "category.name": TrÃ­ch xuáº¥t trá»±c tiáº¿p tÃªn danh má»¥c tá»« object lá»“ng nhau (product.getCategory().getName())
     *   gÃ¡n vÃ o thuá»™c tÃ­nh categoryName cá»§a DTO.
     * - source = "brand.name": TÆ°Æ¡ng tá»±, láº¥y product.getBrand().getName() gÃ¡n vÃ o brandName.
     */
    @Mapping(target = "categoryName", source = "category.name")
    @Mapping(target = "brandName", source = "brand.name")
    ProductSummaryResponse toProductSummaryResponse(Product product);

    /**
     * Ãnh xáº¡ danh sÃ¡ch tÃ³m táº¯t sáº£n pháº©m.
     * MapStruct sáº½ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng duyá»‡t vÃ²ng láº·p List vÃ  gá»i toProductSummaryResponse(Product) cho tá»«ng pháº§n tá»­.
     */
    List<ProductSummaryResponse> toProductSummaryResponseList(List<Product> products);

    // ==========================================

    /** Map Ä‘Æ¡n vÃ  map danh sÃ¡ch cho thuá»™c tÃ­nh Ä‘á»™ng cá»§a sáº£n pháº©m (vÃ­ dá»¥: cháº¥t liá»‡u, kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c...) */
    ProductAttributeResponse toProductAttributeResponse(ProductAttribute attribute);
    List<ProductAttributeResponse> toProductAttributeResponseList(List<ProductAttribute> attributes);

    /** Map Ä‘Æ¡n vÃ  map danh sÃ¡ch cho hÃ¬nh áº£nh cá»§a sáº£n pháº©m */
    ProductImageResponse toProductImageResponse(ProductImage image);
    List<ProductImageResponse> toProductImageResponseList(List<ProductImage> images);

    /** Map Ä‘Æ¡n vÃ  map danh sÃ¡ch cho biáº¿n thá»ƒ sáº£n pháº©m (SKU, giÃ¡ bÃ¡n riÃªng, tá»“n kho riÃªng...) */
    ProductVariantResponse toProductVariantResponse(ProductVariant variant);
    List<ProductVariantResponse> toProductVariantResponseList(List<ProductVariant> variants);

    /**
     * Map thÃ´ng tin nhÃ³m mÃ u sáº¯c cá»§a sáº£n pháº©m sang ProductColorResponse.
     * - target = "images", ignore = true: Bá» qua danh sÃ¡ch áº£nh cá»§a mÃ u nÃ y.
     * - target = "variants", ignore = true: Bá» qua danh sÃ¡ch biáº¿n thá»ƒ con cá»§a mÃ u nÃ y.
     * Cáº£ 2 Ä‘Æ°á»£c ignore nháº±m trÃ¡nh vÃ²ng láº·p quan há»‡ vÃ´ táº­n (circular reference) hoáº·c N+1 query khi lazy-load.

    @Mapping(target = "images", ignore = true)
    @Mapping(target = "variants", ignore = true)
    ProductColorResponse toProductColorResponse(ProductColor color);

package org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository;

import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Brand;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductType;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
@Repository
public interface BrandRepository extends JpaRepository<Brand, Long> {
    boolean existsBySlug(String slug);
    Optional<Brand> findBySlug(String slug);
    boolean existsByNameIgnoreCase(String name);
    @Query("SELECT b FROM Brand b WHERE " +
            "(:keyword IS NULL OR :keyword = '' OR " +
            "LOWER(b.name) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%')) OR " +
            "LOWER(b.slug) LIKE LOWER(CONCAT('%', :keyword, '%')))")
    Page<Brand> searchBrands(@Param("keyword") String keyword, Pageable pageable);
    @Query("SELECT DISTINCT b FROM Brand b JOIN Product p ON p.brand.id = b.id " +
            "WHERE p.category.id IN :categoryIds AND p.productStatus = :status " +
            "ORDER BY b.name ASC")
    List<Brand> findActiveBrandsByCategoryIds(@Param("categoryIds") Collection<Long> categoryIds,
                                              @Param("status") ProductStatus status);
    @Query("SELECT DISTINCT b FROM Brand b JOIN Product p ON p.brand.id = b.id " +
            "WHERE p.type = :type AND p.productStatus = :status " +
            "ORDER BY b.name ASC")
    List<Brand> findActiveBrandsByProductType(@Param("type") ProductType type,
                                              @Param("status") ProductStatus status);
}

package org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository;

import jakarta.persistence.LockModeType;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductVariant;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.InventoryStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.VariantStatus;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Lock;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@Repository
public interface ProductVariantRepository extends JpaRepository<ProductVariant, Long> {
    boolean existsBySku(String sku);
    boolean existsByColorId(Long colorId);
    Optional<ProductVariant> findBySku(String sku);
    List<ProductVariant> findByColorId(Long colorId);
    List<ProductVariant> findByColorProductId(Long productId);

    Optional<ProductVariant> findByIdAndColorProductId(Long id, Long productId);


    @Lock(LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)
    @Query("SELECT v FROM ProductVariant v WHERE v.id = :id")
    Optional<ProductVariant> findByIdForUpdate(@Param("id") Long id);


    @Modifying(clearAutomatically = true, flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("UPDATE ProductVariant v SET v.stock = v.stock - :qty " +
            "WHERE v.id = :id AND v.stock >= :qty AND v.active = :active")
    int deductStockAtomic(@Param("id") Long id,
                          @Param("qty") int qty,
                          @Param("active") VariantStatus active);

    @Modifying(clearAutomatically = true, flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("UPDATE ProductVariant v SET v.stock = v.stock + :qty WHERE v.id = :id")
    int restoreStockAtomic(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("qty") int qty);

    @Modifying(clearAutomatically = true, flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("UPDATE ProductVariant v SET v.inventoryStatus = :status WHERE v.id = :id AND v.stock = 0")
    int markOutOfStockIfEmpty(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("status") InventoryStatus status);

    @Modifying(clearAutomatically = true, flushAutomatically = true)
    @Query("UPDATE ProductVariant v SET v.inventoryStatus = :status " +
            "WHERE v.id = :id AND v.stock > 0 AND (v.inventoryStatus IS NULL OR v.inventoryStatus <> :status)")
    int markInStockIfAvailable(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("status") InventoryStatus status);
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\service\impl\BrandServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.BrandRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.BrandResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Brand;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.mapper.BrandMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.BrandRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.BrandService;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.CategoryService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.dto.PageResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductType;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.file.dto.FileUploadResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.file.enums.FileFolder;
import org.example.smashhub.file.service.FileService;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.CacheEvict;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.Cacheable;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.Caching;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class BrandServiceImpl implements BrandService {

    BrandRepository brandRepository;
    ProductRepository productRepository;
    CategoryService categoryService;
    BrandMapper brandMapper;
    FileService fileService;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    @Caching(evict = {
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brand", allEntries = true),
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brands", allEntries = true)
    })
    public BrandResponse create(BrandRequest request, MultipartFile logo) {
        String name = request.getName().trim();
        String slug = request.getSlug().trim().toLowerCase();

        if (brandRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NAME_EXISTED);
        }
        if (brandRepository.existsBySlug(slug)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }

        Brand brand = brandMapper.toBrand(request);
        brand.setName(name);
        brand.setSlug(slug);
        // logoUrl tá»« request bá»‹ BrandMapper map; Ä‘áº·t láº¡i null Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh nháº­n URL tá»± do tá»« client
        // (logo chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c gÃ¡n qua file upload an toÃ n bÃªn dÆ°á»›i).
        brand.setLogoUrl(null);
        brand.setLogoPublicId(null);

        // Upload SAU khi Ä‘Ã£ qua kiá»ƒm tra nghiá»‡p vá»¥: request sai khÃ´ng sinh file rÃ¡c.
        if (fileService.isPresent(logo)) {
            FileUploadResponse uploaded = fileService.uploadInTransaction(logo, FileFolder.BRAND);
            brand.setLogoUrl(uploaded.getUrl());
            brand.setLogoPublicId(uploaded.getPublicId());
        }

        BrandResponse response = brandMapper.toBrandResponse(saveBrand(brand));
        response.setProductCount(0L);
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    @Caching(evict = {
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brand", allEntries = true),
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brands", allEntries = true),
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "product", allEntries = true),
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "productBySlug", allEntries = true)
    })
    public BrandResponse update(Long id, BrandRequest request, MultipartFile logo) {
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));

        String name = request.getName().trim();
        String slug = request.getSlug().trim().toLowerCase();

        if (!brand.getName().equalsIgnoreCase(name) && brandRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NAME_EXISTED);
        }
        if (!brand.getSlug().equalsIgnoreCase(slug) && brandRepository.existsBySlug(slug)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }

        // LÆ°u láº¡i publicId cÅ© trÆ°á»›c khi mapper ghi Ä‘Ã¨ (mapper IGNORE null, nÃªn logoUrl trong
        // request khÃ´ng lÃ m thay Ä‘á»•i logo náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ file).
        String oldPublicId = brand.getLogoPublicId();

        brandMapper.updateBrand(brand, request);
        brand.setName(name);
        brand.setSlug(slug);

        if (fileService.isPresent(logo)) {
            // CÃ³ file má»›i: upload â†’ gÃ¡n url + publicId má»›i.
            FileUploadResponse uploaded = fileService.uploadInTransaction(logo, FileFolder.BRAND);
            brand.setLogoUrl(uploaded.getUrl());
            brand.setLogoPublicId(uploaded.getPublicId());
        }
        // Náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ file má»›i: giá»¯ nguyÃªn logoUrl + logoPublicId cÅ© (mapper Ä‘Ã£ IGNORE null).

        // Náº¿u publicId thá»±c sá»± thay Ä‘á»•i â†’ xÃ³a áº£nh cÅ© SAU KHI transaction commit.
        deleteOldLogoAfterCommit(oldPublicId, brand.getLogoPublicId());

        BrandResponse response = brandMapper.toBrandResponse(saveBrand(brand));
        response.setProductCount(productRepository.countByBrandIdAndProductStatus(id, ProductStatus.ACTIVE));
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    @Caching(evict = {
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brands", allEntries = true),
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brand", allEntries = true)
    })
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));

        if (productRepository.existsByBrandId(id)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_HAS_PRODUCTS);
        }

        String logoPublicId = brand.getLogoPublicId();
        brandRepository.delete(brand);

        // XÃ³a áº£nh trÃªn Cloudinary SAU KHI DB commit; náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ publicId thÃ¬ bá» qua.
        if (logoPublicId != null && !logoPublicId.isBlank()) {
            fileService.deleteAfterCommit(logoPublicId);
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "brand", key = "'id:' + #id")
    public BrandResponse getById(Long id) {
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));
        BrandResponse response = brandMapper.toBrandResponse(brand);
        response.setProductCount(productRepository.countByBrandIdAndProductStatus(id, ProductStatus.ACTIVE));
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "brand", key = "'slug:' + #slug")
    public BrandResponse getBySlug(String slug) {
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findBySlug(slug)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));
        BrandResponse response = brandMapper.toBrandResponse(brand);
        response.setProductCount(productRepository.countByBrandIdAndProductStatus(brand.getId(), ProductStatus.ACTIVE));
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "brands", key = "'all'")
    public List<BrandResponse> getAll() {
        List<Brand> brands = brandRepository.findAll(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "name"));
        return attachProductCounts(brands);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<BrandResponse> searchBrands(String keyword, Pageable pageable) {
        Page<Brand> page = brandRepository.searchBrands(keyword, pageable);
        List<BrandResponse> content = attachProductCounts(page.getContent());

        return PageResponse.<BrandResponse>builder()
                .pageNo(page.getNumber())
                .pageSize(page.getSize())
                .totalElements(page.getTotalElements())
                .totalPages(page.getTotalPages())
                .isFirst(page.isFirst())
                .isLast(page.isLast())
                .content(content)
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * Cáº­p nháº­t logo báº±ng URL thuáº§n (khÃ´ng qua file upload) â€” chá»‰ dÃ¹ng trong cÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng há»£p
     * migrate dá»¯ liá»‡u cÅ© hoáº·c quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn biáº¿t cháº¯c URL Cloudinary há»£p lá»‡.
     * KhÃ´ng xÃ³a áº£nh cÅ© trÃªn Cloudinary vÃ¬ khÃ´ng cÃ³ publicId cÅ© Ä‘Ã¡ng tin cáº­y.
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    @Caching(evict = {
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brand", allEntries = true),
            @CacheEvict(cacheNames = "brands", allEntries = true)
    })
    public BrandResponse updateLogo(Long id, String newLogoUrl) {
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));

        brand.setLogoUrl(newLogoUrl);
        // KhÃ´ng clear logoPublicId vÃ¬ URL thuáº§n khÃ´ng kÃ¨m publicId má»›i;
        // Ä‘á»ƒ publicId cÅ© trÃ¡nh máº¥t thÃ´ng tin quáº£n lÃ½.

        BrandResponse response = brandMapper.toBrandResponse(brandRepository.save(brand));
        response.setProductCount(productRepository.countByBrandIdAndProductStatus(id, ProductStatus.ACTIVE));
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "brands", key = "'category:' + #categoryId")
    public List<BrandResponse> getBrandsByCategory(Long categoryId) {
        List<Long> categoryIds = categoryService.getSelfAndDescendantIds(categoryId);
        List<Brand> brands = brandRepository.findActiveBrandsByCategoryIds(categoryIds, ProductStatus.ACTIVE);
        return attachProductCounts(brands);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "brands", key = "'type:' + #type.name()")
    public List<BrandResponse> getBrandsByProductType(ProductType type) {
        List<Brand> brands = brandRepository.findActiveBrandsByProductType(type, ProductStatus.ACTIVE);
        return attachProductCounts(brands);
    }

    // ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------

    private List<BrandResponse> attachProductCounts(List<Brand> brands) {
        if (brands.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }

        Map<Long, Long> countMap = productRepository.countProductsGroupByBrand(ProductStatus.ACTIVE)
                .stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(
                        row -> (Long) row[0],
                        row -> (Long) row[1]
                ));

        return brands.stream()
                .map(brand -> {
                    BrandResponse res = brandMapper.toBrandResponse(brand);
                    res.setProductCount(countMap.getOrDefault(brand.getId(), 0L));
                    return res;
                })
                .toList();
    }

    /** Race giá»¯a 2 request cÃ¹ng táº¡o/sá»­a slug: unique constraint DB cháº·n láº§n ghi thá»© hai. */
    private Brand saveBrand(Brand brand) {
        try {
            return brandRepository.saveAndFlush(brand);
        } catch (DataIntegrityViolationException e) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Náº¿u publicId logo thá»±c sá»± thay Ä‘á»•i â†’ lÃªn lá»‹ch xÃ³a áº£nh cÅ© SAU KHI commit.
     * So sÃ¡nh theo publicId (khÃ´ng theo URL) Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh phá»¥ thuá»™c vÃ o format URL Cloudinary.
     */
    private void deleteOldLogoAfterCommit(String oldPublicId, String newPublicId) {
        if (oldPublicId != null && !oldPublicId.isBlank() && !oldPublicId.equals(newPublicId)) {
            fileService.deleteAfterCommit(oldPublicId);
        }
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\service\impl\CategoryServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache.CatalogCacheEvictor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.CategoryRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.CategoryResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Category;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.mapper.CategoryMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.CategoryRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.CategoryService;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.Cacheable;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class CategoryServiceImpl implements CategoryService {

    CategoryRepository categoryRepository;
    ProductRepository productRepository;
    CategoryMapper categoryMapper;
    CatalogCacheEvictor cacheEvictor;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CategoryResponse create(CategoryRequest request) {
        if (categoryRepository.existsBySlug(request.getSlug().trim())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }

        Category category = categoryMapper.toCategory(request);
        category.setName(request.getName().trim());
        category.setSlug(request.getSlug().trim().toLowerCase());
        if (category.getDisplayOrder() == null) {
            category.setDisplayOrder(0);
        }

        if (request.getParentId() != null) {
            Category parent = categoryRepository.findById(request.getParentId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_PARENT_NOT_FOUND));
            category.setParent(parent);
        }

        Category saved = categoryRepository.save(category);
        cacheEvictor.evictCategories();
        return categoryMapper.toCategoryResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CategoryResponse update(Long id, CategoryRequest request) {
        Category category = categoryRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND));

        if (!category.getSlug().equalsIgnoreCase(request.getSlug().trim())
                && categoryRepository.existsBySlug(request.getSlug().trim())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }

        categoryMapper.updateCategory(category, request);
        category.setName(request.getName().trim());
        category.setSlug(request.getSlug().trim().toLowerCase());

        if (request.getParentId() != null) {
            if (request.getParentId().equals(id) || getSelfAndDescendantIds(id).contains(request.getParentId())) {
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_SELF_PARENT);
            }
            Category parent = categoryRepository.findById(request.getParentId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_PARENT_NOT_FOUND));
            category.setParent(parent);
        } else {
            category.setParent(null);
        }

        Category saved = categoryRepository.save(category);
        cacheEvictor.evictCategories();
        return categoryMapper.toCategoryResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Category category = categoryRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND));

        if (!categoryRepository.findByParentId(id).isEmpty()) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_HAS_CHILDREN);
        }
        if (productRepository.existsByCategoryId(id)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_CATEGORY_INVALID,
                    "Cannot delete category because it still contains products");
        }

        categoryRepository.delete(category);
        cacheEvictor.evictCategories();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "category", key = "'id:' + #id")
    public CategoryResponse getById(Long id) {
        Category category = categoryRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND));
        CategoryResponse response = categoryMapper.toCategoryResponse(category);
        response.setChildren(getChildrenByParentId(id));
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "category", key = "'slug:' + #slug")
    public CategoryResponse getBySlug(String slug) {
        Category category = categoryRepository.findBySlug(slug)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND));
        CategoryResponse response = categoryMapper.toCategoryResponse(category);
        response.setChildren(getChildrenByParentId(category.getId()));
        return response;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<CategoryResponse> getChildrenByParentId(Long parentId) {
        return categoryRepository.findByParentId(parentId).stream()
                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(Category::getDisplayOrder, Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.naturalOrder())))
                .map(categoryMapper::toCategoryResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "categoryTree", key = "'all'")
    public List<CategoryResponse> getTree() {
        List<Category> allCategories = categoryRepository.findAll();
        Map<Long, CategoryResponse> dtoMap = allCategories.stream()
                .map(cat -> {
                    CategoryResponse res = categoryMapper.toCategoryResponse(cat);
                    res.setChildren(new ArrayList<>());
                    return res;
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(CategoryResponse::getId, res -> res));

        List<CategoryResponse> roots = new ArrayList<>();

        for (CategoryResponse dto : dtoMap.values()) {
            if (dto.getParentId() == null) {
                roots.add(dto);
            } else {
                CategoryResponse parentDto = dtoMap.get(dto.getParentId());
                if (parentDto != null) {
                    parentDto.getChildren().add(dto);
                }
            }
        }

        Comparator<CategoryResponse> orderComparator = Comparator.comparing(
                CategoryResponse::getDisplayOrder, Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.naturalOrder())
        );
        roots.sort(orderComparator);
        dtoMap.values().forEach(node -> node.getChildren().sort(orderComparator));

        return roots;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<Long> getSelfAndDescendantIds(Long categoryId) {
        List<Category> allCategories = categoryRepository.findAll();
        Map<Long, List<Long>> parentToChildrenMap = new HashMap<>();

        for (Category cat : allCategories) {
            if (cat.getParent() != null) {
                parentToChildrenMap
                        .computeIfAbsent(cat.getParent().getId(), k -> new ArrayList<>())
                        .add(cat.getId());
            }
        }

        List<Long> resultIds = new ArrayList<>();
        Queue<Long> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();
        queue.add(categoryId);

        while (!queue.isEmpty()) {
            Long currentId = queue.poll();
            resultIds.add(currentId);
            queue.addAll(parentToChildrenMap.getOrDefault(currentId, Collections.emptyList()));
        }

        return resultIds;
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\service\impl\InventoryServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache.CatalogCacheEvictor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductVariantRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.ProductVariantResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductColor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductVariant;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.mapper.ProductMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductColorRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductVariantRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.InventoryService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.InventoryStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.VariantStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class InventoryServiceImpl implements InventoryService {

    ProductColorRepository colorRepository;
    ProductVariantRepository variantRepository;
    ProductMapper productMapper;
    CatalogCacheEvictor cacheEvictor;

    // =========================================================================
    // QUáº¢N TRá»Š VARIANT
    // =========================================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductVariantResponse addVariant(Long productId, ProductVariantRequest request) {
        String sku = request.getSku().trim();
        if (variantRepository.existsBySku(sku)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_SKU_EXISTED);
        }

        ProductColor color = requireColor(productId, request.getColorId());

        ProductVariant variant = ProductVariant.builder()
                .color(color)
                .sku(sku)
                .size(request.getSize())
                .stock(request.getStock())
                .active(request.getActive() != null ? request.getActive() : VariantStatus.ACTIVE)
                .inventoryStatus(resolveInventoryStatus(request.getStock(), request.getInventoryStatus()))
                .build();

        ProductVariant saved = saveVariant(variant);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductVariantResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductVariantResponse updateVariant(Long productId, Long variantId, ProductVariantRequest request) {
        // KhÃ³a dÃ²ng (FOR UPDATE): deductStock Ä‘ang cháº¡y song song sáº½ pháº£i chá», khÃ´ng bá»‹ ghi Ä‘Ã¨ stock.
        ProductVariant variant = variantRepository.findByIdForUpdate(variantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND));
        if (!variant.getColor().getProduct().getId().equals(productId)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND);
        }

        String sku = request.getSku().trim();
        if (!variant.getSku().equalsIgnoreCase(sku) && variantRepository.existsBySku(sku)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_SKU_EXISTED);
        }

        // Cho phÃ©p chuyá»ƒn variant sang mÃ u khÃ¡c cá»§a cÃ¹ng product.
        if (!variant.getColor().getId().equals(request.getColorId())) {
            variant.setColor(requireColor(productId, request.getColorId()));
        }

        variant.setSku(sku);
        variant.setSize(request.getSize());
        variant.setStock(request.getStock());
        if (request.getActive() != null) {
            variant.setActive(request.getActive());
        }
        variant.setInventoryStatus(resolveInventoryStatus(request.getStock(), request.getInventoryStatus()));

        ProductVariant saved = saveVariant(variant);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductVariantResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteVariant(Long productId, Long variantId) {
        ProductVariant variant = variantRepository.findByIdAndColorProductId(variantId, productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND));

        // XÃ³a má»m: giá»¯ láº¡i dÃ²ng Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng cÅ© / giá» hÃ ng váº«n tham chiáº¿u Ä‘Æ°á»£c (khÃ´ng vi pháº¡m khÃ³a ngoáº¡i).
        variant.setActive(VariantStatus.INACTIVE);
        variantRepository.save(variant);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Tá»’N KHO (gá»i ná»™i bá»™ tá»« order/checkout)
    // =========================================================================

    /**
     * Trá»« tá»“n kho khi checkout. An toÃ n khi nhiá»u request Ä‘á»“ng thá»i nhá» cÃ¢u UPDATE nguyÃªn tá»­
     * (Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n stock >= qty náº±m ngay trong cÃ¢u lá»‡nh), khÃ´ng cáº§n Redis lock.
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductVariantResponse deductStock(Long variantId, int quantity) {
        if (quantity <= 0) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_QUANTITY_INVALID);
        }

        int updated = variantRepository.deductStockAtomic(variantId, quantity, VariantStatus.ACTIVE);
        if (updated == 0) {
            // 0 dÃ²ng: phÃ¢n biá»‡t 3 nguyÃªn nhÃ¢n Ä‘á»ƒ tráº£ Ä‘Ãºng lá»—i cho client.
            ProductVariant variant = variantRepository.findById(variantId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND));
            if (variant.getActive() != VariantStatus.ACTIVE) {
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INACTIVE);
            }
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INSUFFICIENT_STOCK,
                    "Insufficient stock for SKU: " + variant.getSku());
        }

        // Háº¿t hÃ ng thÃ¬ Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i (cÃ¢u lá»‡nh riÃªng, cháº¡y khi dÃ²ng cÃ²n Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c khÃ³a bá»Ÿi transaction nÃ y).
        variantRepository.markOutOfStockIfEmpty(variantId, InventoryStatus.OUT_OF_STOCK);

        ProductVariant fresh = variantRepository.findById(variantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND));
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(fresh.getColor().getProduct().getId());
        return productMapper.toProductVariantResponse(fresh);
    }

    /**
     * HoÃ n kho khi há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n / háº¿t háº¡n thanh toÃ¡n.
     * LÆ¯U Ã: hÃ m nÃ y chÆ°a idempotent. OrderService pháº£i Ä‘áº£m báº£o má»—i Ä‘Æ¡n chá»‰ hoÃ n kho Ä‘Ãºng má»™t láº§n
     * (vd kiá»ƒm tra chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i PENDING_PAYMENT -> CANCELLED/EXPIRED trong cÃ¹ng transaction).
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductVariantResponse restoreStock(Long variantId, int quantity) {
        if (quantity <= 0) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_QUANTITY_INVALID);
        }

        if (variantRepository.restoreStockAtomic(variantId, quantity) == 0) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        variantRepository.markInStockIfAvailable(variantId, InventoryStatus.IN_STOCK);

        ProductVariant fresh = variantRepository.findById(variantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_NOT_FOUND));
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(fresh.getColor().getProduct().getId());
        return productMapper.toProductVariantResponse(fresh);
    }

    // ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------

    private ProductColor requireColor(Long productId, Long colorId) {
        return colorRepository.findByIdAndProductId(colorId, productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_COLOR_NOT_FOUND));
    }

    /**
     * CÃ³ hÃ ng -> luÃ´n IN_STOCK. Háº¿t hÃ ng -> OUT_OF_STOCK, trá»« khi admin chá»§ Ä‘á»™ng Ä‘áº·t BACKORDER/IN_ORDER.
     * (KhÃ´ng Ä‘á»ƒ admin set IN_STOCK khi stock = 0 nhÆ° trÆ°á»›c.)
     */
    private InventoryStatus resolveInventoryStatus(int stock, InventoryStatus requested) {
        if (stock > 0) {
            return InventoryStatus.IN_STOCK;
        }
        if (requested == InventoryStatus.BACKORDER || requested == InventoryStatus.IN_ORDER) {
            return requested;
        }
        return InventoryStatus.OUT_OF_STOCK;
    }

    /** Race giá»¯a 2 request cÃ¹ng táº¡o 1 SKU: unique constraint cá»§a DB cháº·n láº§n ghi thá»© hai. */
    private ProductVariant saveVariant(ProductVariant variant) {
        try {
            return variantRepository.saveAndFlush(variant);
        } catch (DataIntegrityViolationException e) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_SKU_EXISTED);
        }
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\service\impl\ProductAttributeServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache.CatalogCacheEvictor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductAttributeRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.ProductAttributeResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Product;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductAttribute;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.mapper.ProductMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductAttributeRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.ProductAttributeService;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class ProductAttributeServiceImpl implements ProductAttributeService {

    ProductRepository productRepository;
    ProductAttributeRepository attributeRepository;
    ProductMapper productMapper;
    CatalogCacheEvictor cacheEvictor;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductAttributeResponse addAttribute(Long productId, ProductAttributeRequest request) {
        Product product = productRepository.findById(productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND));

        ProductAttribute attribute = ProductAttribute.builder()
                .product(product)
                .attrName(request.getAttrName().trim())
                .attrValue(request.getAttrValue().trim())
                .build();

        ProductAttribute saved = attributeRepository.save(attribute);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductAttributeResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductAttributeResponse updateAttribute(Long productId, Long attributeId, ProductAttributeRequest request) {
        ProductAttribute attribute = attributeRepository.findByIdAndProductId(attributeId, productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_ATTRIBUTE_NOT_FOUND));

        attribute.setAttrName(request.getAttrName().trim());
        attribute.setAttrValue(request.getAttrValue().trim());

        ProductAttribute saved = attributeRepository.save(attribute);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductAttributeResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteAttribute(Long productId, Long attributeId) {
        ProductAttribute attribute = attributeRepository.findByIdAndProductId(attributeId, productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_ATTRIBUTE_NOT_FOUND));

        attributeRepository.delete(attribute);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache.CatalogCacheEvictor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductColorRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductImageRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.ProductColorResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.ProductImageResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Product;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductColor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductImage;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.mapper.ProductMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductColorRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductImageRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.ProductVariantRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.ProductMediaService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ImagePriority;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.file.dto.FileUploadResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.file.enums.FileFolder;
import org.example.smashhub.file.service.FileService;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class ProductMediaServiceImpl implements ProductMediaService {

    ProductRepository productRepository;
    ProductColorRepository colorRepository;
    ProductImageRepository imageRepository;
    ProductVariantRepository variantRepository;
    ProductMapper productMapper;
    CatalogCacheEvictor cacheEvictor;
    FileService fileService;

    // =========================================================================
    // COLOR
    // =========================================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductColorResponse addColor(Long productId, ProductColorRequest request) {
        Product product = productRepository.findById(productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND));

        String colorName = request.getColorName().trim();
        if (colorRepository.existsByProductIdAndColorNameIgnoreCase(productId, colorName)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_COLOR_NAME_EXISTED);
        }

        ProductColor color = ProductColor.builder()
                .product(product)
                .colorName(colorName)
                .hexCode(request.getHexCode())
                .displayOrder(request.getDisplayOrder() != null ? request.getDisplayOrder() : 0)
                .build();

        ProductColor saved = colorRepository.save(color);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductColorResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductColorResponse updateColor(Long productId, Long colorId, ProductColorRequest request) {
        ProductColor color = requireColor(productId, colorId);

        String colorName = request.getColorName().trim();
        if (colorRepository.existsByProductIdAndColorNameIgnoreCaseAndIdNot(productId, colorName, colorId)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_COLOR_NAME_EXISTED);
        }

        color.setColorName(colorName);
        if (request.getHexCode() != null) {
            color.setHexCode(request.getHexCode());
        }
        if (request.getDisplayOrder() != null) {
            color.setDisplayOrder(request.getDisplayOrder());
        }

        ProductColor saved = colorRepository.save(color);
        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductColorResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteColor(Long productId, Long colorId) {
        ProductColor color = requireColor(productId, colorId);
        if (variantRepository.existsByColorId(colorId)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_COLOR_HAS_VARIANTS);
        }

        // Thu tháº­p publicId cá»§a táº¥t cáº£ áº£nh thuá»™c mÃ u nÃ y TRÆ¯á»šC KHI xÃ³a.
        List<ProductImage> images = imageRepository.findByColorIdOrderByOrderIndexAsc(colorId);
        List<String> publicIds = images.stream()
                .map(ProductImage::getProviderPublicId)
                .filter(pid -> pid != null && !pid.isBlank())
                .collect(Collectors.toList());

        imageRepository.deleteAll(images);
        colorRepository.delete(color);

        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        // XÃ³a áº£nh Cloudinary SAU KHI transaction commit thÃ nh cÃ´ng.
        publicIds.forEach(fileService::deleteAfterCommit);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // IMAGE
    // =========================================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductImageResponse addImage(Long productId, ProductImageRequest request, MultipartFile file) {
        // Pháº£i cÃ³ file hoáº·c url há»£p lá»‡. Kiá»ƒm tra sá»›m Ä‘á»ƒ request thiáº¿u dá»¯ liá»‡u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»¥ng DB/Cloudinary.
        boolean hasFile = fileService.isPresent(file);
        if (!hasFile && isBlank(request.getUrl())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_IMAGE_URL_INVALID);
        }

        ProductColor color = requireColor(productId, request.getColorId());

        String url = request.getUrl();
        String publicId = request.getProviderPublicId();

        if (hasFile) {
            // Upload vÃ o Ä‘Ãºng thÆ° má»¥c sáº£n pháº©m; rollback transaction â†’ áº£nh bá»‹ dá»n tá»± Ä‘á»™ng.
            FileUploadResponse uploaded = fileService.uploadInTransaction(file, FileFolder.PRODUCT);
            url = uploaded.getUrl();
            publicId = uploaded.getPublicId();
        }

        ImagePriority priority = request.getIsMain();
        if (priority == null) {
            // Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh: náº¿u mÃ u chÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh MAIN thÃ¬ áº£nh má»›i lÃ  MAIN, ngÆ°á»£c láº¡i lÃ  SECONDARY.
            priority = imageRepository.hasImageWithPriority(color.getId(), ImagePriority.MAIN)
                    ? ImagePriority.SECONDARY
                    : ImagePriority.MAIN;
        }

        ProductImage image = ProductImage.builder()
                .color(color)
                .url(url)
                // LÆ°u publicId; náº¿u áº£nh tá»« URL ngoÃ i khÃ´ng cÃ³ publicId thÃ¬ Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng (khÃ´ng null Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh NPE).
                .providerPublicId(publicId != null ? publicId : "")
                .title(request.getTitle())
                .altText(request.getAltText())
                .isMain(priority)
                .orderIndex(request.getOrderIndex() != null ? request.getOrderIndex() : 0)
                .build();

        ProductImage saved = imageRepository.save(image);

        // Má»—i mÃ u chá»‰ cÃ³ 1 áº£nh MAIN â€” háº¡ cáº¥p cÃ¡c áº£nh cÃ²n láº¡i.
        if (saved.getIsMain() == ImagePriority.MAIN) {
            imageRepository.demoteOthers(color.getId(), saved.getId(), ImagePriority.SECONDARY);
        }

        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductImageResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductImageResponse updateImage(Long productId, Long imageId,
                                            ProductImageRequest request, MultipartFile file) {
        ProductImage image = requireImage(productId, imageId);

        Long oldColorId = image.getColor().getId();
        String oldPublicId = image.getProviderPublicId();

        // Cho phÃ©p chuyá»ƒn áº£nh sang mÃ u khÃ¡c trong cÃ¹ng product.
        if (!oldColorId.equals(request.getColorId())) {
            image.setColor(requireColor(productId, request.getColorId()));
        }

        if (fileService.isPresent(file)) {
            // CÃ³ file má»›i: upload â†’ gÃ¡n url vÃ  publicId má»›i; rollback â†’ áº£nh má»›i bá»‹ dá»n.
            FileUploadResponse uploaded = fileService.uploadInTransaction(file, FileFolder.PRODUCT);
            image.setUrl(uploaded.getUrl());
            image.setProviderPublicId(uploaded.getPublicId());
        } else {
            // KhÃ´ng cÃ³ file: chá»‰ cáº­p nháº­t url / publicId náº¿u client gá»­i tÆ°á»ng minh.
            if (!isBlank(request.getUrl())) {
                image.setUrl(request.getUrl());
            }
            if (request.getProviderPublicId() != null) {
                image.setProviderPublicId(request.getProviderPublicId());
            }
        }

        if (request.getTitle() != null) {
            image.setTitle(request.getTitle());
        }
        if (request.getAltText() != null) {
            image.setAltText(request.getAltText());
        }
        if (request.getIsMain() != null) {
            image.setIsMain(request.getIsMain());
        }
        if (request.getOrderIndex() != null) {
            image.setOrderIndex(request.getOrderIndex());
        }

        ProductImage saved = imageRepository.save(image);
        Long newColorId = saved.getColor().getId();

        if (saved.getIsMain() == ImagePriority.MAIN) {
            imageRepository.demoteOthers(newColorId, saved.getId(), ImagePriority.SECONDARY);
        }
        // Äáº£m báº£o cáº£ mÃ u má»›i láº«n mÃ u cÅ© (náº¿u chuyá»ƒn mÃ u) Ä‘á»u cÃ²n Ä‘Ãºng 1 áº£nh MAIN.
        ensureMainImage(newColorId);
        if (!oldColorId.equals(newColorId)) {
            ensureMainImage(oldColorId);
        }

        // publicId thá»±c sá»± Ä‘á»•i â†’ áº£nh cÅ© trÃªn Cloudinary khÃ´ng cÃ²n dÃ¹ng, xÃ³a sau commit.
        // KhÃ´ng xÃ³a náº¿u publicId trá»‘ng (áº£nh ngoÃ i khÃ´ng cÃ³ publicId Cloudinary).
        String newPublicId = saved.getProviderPublicId();
        if (!Objects.equals(oldPublicId, newPublicId)
                && oldPublicId != null && !oldPublicId.isBlank()) {
            fileService.deleteAfterCommit(oldPublicId);
        }

        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);
        return productMapper.toProductImageResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteImage(Long productId, Long imageId) {
        ProductImage image = requireImage(productId, imageId);

        Long colorId = image.getColor().getId();
        String publicId = image.getProviderPublicId();

        imageRepository.delete(image);
        imageRepository.flush();

        // XÃ³a Ä‘Ãºng áº£nh MAIN â†’ Ä‘áº©y áº£nh káº¿ tiáº¿p lÃªn lÃ m MAIN.
        ensureMainImage(colorId);

        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(productId);

        // XÃ³a Cloudinary SAU COMMIT; bá» qua náº¿u publicId trá»‘ng (áº£nh URL ngoÃ i).
        if (publicId != null && !publicId.isBlank()) {
            fileService.deleteAfterCommit(publicId);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------

    private ProductColor requireColor(Long productId, Long colorId) {
        return colorRepository.findByIdAndProductId(colorId, productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_COLOR_NOT_FOUND));
    }

    private ProductImage requireImage(Long productId, Long imageId) {
        return imageRepository.findByIdAndColorProductId(imageId, productId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_IMAGE_NOT_FOUND));
    }

    /** MÃ u nÃ o cÃ³ áº£nh mÃ  chÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh MAIN â†’ láº¥y áº£nh Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn (theo orderIndex) lÃ m MAIN. */
    private void ensureMainImage(Long colorId) {
        if (imageRepository.hasImageWithPriority(colorId, ImagePriority.MAIN)) {
            return;
        }
        List<ProductImage> images = imageRepository.findByColorIdOrderByOrderIndexAsc(colorId);
        if (!images.isEmpty()) {
            ProductImage first = images.get(0);
            first.setIsMain(ImagePriority.MAIN);
            imageRepository.save(first);
        }
    }

    private boolean isBlank(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank();
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\service\impl\ProductServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.cache.CatalogCacheEvictor;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductSearchRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.response.*;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.*;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.mapper.ProductMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.repository.*;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.CategoryService;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.ProductService;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.specification.ProductSpecification;
import org.example.smashhub.common.dto.PageResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ImagePriority;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.Cacheable;
import org.springframework.dao.DataIntegrityViolationException;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.domain.Specification;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Pháº§n SPU cá»§a catalog. Pháº§n con (attribute/color/image/variant) náº±m á»Ÿ cÃ¡c service riÃªng,
 * á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y chá»‰ Äá»ŒC chÃºng Ä‘á»ƒ ghÃ©p thÃ nh ProductResponse Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§.
 */
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class ProductServiceImpl implements ProductService {

    ProductRepository productRepository;
    CategoryRepository categoryRepository;
    BrandRepository brandRepository;
    ProductAttributeRepository attributeRepository;
    ProductColorRepository colorRepository;
    ProductImageRepository imageRepository;
    ProductVariantRepository variantRepository;

    CategoryService categoryService;
    ProductMapper productMapper;
    CatalogCacheEvictor cacheEvictor;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductResponse createProduct(ProductRequest request) {
        // Chuáº©n hÃ³a slug TRÆ¯á»šC khi kiá»ƒm tra trÃ¹ng (trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã¢y check báº£n chÆ°a lowercase nhÆ°ng lÆ°u báº£n lowercase).
        String slug = normalizeSlug(request.getSlug());
        if (productRepository.existsBySlug(slug)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }
        validatePrices(request.getPrice(), request.getSalePrice());

        Category category = categoryRepository.findById(request.getCategoryId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND));
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findById(request.getBrandId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));

        Product product = productMapper.toProduct(request);
        product.setName(request.getName().trim());
        product.setSlug(slug);
        product.setCategory(category);
        product.setBrand(brand);

        Product saved = saveProduct(product);

        // Cache brand chá»©a productCount nÃªn pháº£i lÃ m má»›i.
        cacheEvictor.evictBrands();
        return assembleFullProductResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProductResponse updateProduct(Long id, ProductRequest request) {
        Product product = productRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND));

        String slug = normalizeSlug(request.getSlug());
        if (!product.getSlug().equals(slug) && productRepository.existsBySlug(slug)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }
        validatePrices(request.getPrice(), request.getSalePrice());

        Category category = categoryRepository.findById(request.getCategoryId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.CATEGORY_NOT_FOUND));
        Brand brand = brandRepository.findById(request.getBrandId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.BRAND_NOT_FOUND));

        productMapper.updateProduct(product, request);
        product.setName(request.getName().trim());
        product.setSlug(slug);
        product.setCategory(category);
        product.setBrand(brand);
        // Mapper dÃ¹ng NullValuePropertyMappingStrategy.IGNORE nÃªn gá»­i salePrice = null sáº½ KHÃ”NG xÃ³a Ä‘Æ°á»£c giÃ¡ khuyáº¿n mÃ£i.
        // GÃ¡n thá»§ cÃ´ng Ä‘á»ƒ null nghÄ©a lÃ  "bá» khuyáº¿n mÃ£i".
        product.setSalePrice(request.getSalePrice());

        Product updated = saveProduct(product);

        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(id);   // Ä‘Ã£ xÃ³a luÃ´n toÃ n bá»™ cache theo slug (ká»ƒ cáº£ slug cÅ©)
        cacheEvictor.evictBrands();
        return assembleFullProductResponse(updated);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteProduct(Long id) {
        Product product = productRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND));

        product.setProductStatus(ProductStatus.DISCONTINUED);
        productRepository.save(product);

        cacheEvictor.evictProduct(id);
        cacheEvictor.evictBrands();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "product", key = "#id")
    public ProductResponse getProductById(Long id) {
        Product product = productRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND));
        return assembleFullProductResponse(product);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    // key lowercase Ä‘á»ƒ "ABC" vÃ  "abc" dÃ¹ng chung má»™t entry cache.
    @Cacheable(cacheNames = "productBySlug", key = "#slug.trim().toLowerCase()")
    public ProductResponse getProductBySlug(String slug) {
        Product product = productRepository.findBySlug(normalizeSlug(slug))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND));
        return assembleFullProductResponse(product);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<ProductSummaryResponse> searchProducts(ProductSearchRequest request, Pageable pageable) {
        List<Long> targetCategoryIds = null;
        if (request.getCategoryId() != null) {
            // Lá»c theo danh má»¥c cha thÃ¬ gá»“m cáº£ toÃ n bá»™ danh má»¥c con/chÃ¡u.
            targetCategoryIds = categoryService.getSelfAndDescendantIds(request.getCategoryId());
        }

        Specification<Product> spec = ProductSpecification.build(
                request.getKeyword(),
                targetCategoryIds,
                request.getBrandId(),
                request.getType(),
                request.getStatus(),
                request.getMinPrice(),
                request.getMaxPrice(),
                request.getOnSale(),
                request.getAttributes()
        );

        Page<Product> page = productRepository.findAll(spec, pageable);

        // 1 query láº¥y thumbnail cho cáº£ trang (thay vÃ¬ 1 query / sáº£n pháº©m).
        Map<Long, String> thumbnails = resolveThumbnails(
                page.getContent().stream().map(Product::getId).collect(Collectors.toList()));

        List<ProductSummaryResponse> content = page.getContent().stream()
                .map(product -> {
                    ProductSummaryResponse summary = productMapper.toProductSummaryResponse(product);
                    summary.setThumbnailUrl(thumbnails.get(product.getId()));
                    return summary;
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toList());

        return PageResponse.<ProductSummaryResponse>builder()
                .pageNo(page.getNumber())
                .pageSize(page.getSize())
                .totalElements(page.getTotalElements())
                .totalPages(page.getTotalPages())
                .isFirst(page.isFirst())
                .isLast(page.isLast())
                .content(content)
                .build();
    }

    // ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------

    private String normalizeSlug(String slug) {
        return slug.trim().toLowerCase();
    }

    /** GiÃ¡ khuyáº¿n mÃ£i (náº¿u cÃ³) pháº£i > 0 vÃ  nhá» hÆ¡n giÃ¡ gá»‘c. */
    private void validatePrices(BigDecimal price, BigDecimal salePrice) {
        if (salePrice != null && (salePrice.signum() <= 0 || salePrice.compareTo(price) >= 0)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_SALE_PRICE_INVALID);
        }
    }

    /** Race giá»¯a 2 request cÃ¹ng táº¡o 1 slug: check existsBySlug Ä‘á»u qua, unique constraint cá»§a DB cháº·n láº§n ghi thá»© hai. */
    private Product saveProduct(Product product) {
        try {
            return productRepository.saveAndFlush(product);
        } catch (DataIntegrityViolationException e) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_SLUG_EXISTED);
        }
    }

    /** Thumbnail cho nhiá»u sáº£n pháº©m tá»« 1 query: Æ°u tiÃªn áº£nh MAIN Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn, khÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ¬ áº£nh Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn. */
    private Map<Long, String> resolveThumbnails(Collection<Long> productIds) {
        if (productIds.isEmpty()) {
            return Collections.emptyMap();
        }
        Map<Long, String> result = new HashMap<>();
        Set<Long> hasMain = new HashSet<>();

        for (Object[] row : imageRepository.findThumbnailCandidates(productIds)) {
            Long productId = (Long) row[0];
            String url = (String) row[1];
            ImagePriority priority = (ImagePriority) row[2];

            if (priority == ImagePriority.MAIN) {
                if (hasMain.add(productId)) {
                    result.put(productId, url); // ghi Ä‘Ã¨ áº£nh thÆ°á»ng Ä‘Ã£ chá»n táº¡m trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³
                }
            } else {
                result.putIfAbsent(productId, url);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    private ProductResponse assembleFullProductResponse(Product product) {
        Long productId = product.getId();
        ProductResponse response = productMapper.toProductResponse(product);

        List<ProductAttribute> attributes = attributeRepository.findByProductId(productId);
        response.setAttributes(new ArrayList<>(productMapper.toProductAttributeResponseList(attributes)));

        List<ProductColor> colors = colorRepository.findByProductIdOrderByDisplayOrderAsc(productId);
        if (colors.isEmpty()) {
            response.setColors(new ArrayList<>());
            return response;
        }

        List<ProductImage> allImages = imageRepository.findByColorProductIdOrderByOrderIndexAsc(productId);
        List<ProductVariant> allVariants = variantRepository.findByColorProductId(productId);

        Map<Long, List<ProductImageResponse>> imagesByColorId = allImages.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                        img -> img.getColor().getId(),
                        Collectors.mapping(productMapper::toProductImageResponse, Collectors.toList())
                ));

        Map<Long, List<ProductVariantResponse>> variantsByColorId = allVariants.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                        v -> v.getColor().getId(),
                        Collectors.mapping(productMapper::toProductVariantResponse, Collectors.toList())
                ));

        // DÃ¹ng ArrayList (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng Collections.emptyList()/Stream.toList()) cho cÃ¡c list náº±m trong object Ä‘Æ°á»£c cache Redis
        // Ä‘á»ƒ Jackson default-typing serialize/deserialize khÃ´ng vÆ°á»›ng cÃ¡c class List báº¥t biáº¿n Ä‘áº·c biá»‡t cá»§a JDK.
        List<ProductColorResponse> colorResponses = colors.stream()
                .map(color -> {
                    ProductColorResponse colorRes = productMapper.toProductColorResponse(color);
                    colorRes.setImages(imagesByColorId.getOrDefault(color.getId(), new ArrayList<>()));
                    colorRes.setVariants(variantsByColorId.getOrDefault(color.getId(), new ArrayList<>()));
                    return colorRes;
                })
                .collect(Collectors.toList());

        response.setColors(colorResponses);
        return response;
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\specification\ProductSpecification.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.specification;

import jakarta.persistence.criteria.*;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.dto.request.ProductAttributeFilter;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.Product;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.entity.ProductAttribute;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.ProductType;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.domain.Specification;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

public class ProductSpecification {
    private ProductSpecification() {}

    public static Specification<Product> hasKeyword(String keyword) {
        if (keyword == null || keyword.isBlank()) return null;
        String pattern = "%" + keyword.trim().toLowerCase() + "%";

        return (root, query, cb) -> cb.or(
                cb.like(cb.lower(root.get("name")), pattern),
                cb.like(cb.lower(root.get("description")), pattern),
                cb.like(cb.lower(root.get("brand").get("name")), pattern),
                cb.like(cb.lower(root.get("category").get("name")), pattern)
        );
    }

    public static Specification<Product> hasCategory(Long categoryId) {
        if (categoryId == null) return null;
        return (root, query, criteriaBuilder) ->
                criteriaBuilder.equal(root.get("category").get("id"), categoryId);
    }

    /**
        Lá»c sáº£n pháº©m theo táº­p há»£p nhiá»u categoryId (cha + toÃ n bá»™ con/chÃ¡u)
     */
    public static Specification<Product> hasCategoryIn(Collection<Long> categoryIds) {
        if (categoryIds == null || categoryIds.isEmpty()) return null;
        return (root, query, cb) -> root.get("category").get("id").in(categoryIds);
    }

    public static Specification<Product> hasBrand(Long brandId) {
        if (brandId == null) return null;
        return (root, query, criteriaBuilder) ->
                criteriaBuilder.equal(root.get("brand").get("id"), brandId);
    }

    public static Specification<Product> hasType(ProductType type) {
        if (type == null) return null;
        return (root, query, cb) -> cb.equal(root.get("type"), type);
    }

    public static Specification<Product> hasStatus(ProductStatus status) {
        if (status == null) return null;
        return (root, query, criteriaBuilder) ->
                criteriaBuilder.equal(root.get("productStatus"), status);
    }

    private static Expression<BigDecimal> effectivePrice(Root<Product> root, CriteriaBuilder cb) {
        return cb.coalesce(root.get("salePrice"), root.get("price"));
    }

    public static Specification<Product> priceFrom(BigDecimal minPrice) {
        if (minPrice == null) return null;
        return (root, query, criteriaBuilder) ->
                criteriaBuilder.greaterThanOrEqualTo(effectivePrice(root, criteriaBuilder), minPrice);
    }

    public static Specification<Product> priceTo(BigDecimal maxPrice) {
        if (maxPrice == null) return null;
        return (root, query, criteriaBuilder) ->
                criteriaBuilder.lessThanOrEqualTo(effectivePrice(root, criteriaBuilder), maxPrice);
    }

    public static Specification<Product> onSaleOnly(Boolean onSale) {
        if (onSale == null || !onSale) return null;
        return (root, query, criteriaBuilder) -> {
            Predicate notNull = criteriaBuilder.isNotNull(root.get("salePrice"));
            Predicate lowerThanPrice = criteriaBuilder.lessThan(root.get("salePrice"), root.get("price"));
            return criteriaBuilder.and(notNull, lowerThanPrice);
        };
    }

    public static Specification<Product> hasAttribute(String attrName, String attrValue) {
        if (attrName == null || attrName.isBlank() || attrValue == null || attrValue.isBlank())
            return null;
        return (root, query, cb) -> {
            Subquery<Long> subquery = query.subquery(Long.class);
            var attrRoot = subquery.from(ProductAttribute.class);
            subquery.select(attrRoot.get("product").get("id"));
            subquery.where(
                    cb.equal(cb.lower(attrRoot.get("attrName")), attrName.trim().toLowerCase()),
                    cb.equal(cb.lower(attrRoot.get("attrValue")), attrValue.trim().toLowerCase())
            );
            return cb.in(root.get("id")).value(subquery);
        };
    }

    public static Specification<Product> hasAttributes(List<ProductAttributeFilter> attributes) {
        if (attributes == null || attributes.isEmpty()) return null;

        Specification<Product> combined = Specification.allOf();
        for (ProductAttributeFilter filter : attributes) {
            Specification<Product> one = hasAttribute(filter.getAttrName(), filter.getAttrValue());
            if (one != null) combined = combined.and(one);
        }
        return combined;
    }

    public static Specification<Product> build(String keyword,
                                               Collection<Long> categoryIds,
                                               Long brandId,
                                               ProductType type,
                                               ProductStatus status,
                                               BigDecimal minPrice,
                                               BigDecimal maxPrice,
                                               Boolean onSale,
                                               List<ProductAttributeFilter> attributes) {
        List<Specification<Product>> specs = Stream.<Specification<Product>>of(
                        hasKeyword(keyword),
                        hasCategoryIn(categoryIds),
                        hasBrand(brandId),
                        hasType(type),
                        hasStatus(status),
                        priceFrom(minPrice),
                        priceTo(maxPrice),
                        onSaleOnly(onSale),
                        hasAttributes(attributes))
                .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                .toList();

        return Specification.allOf(specs);
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\catalog\util\PriceCalculator.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.catalog.util;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * [Má»šI] Tiá»‡n Ã­ch tÃ­nh toÃ¡n giÃ¡ bÃ¡n thá»‘ng nháº¥t trong toÃ n há»‡ thá»‘ng.
 * Äáº£m báº£o logic tÃ­nh effective price táº­p trung má»™t nÆ¡i duy nháº¥t.
 */
public final class PriceCalculator {

    private PriceCalculator() {}

    /**
     * NÆ¡i duy nháº¥t tÃ­nh giÃ¡ bÃ¡n thá»±c táº¿ cá»§a sáº£n pháº©m (effective price).
     * Náº¿u cÃ³ salePrice > 0 vÃ  salePrice < regularPrice thÃ¬ láº¥y salePrice; ngÆ°á»£c láº¡i láº¥y regularPrice.
     *
     * @param price     GiÃ¡ gá»‘c
     * @param salePrice GiÃ¡ khuyáº¿n mÃ£i
     * @return GiÃ¡ bÃ¡n cÃ³ hiá»‡u lá»±c
     */
    public static BigDecimal effectivePrice(BigDecimal price, BigDecimal salePrice) {
        if (salePrice != null && salePrice.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0
                && (price == null || salePrice.compareTo(price) < 0)) {
            return salePrice;
        }
        return price != null ? price : BigDecimal.ZERO;
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\common\persistence\BaseEntity.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.common.persistence;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.springframework.data.annotation.CreatedDate;
import org.springframework.data.annotation.LastModifiedDate;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.domain.support.AuditingEntityListener;

import java.io.Serializable;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Getter
@Setter
@MappedSuperclass
@EntityListeners(AuditingEntityListener.class)
public abstract class BaseEntity implements Serializable {
    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @CreatedDate
    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createDate;

    @LastModifiedDate
    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updateDate;
}

package org.example.smashhub.config;

import com.cloudinary.Cloudinary;
import com.cloudinary.utils.ObjectUtils;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
public class CloudinaryConfig {
    @Value("${cloudinary.cloud-name}")
    private String cloudName;

    @Value("${cloudinary.api-key}")
    private String apiKey;

    @Value("${cloudinary.api-secret}")
    private String apiSecret;

    @Bean
    public Cloudinary cloudinary(){
        return new Cloudinary(ObjectUtils.asMap(
                "cloud_name", cloudName,
                "api_key", apiKey,
                "api_secret", apiSecret,
                "secure", true
        ));
    }

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\config\CustomJwtDecoder.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.config;

import com.nimbusds.jose.JOSEException;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.dto.request.IntrospectRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.auth.service.JwtService;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.expression.ParseException;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jose.jws.MacAlgorithm;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtDecoder;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtException;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.NimbusJwtDecoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.util.Objects;

@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
@Component
@Configuration
public class CustomJwtDecoder implements JwtDecoder {
    @Value("${jwt.signerKey}")
    private String signerKey;

    private final JwtService jwtService;
    private NimbusJwtDecoder nimbusJwtDecoder = null;

    @Override
    public Jwt decode(String token) throws JwtException {
        try {
            var response = jwtService.introspect(IntrospectRequest.builder()
                    .token(token)
                    .build());
            if (!response.isValid())
                throw new JwtException("Token invalid");
        } catch (JOSEException | ParseException | java.text.ParseException e) {
            throw new JwtException(e.getMessage());
        }

        if (Objects.isNull(nimbusJwtDecoder)) {
            SecretKeySpec secretKeySpec = new SecretKeySpec(signerKey.getBytes(), "HmacSHA256");
            nimbusJwtDecoder = NimbusJwtDecoder
                    .withSecretKey(secretKeySpec)
                    .macAlgorithm(MacAlgorithm.HS256)
                    .build();
        }

        return nimbusJwtDecoder.decode(token);
    }
}
