=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\config\JwtAuthenticationEntryPoint.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.config;

import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException;
import org.springframework.security.web.AuthenticationEntryPoint;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import tools.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.io.IOException;
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class JwtAuthenticationEntryPoint implements AuthenticationEntryPoint {
    @Override
    public void commence(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response, AuthenticationException authException)
            throws IOException, ServletException {
            ErrorCode errorCode = ErrorCode.UNAUTHENTICATED;

        response.setStatus(errorCode.getStatusCode().value());
        response.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE);

        ApiResponse<?> apiResponse = ApiResponse.builder()
                .code(errorCode.getCode())
                .message(errorCode.getMessage())
                .build();

        ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

        response.getWriter().write(objectMapper.writeValueAsString(apiResponse));
        response.flushBuffer();
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\config\RedisConfig.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.config;


import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary;
import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisConnectionFactory;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.serializer.StringRedisSerializer;

@Configuration
public class RedisConfig {
    @Primary
    @Bean
    public RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate(RedisConnectionFactory connectionFactory) {
        RedisTemplate<String, String> template = new RedisTemplate<>();
        template.setConnectionFactory(connectionFactory);
        template.setKeySerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        template.setValueSerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        template.setHashKeySerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        template.setHashValueSerializer(new StringRedisSerializer());
        template.afterPropertiesSet();
        return template;
    }
}




=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\config\SecurityConfig.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.config;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.method.configuration.EnableMethodSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.configurers.AbstractHttpConfigurer;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.server.resource.authentication.JwtAuthenticationConverter;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.server.resource.authentication.JwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfiguration;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfigurationSource;
import org.springframework.web.cors.UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource;

import java.util.List;

@Configuration
// Báº­t @PreAuthorize trÃªn controller. AccessDeniedException nÃ©m ra tá»« Ä‘Ã¢y sáº½ Ä‘i
// qua GlobalExceptionHandler -> 403 JSON.
@EnableMethodSecurity
public class SecurityConfig {

        private static final String[] PUBLIC_POST_ENDPOINTS = {
                        "/auth/login", "/users",
                        "/auth/introspect", "/auth/logout",
                        "/auth/refresh", "/auth/refresh-token",
                        "/auth/forgot-password",
                        "/auth/resend-otp",
                        "/auth/reset-password", "/auth/verify-account",
                        "/shipping/ghn/webhook", // [Má»šI] Webhook GHN (xÃ¡c thá»±c token riÃªng)
                        "/shipping/fee", // [Má»šI] TÃ­nh phÃ­ ship cho khÃ¡ch vÃ£ng lai & user
                        "/payment/vnpay/ipn", // [Má»šI] VNPay IPN (náº¿u gá»i báº±ng POST)
        };

        // Dá»¯ liá»‡u cÃ´ng khai xem Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÃ  khÃ´ng cáº§n Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p.
        private static final String[] PUBLIC_GET_ENDPOINTS = {
                        "/auth/reset-password/validate",
                        "/products",
                        "/products/**",
                        "/brands",
                        "/brands/**",
                        "/categories",
                        "/categories/**",
                        "/locations/**", // [Má»šI] Xem danh sÃ¡ch tá»‰nh/quáº­n/phÆ°á»ng
                        "/payment/vnpay/ipn", // [Má»šI] VNPay IPN callback
                        "/payment/vnpay/return", // [Má»šI] VNPay Return URL redirect cho khÃ¡ch
        };

        // Cart supports both guest (X-Cart-Token) and authenticated (JWT) users.
        // Auth resolution is handled inside CartService, not at the security layer.
        private static final String[] PUBLIC_CART_ENDPOINTS = {
                        "/cart",
                        "/cart/**",
                        "/cart/items",
                        "/cart/items/**",
                        "/cart/merge",
        };

        @Autowired
        private CustomJwtDecoder customJwtDecoder;

        @Bean
        public PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {
                return new BCryptPasswordEncoder(10);
        }

        @Bean
        public SecurityFilterChain filterChain(HttpSecurity httpSecurity) {
                httpSecurity.authorizeHttpRequests(
                                request -> request.requestMatchers(HttpMethod.POST, PUBLIC_POST_ENDPOINTS).permitAll()
                                                .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.GET, PUBLIC_GET_ENDPOINTS).permitAll()
                                                .requestMatchers(PUBLIC_CART_ENDPOINTS).permitAll()
                                                .anyRequest().authenticated());

                httpSecurity.oauth2ResourceServer(oauth2 -> oauth2
                                .jwt(jwtConfigurer -> jwtConfigurer.decoder(customJwtDecoder)
                                                .jwtAuthenticationConverter(jwtAuthenticationConverter()))
                                .authenticationEntryPoint(new JwtAuthenticationEntryPoint()));
                httpSecurity.cors(cors -> cors.configurationSource(corsConfigurationSource()));
                httpSecurity.csrf(AbstractHttpConfigurer::disable);

                return httpSecurity.build();
        }

        @Bean
        public CorsConfigurationSource corsConfigurationSource() {
                CorsConfiguration configuration = new CorsConfiguration();
                configuration.setAllowedOriginPatterns(List.of("*"));
                configuration.setAllowedMethods(List.of("GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"));
                configuration.setAllowedHeaders(List.of("*"));
                configuration.setExposedHeaders(List.of("X-Cart-Token", "Authorization"));
                configuration.setAllowCredentials(true);

                UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource source = new UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource();
                source.registerCorsConfiguration("/**", configuration);
                return source;
        }

        @Bean
        JwtAuthenticationConverter jwtAuthenticationConverter() {
                JwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter jwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter = new JwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter();
                // Token Ä‘Ã£ chá»©a scope "ROLE_ADMIN", nÃªn khÃ´ng thÃªm prefix SCOPE_ ->
                // hasRole('ADMIN') khá»›p "ROLE_ADMIN".
                jwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter.setAuthorityPrefix("");
                JwtAuthenticationConverter jwtAuthenticationConverter = new JwtAuthenticationConverter();
                jwtAuthenticationConverter.setJwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter(jwtGrantedAuthoritiesConverter);

                return jwtAuthenticationConverter;
        }

}

package org.example.smashhub.exception;

public class AppException extends RuntimeException{
    private ErrorCode errorCode;

    public AppException(ErrorCode errorCode) {
        super(errorCode.getMessage());
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
    }

    /**
     * Dung khi can nhet gia tri dong (VD: so giay con lai) vao message,
     * thay vi dung nguyen message tinh trong ErrorCode. code/status van
     * lay tu errorCode nhu binh thuong.
     */
    public AppException(ErrorCode errorCode, String message) {
        super(message);
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
    }

    public ErrorCode getErrorCode() {
        return errorCode;
    }

    public void setErrorCode(ErrorCode errorCode) {
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\exception\GlobalExceptionHandler.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.exception;

import jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolationException;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.http.converter.HttpMessageNotReadableException;
import org.springframework.security.access.AccessDeniedException;
import org.springframework.validation.FieldError;
import org.springframework.web.HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MethodArgumentNotValidException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MissingServletRequestParameterException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ControllerAdvice;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.method.annotation.MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MaxUploadSizeExceededException;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.resource.NoResourceFoundException;

import java.util.Objects;

@ControllerAdvice
@Slf4j
public class GlobalExceptionHandler {

    /** Lá»—i khÃ´ng lÆ°á»ng trÆ°á»›c -> 500, chá»‰ log chi tiáº¿t á»Ÿ server, khÃ´ng lá»™ ra client. */
    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handlingRuntimeException(Exception exception) {
        log.error("Exception: ", exception);
        return build(ErrorCode.UNCATEGORIZED_EXCEPTION, null);
    }

    /** Lá»—i nghiá»‡p vá»¥ do mÃ¬nh chá»§ Ä‘á»™ng nÃ©m. */
    @ExceptionHandler(AppException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handlingAppException(AppException exception) {
        return build(exception.getErrorCode(), exception.getMessage());
    }

    /**
     * Bá»‹ tá»« chá»‘i quyá»n (@PreAuthorize) -> 403.
     * TrÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã¢y bá»‹ handler Exception.class nuá»‘t vÃ  tráº£ 500.
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(AccessDeniedException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handlingAccessDenied(AccessDeniedException exception) {
        return build(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, null);
    }

    /**
     * Lá»—i validation cá»§a @Valid.
     * message trong DTO cÃ³ thá»ƒ lÃ :
     *  - tÃªn má»™t ErrorCode (vd "PRODUCT_NAME_INVALID") -> dÃ¹ng code/status/message cá»§a ErrorCode Ä‘Ã³
     *  - má»™t cÃ¢u báº¥t ká»³ (vd "email must not be blank")  -> tráº£ INVALID_REQUEST kÃ¨m Ä‘Ãºng cÃ¢u Ä‘Ã³
     * TrÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã¢y ErrorCode.valueOf() nÃ©m IllegalArgumentException cho trÆ°á»ng há»£p thá»© hai (vÃ  cho key bá»‹ thiáº¿u).
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException exception) {
        String key = exception.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors().stream()
                .map(FieldError::getDefaultMessage)
                .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);

        ErrorCode errorCode = resolveErrorCode(key);
        if (errorCode != null) {
            return build(errorCode, null);
        }
        return build(ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, key);
    }

    /** Validation trÃªn @RequestParam / @PathVariable (khi dÃ¹ng @Validated). */
    @ExceptionHandler(ConstraintViolationException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleConstraintViolation(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        String key = exception.getConstraintViolations().stream()
                .map(v -> v.getMessage())
                .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);
        ErrorCode errorCode = resolveErrorCode(key);
        return errorCode != null ? build(errorCode, null) : build(ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, key);
    }

    /** Body JSON sai cÃº phÃ¡p / enum khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ -> 400 thay vÃ¬ 500. */
    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleUnreadable(HttpMessageNotReadableException exception) {
        log.debug("Unreadable request body", exception);
        return build(ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, "Malformed request body");
    }

    /** Sai kiá»ƒu tham sá»‘ (vd ?type=ABC vá»›i enum, ?id=abc vá»›i Long) -> 400. */
    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleTypeMismatch(MethodArgumentTypeMismatchException exception) {
        return build(ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, "Invalid value for parameter '" + exception.getName() + "'");
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(MissingServletRequestParameterException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleMissingParam(MissingServletRequestParameterException exception) {
        return build(ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST, "Missing parameter '" + exception.getParameterName() + "'");
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleMethodNotSupported(HttpRequestMethodNotSupportedException exception) {
        return build(ErrorCode.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED, null);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(NoResourceFoundException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleNoResource(NoResourceFoundException exception) {
        return build(ErrorCode.RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND, null);
    }

    /**
     * File vÆ°á»£t giá»›i háº¡n max-file-size / max-request-size trong application.yaml.
     * Spring nÃ©m exception nÃ y trÆ°á»›c khi request vÃ o controller, nÃªn FileService khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»i.
     */
    @ExceptionHandler(MaxUploadSizeExceededException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> handleMaxUploadSize(MaxUploadSizeExceededException exception) {
        return build(ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE, null);
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private ErrorCode resolveErrorCode(String key) {
        if (key == null) return null;
        try {
            return ErrorCode.valueOf(key);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** Gom viá»‡c dá»±ng response vá» má»™t chá»—: customMessage == null thÃ¬ dÃ¹ng message máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cá»§a ErrorCode. */
    private ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Object>> build(ErrorCode errorCode, String customMessage) {
        ApiResponse<Object> response = new ApiResponse<>();
        response.setCode(errorCode.getCode());
        response.setMessage(customMessage != null ? customMessage : errorCode.getMessage());
        return ResponseEntity
                .status(errorCode.getStatusCode() != null ? errorCode.getStatusCode() : HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .body(response);
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.file.controller;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.file.dto.FileUploadResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.file.enums.FileFolder;
import org.example.smashhub.file.service.FileService;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

/**
 * Endpoint upload/delete áº£nh trá»±c tiáº¿p lÃªn Cloudinary â€” chá»‰ dÃ nh cho ADMIN/STAFF.
 *
 * <p>Upload qua Ä‘Ã¢y KHÃ”NG náº±m trong transaction DB: náº¿u cáº§n transaction an toÃ n,
 * dÃ¹ng endpoint chuyÃªn biá»‡t cá»§a tá»«ng module (BrandController, ProductControllerâ€¦).
 *
 * <p>XÃ³a file: chá»‰ nháº­n {@code publicId} â€” khÃ´ng nháº­n URL Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh client tá»± Ã½
 * xÃ³a áº£nh báº¥t ká»³ trÃªn Cloudinary báº±ng cÃ¡ch giáº£ máº¡o URL.
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/files")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
@PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN','STAFF')")
public class FileController {

    FileService fileService;

    /**
     * Upload áº£nh vÃ o thÆ° má»¥c Cloudinary tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng vá»›i {@code folder}.
     * Client PHáº¢I chá»‰ Ä‘á»‹nh folder báº±ng enum â€” khÃ´ng nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tá»± do.
     *
     * <p>VÃ­ dá»¥: {@code POST /files/upload?folder=PRODUCT}
     */
    @PostMapping(value = "/upload", consumes = "multipart/form-data")
    public ApiResponse<FileUploadResponse> upload(
            @RequestParam("file") MultipartFile file,
            @RequestParam("folder") FileFolder folder) {
        return ApiResponse.<FileUploadResponse>builder()
                .result(fileService.upload(file, folder))
                .message("File uploaded successfully")
                .build();
    }

    /**
     * XÃ³a áº£nh khá»i Cloudinary theo {@code publicId}.
     *
     * <p>LÆ°u Ã½: endpoint nÃ y xÃ³a ngay láº­p tá»©c, khÃ´ng kiá»ƒm tra xem publicId cÃ²n Ä‘Æ°á»£c
     * báº£n ghi DB nÃ o tham chiáº¿u hay khÃ´ng. Chá»‰ dÃ¹ng khi cháº¯c cháº¯n áº£nh khÃ´ng cÃ²n dÃ¹ng.
     *
     * <p>DÃ¹ng {@code @RequestParam} thay vÃ¬ {@code @PathVariable} vÃ¬ public_id cá»§a
     * Cloudinary thÆ°á»ng chá»©a dáº¥u '/' (vd: "smashhub/products/uuid"), náº¿u Ä‘á»ƒ path
     * variable Spring sáº½ hiá»ƒu nháº§m thÃ nh nhiá»u segment.
     */
    @DeleteMapping
    public ApiResponse<Void> delete(@RequestParam("publicId") String publicId) {
        fileService.delete(publicId);
        return ApiResponse.<Void>builder()
                .message("File deleted successfully")
                .build();
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\file\enums\FileFolder.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.file.enums;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;


@Getter
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public enum FileFolder {
    BRAND("brands"),
    CATEGORY("categories"),
    PRODUCT("products"),
    REVIEW("reviews"),
    AVATAR("avatars"),
    // áº¢nh khÃ´ng thuá»™c loáº¡i nÃ o á»Ÿ trÃªn (banner, áº£nh táº¡m...)
    OTHER("others");


    String path;

    /** GhÃ©p thÆ° má»¥c gá»‘c + thÆ° má»¥c con, vd: ("smashhub", PRODUCT) -> "smashhub/products". */
    public String resolve(String rootFolder) {
        if (rootFolder == null || rootFolder.isBlank()) {
            return path;
        }
        String root = rootFolder.endsWith("/")
                ? rootFolder.substring(0, rootFolder.length() - 1)
                : rootFolder;
        return root + "/" + path;
    }
}



=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\file\service\impl\FileServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.file.service.impl;

import com.cloudinary.Cloudinary;
import com.cloudinary.utils.ObjectUtils;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.experimental.NonFinal;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.file.dto.FileUploadResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.file.enums.FileFolder;
import org.example.smashhub.file.service.FileService;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.IntConsumer;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class FileServiceImpl implements FileService {

    // ---- CÃ¡c MIME type client khai bÃ¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c cháº¥p nháº­n ----
    static Set<String> ALLOWED_CONTENT_TYPES = Set.of(
            "image/jpeg", "image/jpg", "image/png", "image/webp"
    );

    // ---- Magic bytes Ä‘á»ƒ xÃ¡c minh Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng thá»±c sá»± cá»§a file (khÃ´ng tin hoÃ n toÃ n MIME) ----
    // JPEG: FF D8 FF
    static byte[] MAGIC_JPEG = {(byte) 0xFF, (byte) 0xD8, (byte) 0xFF};
    // PNG:  89 50 4E 47
    static byte[] MAGIC_PNG  = {(byte) 0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47};
    // WEBP: RIFF....WEBP (byte 0-3: RIFF, byte 8-11: WEBP)
    static byte[] MAGIC_RIFF = {0x52, 0x49, 0x46, 0x46}; // "RIFF"
    static byte[] MAGIC_WEBP = {0x57, 0x45, 0x42, 0x50}; // "WEBP"

    static long MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 MB

    Cloudinary cloudinary;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${cloudinary.folder}")
    String rootFolder;

    // =========================================================================
    // PUBLIC API
    // =========================================================================

    @Override
    public FileUploadResponse upload(MultipartFile file, FileFolder folder) {
        validate(file);
        String targetFolder = folder.resolve(rootFolder);
        return doUpload(file, targetFolder);
    }

    @Override
    public FileUploadResponse uploadInTransaction(MultipartFile file, FileFolder folder) {
        FileUploadResponse uploaded = upload(file, folder);
        String publicId = uploaded.getPublicId();
        // Náº¿u Ä‘ang trong transaction: rollback â†’ dá»n áº£nh; commit â†’ giá»¯ nguyÃªn.
        runAfterCompletion(status -> {
            if (status != TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_COMMITTED) {
                log.info("Transaction rolled back â€” deleting uploaded Cloudinary asset: {}", publicId);
                deleteQuietly(publicId);
            }
        });
        return uploaded;
    }

    @Override
    public void delete(String publicId) {
        if (publicId == null || publicId.isBlank()) return;
        try {
            Map<?, ?> result = cloudinary.uploader()
                    .destroy(publicId, ObjectUtils.asMap("resource_type", "image"));
            // Cloudinary tráº£ "not found" náº¿u áº£nh khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i â€” log cáº£nh bÃ¡o nhÆ°ng khÃ´ng throw.
            Object resultCode = result.get("result");
            if (!"ok".equals(resultCode)) {
                log.warn("Cloudinary destroy returned '{}' for publicId={}", resultCode, publicId);
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            log.error("Delete file {} on Cloudinary failed", publicId, e);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.FILE_DELETE_FAILED);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteAfterCommit(String publicId) {
        if (publicId == null || publicId.isBlank()) return;
        runAfterCommit(() -> deleteQuietly(publicId));
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // PRIVATE HELPERS
    // =========================================================================

    private FileUploadResponse doUpload(MultipartFile file, String folder) {
        try {
            // TÃªn file ngáº«u nhiÃªn Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh ghi Ä‘Ã¨ áº£nh khÃ¡c trong cÃ¹ng thÆ° má»¥c.
            String publicId = folder + "/" + UUID.randomUUID();
            Map<?, ?> result = cloudinary.uploader().upload(
                    file.getBytes(),
                    ObjectUtils.asMap(
                            "public_id", publicId,
                            "resource_type", "image",
                            // overwrite=false vÃ¬ Ä‘Ã£ dÃ¹ng UUID nÃªn khÃ´ng bao giá» trÃ¹ng;
                            // giá»¯ false Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh vÃ´ tÃ¬nh ghi Ä‘Ã¨ náº¿u logic UUID bá»‹ lá»—i.
                            "overwrite", false
                    )
            );
            return FileUploadResponse.builder()
                    .url((String) result.get("secure_url"))
                    .publicId((String) result.get("public_id"))
                    .format((String) result.get("format"))
                    .bytes(result.get("bytes") == null ? null
                            : Long.valueOf(result.get("bytes").toString()))
                    .width(result.get("width") == null ? null
                            : Integer.valueOf(result.get("width").toString()))
                    .height(result.get("height") == null ? null
                            : Integer.valueOf(result.get("height").toString()))
                    .build();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            log.error("Upload file to Cloudinary failed (folder={})", folder, e);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.FILE_UPLOAD_FAILED);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra toÃ n diá»‡n file trÆ°á»›c khi upload:
     * 1. Non-null, non-empty.
     * 2. KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c â‰¤ MAX_FILE_SIZE.
     * 3. MIME type client khai bÃ¡o náº±m trong whitelist.
     * 4. Magic bytes thá»±c sá»± khá»›p vá»›i Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng áº£nh (khÃ´ng tin MIME type mÃ¹ quÃ¡ng).
     */
    private void validate(MultipartFile file) {
        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.FILE_EMPTY);
        }
        if (file.getSize() > MAX_FILE_SIZE) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.FILE_TOO_LARGE);
        }
        String contentType = file.getContentType();
        if (contentType == null || !ALLOWED_CONTENT_TYPES.contains(contentType.toLowerCase())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.FILE_TYPE_NOT_SUPPORTED);
        }
        if (!hasValidMagicBytes(file)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.FILE_TYPE_NOT_SUPPORTED);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Äá»c tá»‘i Ä‘a 12 byte Ä‘áº§u Ä‘á»ƒ xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng thá»±c cá»§a file.
     * TrÃ¡nh trÆ°á»ng há»£p client gá»­i file HTML/script nhÆ°ng khai bÃ¡o Content-Type lÃ  image/jpeg.
     */
    private boolean hasValidMagicBytes(MultipartFile file) {
        try (InputStream is = file.getInputStream()) {
            byte[] header = new byte[12];
            int read = is.read(header);
            if (read < 3) return false;

            // JPEG
            if (startsWith(header, MAGIC_JPEG)) return true;
            // PNG
            if (read >= 4 && startsWith(header, MAGIC_PNG)) return true;
            // WEBP: bytes 0-3 = RIFF, bytes 8-11 = WEBP
            if (read >= 12 && startsWith(header, MAGIC_RIFF)) {
                byte[] webpMark = new byte[4];
                System.arraycopy(header, 8, webpMark, 0, 4);
                if (startsWith(webpMark, MAGIC_WEBP)) return true;
            }
            return false;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            log.warn("Cannot read magic bytes of uploaded file", e);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private boolean startsWith(byte[] data, byte[] prefix) {
        if (data.length < prefix.length) return false;
        for (int i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
            if (data[i] != prefix[i]) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    /** XÃ³a áº£nh mÃ  khÃ´ng throw â€” dÃ¹ng trong afterCompletion / afterCommit hooks. */
    private void deleteQuietly(String publicId) {
        try {
            delete(publicId);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("Could not delete Cloudinary asset '{}' (will be orphaned): {}", publicId, e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ action cháº¡y sau khi transaction káº¿t thÃºc (commit hoáº·c rollback).
     * Náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng thÃ¬ khÃ´ng lÃ m gÃ¬ (caller tá»± quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh fallback).
     */
    private void runAfterCompletion(IntConsumer action) {
        if (!TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
            return;
        }
        TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
            @Override
            public void afterCompletion(int status) {
                action.accept(status);
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Cháº¡y action SAU KHI transaction commit. Náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction, cháº¡y ngay (fallback).
     * ÄÃ¢y lÃ  hÃ nh vi mong muá»‘n cho "xÃ³a áº£nh cÅ©": náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction (vÃ­ dá»¥: endpoint admin
     * xÃ³a trá»±c tiáº¿p), xÃ³a ngay; náº¿u Ä‘ang trong transaction, chá» commit má»›i xÃ³a.
     */
    private void runAfterCommit(Runnable action) {
        if (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
            TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
                @Override
                public void afterCommit() {
                    action.run();
                }
            });
        } else {
            action.run();
        }
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.file.service;

import org.example.smashhub.file.dto.FileUploadResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.file.enums.FileFolder;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

public interface FileService {

    /**
     * Upload file lÃªn Cloudinary vÃ o Ä‘Ãºng thÆ° má»¥c tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng vá»›i {@code folder}.
     * KhÃ´ng dÃ¹ng trong transaction DB â€” khÃ´ng cÃ³ cÆ¡ cháº¿ rollback tá»± Ä‘á»™ng.
     * DÃ¹ng {@link #uploadInTransaction} khi Ä‘ang trong má»™t transaction.
     */
    FileUploadResponse upload(MultipartFile file, FileFolder folder);

    /**
     * XÃ³a file trÃªn Cloudinary ngay láº­p tá»©c theo {@code publicId}.
     * Chá»‰ gá»i trá»±c tiáº¿p tá»« cÃ¡c endpoint admin khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction DB kÃ¨m theo.
     * Trong transaction, dÃ¹ng {@link #deleteAfterCommit}.
     */
    void delete(String publicId);

    /**
     * Upload file trong pháº¡m vi má»™t transaction DB Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng.
     * Náº¿u transaction rollback, áº£nh vá»«a upload trÃªn Cloudinary sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c dá»n tá»± Ä‘á»™ng.
     * Náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction, hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng giá»‘ng {@link #upload}.
     */
    FileUploadResponse uploadInTransaction(MultipartFile file, FileFolder folder);

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ xÃ³a file trÃªn Cloudinary SAU KHI transaction commit thÃ nh cÃ´ng.
     * Náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ transaction Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng, xÃ³a ngay (fallback an toÃ n).
     * Lá»—i xÃ³a áº£nh chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c log, khÃ´ng throw Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh lÃ m há»ng dá»¯ liá»‡u DB Ä‘Ã£ commit.
     */
    void deleteAfterCommit(String publicId);

    /** Kiá»ƒm tra file cÃ³ há»£p lá»‡ (non-null, non-empty) Ä‘á»ƒ upload khÃ´ng. */
    default boolean isPresent(MultipartFile file) {
        return file != null && !file.isEmpty();
    }
}

import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;

import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/addresses")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class AddressController {

    AddressService addressService;

    private String currentUsername() {
        Authentication auth = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        return auth != null ? auth.getName() : null;
    }

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<List<AddressResponse>>> getMyAddresses() {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<List<AddressResponse>>builder()
                .result(addressService.getMyAddresses(currentUsername()))
                .message("Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<AddressResponse>> getAddressById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<AddressResponse>builder()
                .result(addressService.getMyAddressById(currentUsername(), id))
                .build());
    }

    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<AddressResponse>> createAddress(@RequestBody @Valid AddressRequest request) {
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED).body(ApiResponse.<AddressResponse>builder()
                .result(addressService.createAddress(currentUsername(), request))
                .message("ThÃªm Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ má»›i thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<AddressResponse>> updateAddress(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @RequestBody @Valid AddressRequest request) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<AddressResponse>builder()
                .result(addressService.updateAddress(currentUsername(), id, request))
                .message("Cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Void>> deleteAddress(@PathVariable Long id) {
        addressService.deleteAddress(currentUsername(), id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<Void>builder()
                .message("XÃ³a Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @PatchMapping("/{id}/primary")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<AddressResponse>> setPrimaryAddress(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<AddressResponse>builder()
                .result(addressService.setPrimaryAddress(currentUsername(), id))
                .message("Äáº·t lÃ m Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.location.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;

import jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;
import jakarta.persistence.FetchType;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;
import lombok.ToString;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.AddressPriority;
import org.example.smashhub.common.persistence.BaseEntity;
import org.example.smashhub.user.entity.User;

@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@ToString(callSuper = true, exclude = {"user"})
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
@Entity
@Table(name = "addresses")
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE)
public class Address extends BaseEntity {

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "user_id", nullable = false)
    User user;

    @Column(name = "recipient_name", nullable = false)
    String recipientName;

    @Column(name = "phone", length = 20)
    String phone; // [Má»šI] Sá»‘ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i ngÆ°á»i nháº­n hÃ ng táº¡i Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ nÃ y

    @Column(nullable = false)
    String line1;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "province_id", nullable = false)
    Province province;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "district_id", nullable = false)
    District district;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "ward_code", nullable = false)
    Ward ward;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "primary_address", nullable = false)
    AddressPriority primaryAddress;
}

package org.example.smashhub.location.repository;

import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.AddressPriority;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.Address;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Modifying;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@Repository
public interface AddressRepository extends JpaRepository<Address, Long> {

    @Query("""
            SELECT a FROM Address a
            JOIN FETCH a.province p
            JOIN FETCH a.district d
            JOIN FETCH a.ward w
            WHERE a.user.id = :userId
            ORDER BY a.primaryAddress ASC, a.updateDate DESC
            """)
    List<Address> findByUserIdWithDetails(@Param("userId") Long userId);

    @Query("""
            SELECT a FROM Address a
            JOIN FETCH a.province p
            JOIN FETCH a.district d
            JOIN FETCH a.ward w
            WHERE a.id = :id AND a.user.id = :userId
            """)
    Optional<Address> findByIdAndUserId(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("userId") Long userId);

    Optional<Address> findByUserIdAndPrimaryAddress(Long userId, AddressPriority primaryAddress);

    long countByUserId(Long userId);

    @Modifying
    @Query("UPDATE Address a SET a.primaryAddress = :secondary WHERE a.user.id = :userId")
    void resetAllPrimaryForUser(@Param("userId") Long userId, @Param("secondary") AddressPriority secondary);
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\location\scheduler\LocationSyncScheduler.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.location.scheduler;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.location.service.GhnLocationSyncService;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.Duration;

/**
 * [Má»šI] Scheduler Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ Ä‘á»‹nh ká»³ Master Data Ä‘á»‹a giá»›i hÃ nh chÃ­nh tá»« GHN vÃ o DB.
 */
@Component
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class LocationSyncScheduler {

    private static final String LOCK_KEY = "lock:location:sync";

    GhnLocationSyncService syncService;
    StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    @Scheduled(cron = "${ghn.sync-cron:0 0 3 * * ?}")
    public void runLocationSync() {
        Boolean acquired = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(LOCK_KEY, "LOCKED", Duration.ofHours(1));
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(acquired)) {
            log.info("Instance khÃ¡c Ä‘ang thá»±c hiá»‡n LocationSyncScheduler, bá» qua.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            log.info("Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u cháº¡y scheduled Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ Ä‘á»‹a giá»›i hÃ nh chÃ­nh tá»« GHN...");
            String result = syncService.syncAllLocations();
            log.info("Scheduled Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ GHN káº¿t thÃºc: {}", result);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Lá»—i trong quÃ¡ trÃ¬nh cháº¡y LocationSyncScheduler: ", e);
        } finally {
            redisTemplate.delete(LOCK_KEY);
        }
    }
}





=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\location\service\impl\AddressServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.location.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.AddressPriority;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.location.dto.request.AddressRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.location.dto.response.AddressResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.Address;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.District;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.Province;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.Ward;
import org.example.smashhub.location.mapper.AddressMapper;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.AddressRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.DistrictRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.ProvinceRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.WardRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.service.AddressService;
import org.example.smashhub.user.entity.User;
import org.example.smashhub.user.repository.UserRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class AddressServiceImpl implements AddressService {

    AddressRepository addressRepository;
    ProvinceRepository provinceRepository;
    DistrictRepository districtRepository;
    WardRepository wardRepository;
    UserRepository userRepository;
    AddressMapper addressMapper;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AddressResponse> getMyAddresses(String username) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        return addressMapper.toAddressResponseList(addressRepository.findByUserIdWithDetails(user.getId()));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AddressResponse getMyAddressById(String username, Long id) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        Address address = addressRepository.findByIdAndUserId(id, user.getId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND));
        return addressMapper.toAddressResponse(address);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public AddressResponse createAddress(String username, AddressRequest request) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        ValidatedLocation location = validateHierarchy(request.getProvinceId(), request.getDistrictId(), request.getWardCode());

        long count = addressRepository.countByUserId(user.getId());
        boolean makePrimary = count == 0 || Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getPrimary());

        if (makePrimary && count > 0) {
            addressRepository.resetAllPrimaryForUser(user.getId(), AddressPriority.SECONDARY);
        }

        Address address = Address.builder()
                .user(user)
                .recipientName(request.getRecipientName().trim())
                .phone(request.getPhone() != null ? request.getPhone().trim() : user.getPhone())
                .line1(request.getLine1().trim())
                .province(location.province())
                .district(location.district())
                .ward(location.ward())
                .primaryAddress(makePrimary ? AddressPriority.PRIMARY : AddressPriority.SECONDARY)
                .build();

        Address saved = addressRepository.save(address);
        return addressMapper.toAddressResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public AddressResponse updateAddress(String username, Long id, AddressRequest request) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        Address address = addressRepository.findByIdAndUserId(id, user.getId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND));

        ValidatedLocation location = validateHierarchy(request.getProvinceId(), request.getDistrictId(), request.getWardCode());

        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getPrimary()) && address.getPrimaryAddress() != AddressPriority.PRIMARY) {
            addressRepository.resetAllPrimaryForUser(user.getId(), AddressPriority.SECONDARY);
            address.setPrimaryAddress(AddressPriority.PRIMARY);
        }

        address.setRecipientName(request.getRecipientName().trim());
        address.setPhone(request.getPhone() != null ? request.getPhone().trim() : user.getPhone());
        address.setLine1(request.getLine1().trim());
        address.setProvince(location.province());
        address.setDistrict(location.district());
        address.setWard(location.ward());

        Address saved = addressRepository.save(address);
        return addressMapper.toAddressResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteAddress(String username, Long id) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        Address address = addressRepository.findByIdAndUserId(id, user.getId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND));

        boolean wasPrimary = address.getPrimaryAddress() == AddressPriority.PRIMARY;
        addressRepository.delete(address);

        // Náº¿u xÃ³a Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ PRIMARY thÃ¬ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng chá»n Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ gáº§n nháº¥t cÃ²n láº¡i lÃ m PRIMARY
        if (wasPrimary) {
            List<Address> remaining = addressRepository.findByUserIdWithDetails(user.getId());
            if (!remaining.isEmpty()) {
                Address newPrimary = remaining.get(0);
                newPrimary.setPrimaryAddress(AddressPriority.PRIMARY);
                addressRepository.save(newPrimary);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public AddressResponse setPrimaryAddress(String username, Long id) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        Address address = addressRepository.findByIdAndUserId(id, user.getId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND));

        if (address.getPrimaryAddress() != AddressPriority.PRIMARY) {
            addressRepository.resetAllPrimaryForUser(user.getId(), AddressPriority.SECONDARY);
            address.setPrimaryAddress(AddressPriority.PRIMARY);
            address = addressRepository.save(address);
        }
        return addressMapper.toAddressResponse(address);
    }

    // ---------------------- Helpers ----------------------

    private User requireUser(String username) {
        return userRepository.findByUsername(username)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.USER_NOT_EXISTED));
    }

    private record ValidatedLocation(Province province, District district, Ward ward) {}

    private ValidatedLocation validateHierarchy(Integer provinceId, Integer districtId, String wardCode) {
        Province province = provinceRepository.findById(provinceId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.PROVINCE_NOT_FOUND));

        District district = districtRepository.findById(districtId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.DISTRICT_NOT_FOUND));

        Ward ward = wardRepository.findById(wardCode)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.WARD_NOT_FOUND));

        // Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh toÃ n váº¹n phÃ¢n cáº¥p hÃ nh chÃ­nh: district thuá»™c province, ward thuá»™c district
        if (!district.getProvince().getId().equals(province.getId())
                || !ward.getDistrict().getId().equals(district.getId())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ADDRESS_LOCATION_MISMATCH);
        }

        return new ValidatedLocation(province, district, ward);
    }
}





=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\location\service\impl\GhnLocationSyncServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.location.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.config.GhnConfig;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.District;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.Province;
import org.example.smashhub.location.entity.Ward;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.DistrictRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.ProvinceRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.WardRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.service.GhnLocationSyncService;
import org.springframework.cache.CacheManager;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import tools.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * [Má»šI] Äá»“ng bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘á»‹a giá»›i hÃ nh chÃ­nh (Tá»‰nh, Quáº­n, PhÆ°á»ng) tá»« GHN Master Data API.
 * Thá»±c hiá»‡n upsert, khÃ´ng xÃ³a báº£n ghi cÅ© Ä‘á»ƒ giá»¯ váº¹n toÃ n dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng / Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰.
 */
@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class GhnLocationSyncServiceImpl implements GhnLocationSyncService {

    GhnConfig ghnConfig;
    ProvinceRepository provinceRepository;
    DistrictRepository districtRepository;
    WardRepository wardRepository;
    CacheManager cacheManager;

    ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public String syncAllLocations() {
        log.info("Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ Master Data Location tá»« GHN...");
        RestClient restClient = RestClient.builder()
                .baseUrl(ghnConfig.getBaseUrl())
                .defaultHeader("Token", ghnConfig.getToken())
                .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_TYPE, MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)
                .build();

        int provinceCount = 0;
        int districtCount = 0;
        int wardCount = 0;

        try {
            // 1. Sync Provinces (GET /master-data/province)
            String provinceJson = restClient.get()
                    .uri("/master-data/province")
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            JsonNode provinceRoot = objectMapper.readTree(provinceJson);
            JsonNode provinceData = provinceRoot.get("data");

            List<Province> provincesToSave = new ArrayList<>();
            if (provinceData != null && provinceData.isArray()) {
                for (JsonNode pNode : provinceData) {
                    int pId = pNode.get("ProvinceID").asInt();
                    String pName = pNode.get("ProvinceName").asText();
                    provincesToSave.add(Province.builder()
                            .id(pId)
                            .name(pName)
                            .build());
                }
            }
            provinceRepository.saveAll(provincesToSave);
            provinceCount = provincesToSave.size();
            log.info("ÄÃ£ lÆ°u {} tá»‰nh/thÃ nh phá»‘.", provinceCount);

            // 2. Sync Districts & Wards cho tá»«ng Province
            for (Province province : provincesToSave) {
                // Rate limit pacing: 50ms giá»¯a cÃ¡c request Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh HTTP 429
                Thread.sleep(50);

                String districtJson;
                try {
                    districtJson = restClient.post()
                            .uri("/master-data/district")
                            .body(Map.of("province_id", province.getId()))
                            .retrieve()
                            .body(String.class);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.warn("Lá»—i khi táº£i quáº­n/huyá»‡n cho tá»‰nh ID={}: {}", province.getId(), e.getMessage());
                    continue;
                }

                JsonNode districtRoot = objectMapper.readTree(districtJson);
                JsonNode districtData = districtRoot.get("data");

                List<District> districtsToSave = new ArrayList<>();
                if (districtData != null && districtData.isArray()) {
                    for (JsonNode dNode : districtData) {
                        int dId = dNode.get("DistrictID").asInt();
                        String dName = dNode.get("DistrictName").asText();
                        districtsToSave.add(District.builder()
                                .id(dId)
                                .province(province)
                                .name(dName)
                                .build());
                    }
                }
                districtRepository.saveAll(districtsToSave);
                districtCount += districtsToSave.size();

                // 3. Sync Wards cho tá»«ng District
                for (District district : districtsToSave) {
                    Thread.sleep(50);

                    String wardJson;
                    try {
                        wardJson = restClient.post()
                                .uri("/master-data/ward")
                                .body(Map.of("district_id", district.getId()))
                                .retrieve()
                                .body(String.class);
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        log.warn("Lá»—i khi táº£i phÆ°á»ng/xÃ£ cho huyá»‡n ID={}: {}", district.getId(), e.getMessage());
                        continue;
                    }

                    JsonNode wardRoot = objectMapper.readTree(wardJson);
                    JsonNode wardData = wardRoot.get("data");

                    List<Ward> wardsToSave = new ArrayList<>();
                    if (wardData != null && wardData.isArray()) {
                        for (JsonNode wNode : wardData) {
                            String wCode = wNode.get("WardCode").asText();
                            String wName = wNode.get("WardName").asText();
                            wardsToSave.add(Ward.builder()
                                    .code(wCode)
                                    .district(district)
                                    .name(wName)
                                    .build());
                        }
                    }
                    wardRepository.saveAll(wardsToSave);
                    wardCount += wardsToSave.size();
                }
            }

            // XÃ³a cache redis Ä‘á»ƒ client nháº­n dá»¯ liá»‡u má»›i nháº¥t
            clearLocationCache();

            String report = String.format("Äá»“ng bá»™ hoÃ n táº¥t: %d Tá»‰nh/ThÃ nh, %d Quáº­n/Huyá»‡n, %d PhÆ°á»ng/XÃ£",
                    provinceCount, districtCount, wardCount);
            log.info(report);
            return report;
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.LOCATION_SYNC_FAILED, "QuÃ¡ trÃ¬nh Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ bá»‹ giÃ¡n Ä‘oáº¡n");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Lá»—i khi Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ location tá»« GHN: ", e);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.LOCATION_SYNC_FAILED, "Lá»—i Ä‘á»“ng bá»™: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void clearLocationCache() {
        if (cacheManager != null) {
            var pCache = cacheManager.getCache("locations:provinces");
            if (pCache != null) pCache.clear();
            var dCache = cacheManager.getCache("locations:districts");
            if (dCache != null) dCache.clear();
            var wCache = cacheManager.getCache("locations:wards");
            if (wCache != null) wCache.clear();
        }
    }
}





=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\location\service\impl\LocationServiceImpl.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.location.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.location.dto.response.DistrictResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.location.dto.response.ProvinceResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.location.dto.response.WardResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.DistrictRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.ProvinceRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.repository.WardRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.location.service.LocationService;
import org.springframework.cache.annotation.Cacheable;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class LocationServiceImpl implements LocationService {

    ProvinceRepository provinceRepository;
    DistrictRepository districtRepository;
    WardRepository wardRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(value = "locations:provinces")
    public List<ProvinceResponse> getProvinces() {
        return provinceRepository.findAllByOrderByNameAsc().stream()
                .map(p -> ProvinceResponse.builder()
                        .id(p.getId())
                        .name(p.getName())
                        .build())
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(value = "locations:districts", key = "#provinceId")
    public List<DistrictResponse> getDistrictsByProvince(Integer provinceId) {
        return districtRepository.findByProvinceIdOrderByNameAsc(provinceId).stream()
                .map(d -> DistrictResponse.builder()
                        .id(d.getId())
                        .provinceId(d.getProvince().getId())
                        .name(d.getName())
                        .build())
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    @Cacheable(value = "locations:wards", key = "#districtId")
    public List<WardResponse> getWardsByDistrict(Integer districtId) {
        return wardRepository.findByDistrictIdOrderByNameAsc(districtId).stream()
                .map(w -> WardResponse.builder()
                        .code(w.getCode())
                        .districtId(w.getDistrict().getId())
                        .name(w.getName())
                        .build())
                .toList();
    }
}

import jakarta.mail.internet.MimeMessage;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.experimental.NonFinal;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.notification.service.EmailService;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.MimeMessageHelper;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class EmailServiceImpl implements EmailService {

    JavaMailSender mailSender;

    @NonFinal
    @Value("${spring.mail.username}")
    String fromEmail;

    @Override
    public void sendOtpEmail(String toEmail, String otp) {
        try {
            MimeMessage message = mailSender.createMimeMessage();
            MimeMessageHelper helper = new MimeMessageHelper(message, false, "UTF-8");
            helper.setFrom(fromEmail, "SmashHub");
            helper.setTo(toEmail);
            helper.setSubject("[SmashHub] Ma xac thuc tai khoan cua ban");
            helper.setText(buildOtpEmailContent(otp), true);
            mailSender.send(message);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Send OTP email failed for {}", toEmail, e);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.MAIL_SEND_FAILED);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void sendPasswordResetOtpEmail(String toEmail, String otp) {
        try {
            MimeMessage message = mailSender.createMimeMessage();
            MimeMessageHelper helper = new MimeMessageHelper(message, false, "UTF-8");
            helper.setFrom(fromEmail, "SmashHub");
            helper.setTo(toEmail);
            helper.setSubject("[SmashHub] Ma dat lai mat khau cua ban");
            helper.setText(buildPasswordResetEmailContent(otp), true);
            mailSender.send(message);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Send password reset email failed for {}", toEmail, e);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.MAIL_SEND_FAILED);
        }
    }

                .message("Láº¥y thÃ´ng tin chi tiáº¿t Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<OrderResponse>> updateStatus(
            @PathVariable Long id,

package org.example.smashhub.order.controller;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.order.dto.request.CheckoutRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.order.dto.response.OrderResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.order.service.OrderService;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.data.web.PageableDefault;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/orders")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class OrderController {

    OrderService orderService;

    private String currentUsername() {
        Authentication auth = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        return auth != null ? auth.getName() : null;
    }

    private String getClientIp(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String xf = request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For");
        if (xf != null && !xf.isBlank()) {
            return xf.split(",")[0].trim();
        }
        return request.getRemoteAddr();
    }

    @PostMapping("/checkout")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<OrderResponse>> checkout(
            @RequestBody @Valid CheckoutRequest request,
            HttpServletRequest servletRequest) {
        OrderResponse result = orderService.checkout(currentUsername(), request, getClientIp(servletRequest));
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED).body(ApiResponse.<OrderResponse>builder()
                .result(result)
                .message("Äáº·t hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<Page<OrderResponse>>> getMyOrders(
            @PageableDefault(size = 10, sort = "createDate", direction = Sort.Direction.DESC) Pageable pageable) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<Page<OrderResponse>>builder()
                .result(orderService.getMyOrders(currentUsername(), pageable))
                .message("Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<OrderResponse>> getOrderById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<OrderResponse>builder()
                .result(orderService.getOrderById(currentUsername(), id))
                .message("Láº¥y chi tiáº¿t Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<OrderResponse>> cancelOrder(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @RequestParam(required = false, defaultValue = "KhÃ¡ch hÃ ng yÃªu cáº§u há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n") String reason) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<OrderResponse>builder()
                .result(orderService.cancelOrder(currentUsername(), id, reason))
                .message("Há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/repay")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<OrderResponse>> repay(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            HttpServletRequest servletRequest) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<OrderResponse>builder()
                .result(orderService.repay(currentUsername(), id, getClientIp(servletRequest)))
                .message("Táº¡o Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n thanh toÃ¡n má»›i thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.order.dto.request;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OrderStatus;

@Data
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\order\repository\OrderRepository.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.order.repository;

import jakarta.persistence.LockModeType;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OrderStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.Order;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.JpaRepository;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Lock;
import org.springframework.data.jpa.repository.Query;
import org.springframework.data.repository.query.Param;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@Repository
public interface OrderRepository extends JpaRepository<Order, Long> {

    Page<Order> findByUserIdOrderByCreateDateDesc(Long userId, Pageable pageable);

    @Query("""
            SELECT o FROM Order o
            JOIN FETCH o.user u
            JOIN FETCH o.shippingProvince p
            JOIN FETCH o.shippingDistrict d
            JOIN FETCH o.shippingWard w
            LEFT JOIN FETCH o.items i
            WHERE o.id = :id AND o.user.id = :userId
            """)
    Optional<Order> findByIdAndUserIdWithDetails(@Param("id") Long id, @Param("userId") Long userId);

    @Query("""
            SELECT o FROM Order o
            JOIN FETCH o.user u
            JOIN FETCH o.shippingProvince p
            JOIN FETCH o.shippingDistrict d
            JOIN FETCH o.shippingWard w
            LEFT JOIN FETCH o.items i
            WHERE o.id = :id
            """)
    Optional<Order> findByIdWithDetails(@Param("id") Long id);

    @Lock(LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)
    @Query("SELECT o FROM Order o WHERE o.id = :id")
    Optional<Order> findByIdForUpdate(@Param("id") Long id);

    List<Order> findByOrderStatusAndCreateDateBefore(OrderStatus status, LocalDateTime before);

    Page<Order> findAllByOrderByCreateDateDesc(Pageable pageable);

    Page<Order> findByOrderStatusOrderByCreateDateDesc(OrderStatus status, Pageable pageable);
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\order\scheduler\OrderExpirationScheduler.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.order.scheduler;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OrderStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.config.VnpayConfig;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.Order;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.order.service.OrderStateMachine;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.entity.PaymentTransaction;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.repository.PaymentTransactionRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.service.PaymentService;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.script.DefaultRedisScript;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * [Sá»¬A] Scheduler quÃ©t vÃ  xá»­ lÃ½ cÃ¡c Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng VNPay bá»‹ quÃ¡ háº¡n thanh toÃ¡n.
 * - Sá»­ dá»¥ng Redis Distributed Lock an toÃ n vá»›i Token UUID + Lua Script release Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng giáº£i nháº§m lock cá»§a instance khÃ¡c.
 * - KhÃ³a dÃ²ng Order báº±ng PESSIMISTIC_WRITE Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‘ng race condition vá»›i IPN Ä‘ang thanh toÃ¡n dá»Ÿ.
 * - Gá»i VNPay QueryDR kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t trÆ°á»›c khi há»§y, trÃ¡nh há»§y nháº§m Ä‘Æ¡n khÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ tiá»n mÃ  IPN bá»‹ trá»….
 * - Chuyá»ƒn sang EXPIRED vÃ  hoÃ n láº¡i tá»“n kho Ä‘Ãºng 1 láº§n duy nháº¥t (idempotent).
 */
@Component
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class OrderExpirationScheduler {

    private static final String LOCK_KEY = "lock:order:expiration";
    private static final String UNLOCK_LUA_SCRIPT =
            "if redis.call('get', KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then return redis.call('del', KEYS[1]) else return 0 end";

    OrderRepository orderRepository;
    PaymentTransactionRepository transactionRepository;
    PaymentService paymentService;
    OrderStateMachine orderStateMachine;
    StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;
    VnpayConfig vnpayConfig;

    @Scheduled(cron = "${app.order.expire-cron:0 * * * * ?}")
    public void scanAndExpireOrders() {
        // [Sá»¬A] DÃ¹ng Token ngáº«u nhiÃªn cho Redis Lock
        String lockToken = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        Boolean acquired = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(LOCK_KEY, lockToken, Duration.ofSeconds(55));
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(acquired)) {
            log.debug("Instance khÃ¡c Ä‘ang cháº¡y OrderExpirationScheduler, bá» qua lÆ°á»£t quÃ©t nÃ y.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            int expireMinutes = vnpayConfig.getExpireMinutes() != null ? vnpayConfig.getExpireMinutes() : 15;
            LocalDateTime cutoffTime = LocalDateTime.now().minusMinutes(expireMinutes);

            List<Order> expiredCandidates = orderRepository.findByOrderStatusAndCreateDateBefore(
                    OrderStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, cutoffTime);

            if (expiredCandidates.isEmpty()) {
                return;
            }

            log.info("TÃ¬m tháº¥y {} Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng PENDING_PAYMENT quÃ¡ háº¡n cáº§n xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t.", expiredCandidates.size());

            for (Order order : expiredCandidates) {
                try {
                    processExpiredOrder(order);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("Lá»—i khi xá»­ lÃ½ háº¿t háº¡n cho Order #{}: {}", order.getId(), e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            // [Sá»¬A] Giáº£i phÃ³ng lock báº±ng Lua Script chá»‰ khi Ä‘Ãºng Token chá»§ sá»Ÿ há»¯u
            try {
                DefaultRedisScript<Long> script = new DefaultRedisScript<>(UNLOCK_LUA_SCRIPT, Long.class);
                redisTemplate.execute(script, Collections.singletonList(LOCK_KEY), lockToken);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("Lá»—i khi giáº£i phÃ³ng Redis Lock háº¿t háº¡n Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng: {}", e.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    private void processExpiredOrder(Order order) {
        log.info("Äang kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t trÆ°á»›c khi há»§y Order #{}", order.getId());

        // [Sá»¬A] KhÃ³a dÃ²ng Order vÃ  kiá»ƒm tra láº¡i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i tÆ°Æ¡i nháº¥t tá»« DB
        // TrÃ¡nh race condition náº¿u luá»“ng IPN vá»«a má»›i chuyá»ƒn Ä‘Æ¡n sang PAID ngay trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³
        Optional<Order> lockedOrderOpt = orderRepository.findByIdForUpdate(order.getId());
        if (lockedOrderOpt.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Order lockedOrder = lockedOrderOpt.get();
        if (lockedOrder.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT) {
            log.info("Order #{} Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i sang {} (bá»Ÿi luá»“ng khÃ¡c), bá» qua viá»‡c há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n.",
                    lockedOrder.getId(), lockedOrder.getOrderStatus());
            return;
        }

        // Láº¥y giao dá»‹ch thanh toÃ¡n gáº§n nháº¥t
        Optional<PaymentTransaction> txOpt = transactionRepository.findTopByOrderIdOrderByCreateDateDesc(lockedOrder.getId());

        boolean isPaidOnVnpay = false;
        if (txOpt.isPresent()) {
            PaymentTransaction transaction = txOpt.get();
            // Truy váº¥n láº¡i vá»›i VNPay QueryDR
            isPaidOnVnpay = paymentService.queryTransactionStatus(transaction);
        }

        if (isPaidOnVnpay) {
            log.info("Order #{} Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng trÃªn VNPay (xÃ¡c nháº­n qua QueryDR).", lockedOrder.getId());
            return;
        }

        // ÄÆ¡n chÆ°a thanh toÃ¡n vÃ  Ä‘Ã£ quÃ¡ háº¡n -> Chuyá»ƒn sang EXPIRED (tá»± Ä‘á»™ng hoÃ n kho 1 láº§n qua State Machine)
        orderStateMachine.transition(lockedOrder, OrderStatus.EXPIRED, "SCHEDULER",
                "QuÃ¡ thá»i gian thanh toÃ¡n (" + vnpayConfig.getExpireMinutes() + " phÃºt)");

        if (txOpt.isPresent()) {
            PaymentTransaction tx = txOpt.get();
            if ("PENDING".equals(tx.getStatus())) {
                tx.setStatus("EXPIRED");
                transactionRepository.save(tx);
            }
        }

        log.info("ÄÃ£ Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u háº¿t háº¡n vÃ  hoÃ n kho cho Order #{}", lockedOrder.getId());
    }
}





=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\order\service\impl\OrderServiceImpl.java ===

import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Duration;
