import java.util.List;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class OrderServiceImpl implements OrderService {

    UserRepository userRepository;
    AddressRepository addressRepository;
    CartItemRepository cartItemRepository;
    OrderRepository orderRepository;
    OrderItemRepository orderItemRepository;
    OrderStatusHistoryRepository statusHistoryRepository;
    InventoryService inventoryService;
    ShippingService shippingService;
    PaymentService paymentService;
    OrderStateMachine orderStateMachine;
    OrderMapper orderMapper;
    StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderResponse checkout(String username, CheckoutRequest request, String ipAddress) {
        log.info("User {} báº¯t Ä‘áº§u quy trÃ¬nh Checkout Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng", username);

        // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ i khoáº£n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng
        User user = requireUser(username);

        // [Sá»¬A] KhÃ³a Redis theo User ID Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‘ng double-click hoáº·c 2 tab checkout Ä‘á»“ng thá»i táº¡o 2 Ä‘Æ¡n trÃ¹ng láº·p
        String checkoutLockKey = "lock:checkout:" + user.getId();
        if (redisTemplate != null) {
            Boolean lockAcquired = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(checkoutLockKey, "LOCKED", Duration.ofSeconds(5));
            if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(lockAcquired)) {
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_CANNOT_BE_CANCELLED,
                        "YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘áº·t hÃ ng Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c xá»­ lÃ½, vui lÃ²ng khÃ´ng nháº¥n liÃªn tá»¥c!");
            }
        }

        try {
            // 2. Láº¥y giá» hÃ ng trong DB
            List<CartItem> cartItems = cartItemRepository.findByUserIdWithDetails(user.getId());
            if (cartItems == null || cartItems.isEmpty()) {
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.CART_EMPTY);
            }

            // 3. Kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ giao hÃ ng thuá»™c vá» chÃ­nh ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng (Cháº·n IDOR)
            Address address = addressRepository.findByIdAndUserId(request.getAddressId(), user.getId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND));

            // 4. TÃ­nh láº¡i giÃ¡ tá»«ng máº·t hÃ ng tá»« DB báº±ng PriceCalculator vÃ  kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh kháº£ dá»¥ng
            BigDecimal subtotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
            for (CartItem ci : cartItems) {
                ProductVariant variant = ci.getVariant();
                Product product = variant.getColor().getProduct();

                if (variant.getActive() != VariantStatus.ACTIVE || product.getProductStatus() != ProductStatus.ACTIVE) {
                    throw new AppException(ErrorCode.PRODUCT_VARIANT_INACTIVE,
                            "Sáº£n pháº©m " + product.getName() + " hiá»‡n khÃ´ng thá»ƒ mua");
                }

                BigDecimal unitPrice = PriceCalculator.effectivePrice(product.getPrice(), product.getSalePrice());
                BigDecimal lineTotal = unitPrice.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(ci.getQuantity()));
                subtotal = subtotal.add(lineTotal);
            }

            // 5. Gá»i GHN Ä‘á»ƒ SERVER tá»± tÃ­nh phÃ­ váº­n chuyá»ƒn (Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng tin phÃ­ tá»« client)
            ShippingFeeRequest feeReq = ShippingFeeRequest.builder()
                    .addressId(address.getId())
                    .toDistrictId(address.getDistrict().getId())
                    .toWardCode(address.getWard().getCode())
                    .shippingMethod(request.getShippingMethod())
                    .build();

            ShippingFeeResponse feeRes = shippingService.calculateFee(username, feeReq);
            BigDecimal shippingCost = feeRes.getShippingFee();
            BigDecimal discount = BigDecimal.ZERO;
            BigDecimal total = subtotal.add(shippingCost).subtract(discount);

            // 6. Trá»« tá»“n kho nguyÃªn tá»­ báº±ng InventoryService.deductStock cho tá»«ng dÃ²ng
            // Náº¿u cÃ³ SKU nÃ o khÃ´ng Ä‘á»§ hÃ ng -> nÃ©m lá»—i vÃ  tá»± Ä‘á»™ng rollback toÃ n bá»™ transaction
            for (CartItem ci : cartItems) {
                inventoryService.deductStock(ci.getVariant().getId(), ci.getQuantity());
            }

            // 7. Táº¡o Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng Order
            OrderStatus initialStatus = (request.getPaymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.VNPAY)
                    ? OrderStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT
                    : OrderStatus.PROCESSING;

            Order order = Order.builder()
                    .user(user)
                    .orderStatus(initialStatus)
                    .shippingMethod(request.getShippingMethod())
                    .shippingRecipientName(address.getRecipientName())
                    .shippingPhone(address.getPhone() != null ? address.getPhone() : user.getPhone())
                    .shippingLine1(address.getLine1())
                    .shippingProvince(address.getProvince())
                    .shippingDistrict(address.getDistrict())
                    .shippingWard(address.getWard())
                    .subtotal(subtotal)
                    .shippingCost(shippingCost)
                    .discount(discount)
                    .total(total)
                    .paymentMethod(request.getPaymentMethod())
                    .note(request.getNote())
                    .stockRestored(false)
                    .build();

            Order savedOrder = orderRepository.save(order);

            // 8. Táº¡o OrderItem snapshot lÆ°u láº¡i dá»¯ liá»‡u lá»‹ch sá»­ giÃ¡ & sáº£n pháº©m
            List<OrderItem> orderItems = new ArrayList<>();
            for (CartItem ci : cartItems) {
                ProductVariant variant = ci.getVariant();
                Product product = variant.getColor().getProduct();
                BigDecimal unitPrice = PriceCalculator.effectivePrice(product.getPrice(), product.getSalePrice());
                BigDecimal totalPrice = unitPrice.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(ci.getQuantity()));

                OrderItem item = OrderItem.builder()
                        .order(savedOrder)
                        .variant(variant)
                        .productName(product.getName() + " - " + variant.getColor().getColorName() + " (" + variant.getSize() + ")")
                        .sku(variant.getSku())
                        .unitPrice(unitPrice)
                        .quantity(ci.getQuantity())
                        .totalPrice(totalPrice)
                        .build();
                orderItems.add(item);
            }
            orderItemRepository.saveAll(orderItems);
            savedOrder.setItems(orderItems);

            // 9. Ghi váº¿t audit lá»‹ch sá»­ táº¡o Ä‘Æ¡n
            statusHistoryRepository.save(OrderStatusHistory.builder()
                    .order(savedOrder)
                    .fromStatus(null)
                    .toStatus(initialStatus)
                    .updatedBy(username)
                    .note("ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c khá»Ÿi táº¡o qua Checkout")
                    .build());

            // 10. XÃ³a sáº¡ch giá» hÃ ng cá»§a user sau khi Ä‘áº·t Ä‘Æ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng
            cartItemRepository.deleteAllByUserId(user.getId());

            // 11. Xá»­ lÃ½ sau khá»Ÿi táº¡o theo phÆ°Æ¡ng thá»©c thanh toÃ¡n
            String paymentUrl = null;
            if (request.getPaymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.COD) {
                // [Sá»¬A] ÄÆ¡n COD: KÃ­ch hoáº¡t táº¡o váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN sau khi transaction DB Ä‘Ã£ commit hoÃ n táº¥t
                // TrÃ¡nh giá»¯ connection DB Hikari vÃ  khÃ´ng block database khi máº¡ng GHN cháº­m
                if (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
                    TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
                        @Override
                        public void afterCommit() {
                            try {
                                shippingService.createShipmentForOrder(savedOrder);
                            } catch (Exception e) {
                                log.error("Táº¡o váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN cho Ä‘Æ¡n COD #{} gáº·p sá»± cá»‘: {}", savedOrder.getId(), e.getMessage());
                            }
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    try {
                        shippingService.createShipmentForOrder(savedOrder);
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        log.error("Táº¡o váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN cho Ä‘Æ¡n COD #{} gáº·p sá»± cá»‘: {}", savedOrder.getId(), e.getMessage());
                    }
                }
            } else if (request.getPaymentMethod() == PaymentMethod.VNPAY) {
                // ÄÆ¡n VNPay: Táº¡o URL thanh toÃ¡n
                paymentUrl = paymentService.createPaymentUrl(savedOrder, ipAddress);
            }

            OrderResponse response = orderMapper.toOrderResponse(savedOrder);
            response.setItems(orderMapper.toOrderItemResponseList(orderItems));
            response.setPaymentUrl(paymentUrl);

            log.info("Äáº·t hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng: Order #{}", savedOrder.getId());
            return response;
        } finally {
            if (redisTemplate != null) {
                redisTemplate.delete(checkoutLockKey);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<OrderResponse> getMyOrders(String username, Pageable pageable) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        return orderRepository.findByUserIdOrderByCreateDateDesc(user.getId(), pageable)
                .map(this::mapOrderWithItems);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public OrderResponse getOrderById(String username, Long orderId) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        Order order = orderRepository.findByIdAndUserIdWithDetails(orderId, user.getId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND));

        return mapOrderWithItems(order);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderResponse cancelOrder(String username, Long orderId, String reason) {
        User user = requireUser(username);
        // [Sá»¬A] KhÃ³a dÃ²ng Order báº±ng findByIdForUpdate Ä‘á»ƒ tuáº§n tá»± hÃ³a an toÃ n tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i vá»›i IPN / Scheduler
        Order order = orderRepository.findByIdForUpdate(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND));

        if (!order.getUser().getId().equals(user.getId())) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_ACCESS_DENIED);
        }

        // KhÃ¡ch hÃ ng chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c tá»± há»§y khi Ä‘Æ¡n chÆ°a giao (PENDING_PAYMENT hoáº·c PROCESSING)
        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT && order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PROCESSING) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_CANNOT_BE_CANCELLED,
                    "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng khi Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c giao cho shipper hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ káº¿t thÃºc");
        }

        // Chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i sang CANCELLED (State machine sáº½ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng hoÃ n kho idempotent Ä‘Ãºng 1 láº§n)
        Order cancelledOrder = orderStateMachine.transition(order, OrderStatus.CANCELLED, username,
                reason != null ? reason : "NgÆ°á»i mua yÃªu cáº§u há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n");

        // Há»§y váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n trÃªn GHN náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ táº¡o
        shippingService.cancelShipment(cancelledOrder, reason);

        return mapOrderWithItems(cancelledOrder);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderResponse repay(String username, Long orderId, String ipAddress) {
        String paymentUrl = paymentService.repay(orderId, username, ipAddress);
        Order order = orderRepository.findByIdWithDetails(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND));

        OrderResponse res = mapOrderWithItems(order);
        res.setPaymentUrl(paymentUrl);
        return res;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<OrderResponse> getOrdersForAdmin(OrderStatus status, Pageable pageable) {
        Page<Order> page = (status != null)
                ? orderRepository.findByOrderStatusOrderByCreateDateDesc(status, pageable)
                : orderRepository.findAllByOrderByCreateDateDesc(pageable);

        return page.map(this::mapOrderWithItems);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public OrderResponse getOrderDetailForAdmin(Long orderId) {
        Order order = orderRepository.findByIdWithDetails(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND));
        return mapOrderWithItems(order);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderResponse updateOrderStatusAdmin(Long orderId, OrderStatusUpdateRequest request, String staffUsername) {
        Order order = orderRepository.findByIdWithDetails(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND));

        Order updated = orderStateMachine.transition(order, request.getStatus(), staffUsername, request.getNote());
        return mapOrderWithItems(updated);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public Shipment retryShipmentAdmin(Long orderId) {
        return shippingService.retryShipment(orderId);
    }

    // ---------------------- Helpers ----------------------

    private User requireUser(String username) {
        return userRepository.findByUsername(username)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.USER_NOT_EXISTED));
    }

    private OrderResponse mapOrderWithItems(Order order) {
        OrderResponse res = orderMapper.toOrderResponse(order);
        List<OrderItem> items = order.getItems();
        if (items == null || items.isEmpty()) {
            items = orderItemRepository.findByOrderId(order.getId());
        }
        res.setItems(orderMapper.toOrderItemResponseList(items));
        return res;
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\order\service\OrderStateMachine.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.order.service;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.catalog.service.InventoryService;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OrderStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.Order;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.OrderItem;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.OrderStatusHistory;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderItemRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderStatusHistoryRepository;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.EnumSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * [Má»šI] State machine quáº£n lÃ½ chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng táº­p trung má»™t nÆ¡i duy nháº¥t.
 * Äáº£m báº£o:
 * 1. Chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i há»£p lá»‡ theo luá»“ng nghiá»‡p vá»¥.
 * 2. HoÃ n kho idempotent Ä‘Ãºng 1 láº§n duy nháº¥t khi Ä‘Æ¡n bá»‹ CANCELLED / EXPIRED.
 * 3. Ghi váº¿t audit vÃ o báº£ng order_status_history.
 */
@Component
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class OrderStateMachine {

    OrderRepository orderRepository;
    OrderItemRepository orderItemRepository;
    OrderStatusHistoryRepository statusHistoryRepository;
    InventoryService inventoryService;

    // Báº£ng Ä‘á»‹nh nghÄ©a cÃ¡c chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i há»£p lá»‡
    private static final Map<OrderStatus, Set<OrderStatus>> VALID_TRANSITIONS = new EnumMap<>(OrderStatus.class);

    static {
        // ÄÆ¡n chá» thanh toÃ¡n VNPAY: cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng, hoáº·c bá»‹ há»§y/háº¿t háº¡n
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT,
                EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.PAID, OrderStatus.CANCELLED, OrderStatus.EXPIRED));

        // ÄÃ£ thanh toÃ¡n: chuyá»ƒn sang xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i, hoáº·c hoÃ n tiá»n náº¿u há»§y
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.PAID,
                EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.PROCESSING, OrderStatus.CANCELLED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED));

        // Äang xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i (COD hoáº·c sau khi PAID): giao cho shipper, hoáº·c há»§y
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
                EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.SHIPPING, OrderStatus.CANCELLED));

        // Äang giao hÃ ng: giao thÃ nh cÃ´ng, hoáº·c giao tháº¥t báº¡i/tráº£ hÃ ng -> há»§y
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.SHIPPING,
                EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.DELIVERED, OrderStatus.CANCELLED));

        // ÄÃ£ giao: cÃ³ thá»ƒ hoÃ n tiá»n náº¿u khÃ¡ch tráº£ hÃ ng
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.DELIVERED,
                EnumSet.of(OrderStatus.REFUNDED));

        // CÃ¡c tráº¡ng thÃ¡i káº¿t thÃºc (Terminal): khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c chuyá»ƒn tiáº¿p
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.CANCELLED, Collections.emptySet());
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.EXPIRED, Collections.emptySet());
        VALID_TRANSITIONS.put(OrderStatus.REFUNDED, Collections.emptySet());
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh há»£p lá»‡ cá»§a viá»‡c chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i mÃ  khÃ´ng thay Ä‘á»•i dá»¯ liá»‡u.
     */
    public boolean canTransition(OrderStatus from, OrderStatus to) {
        if (from == null || to == null) return false;
        if (from == to) return true; // Idempotent check
        Set<OrderStatus> allowed = VALID_TRANSITIONS.get(from);
        return allowed != null && allowed.contains(to);
    }

    /**
     * Thá»±c hiá»‡n chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng.
     *
     * @param order        ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng
     * @param targetStatus Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Ã­ch
     * @param updatedBy    NgÆ°á»i/Há»‡ thá»‘ng thá»±c hiá»‡n (VD: "username", "GHN_WEBHOOK", "VNPAY_IPN", "SCHEDULER")
     * @param note         Ghi chÃº lÃ½ do chuyá»ƒn
     * @return ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng sau khi Ä‘Ã£ chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i
     */
    @Transactional
    public Order transition(Order order, OrderStatus targetStatus, String updatedBy, String note) {
        // [Sá»¬A] KhÃ³a dÃ²ng Order báº±ng PESSIMISTIC_WRITE (SELECT FOR UPDATE) Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ hÃ³a tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i
        // chá»‘ng race condition giá»¯a IPN, Scheduler háº¿t háº¡n, Webhook GHN vÃ  User há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n.
        Order lockedOrder = (order.getId() != null)
                ? orderRepository.findByIdForUpdate(order.getId()).orElse(order)
                : order;

        OrderStatus currentStatus = lockedOrder.getOrderStatus();

        // 1. Náº¿u tráº¡ng thÃ¡i trÃ¹ng nhau -> Idempotent, khÃ´ng lÃ m gÃ¬ thÃªm
        if (currentStatus == targetStatus) {
            log.info("Order #{} Ä‘Ã£ á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i {}, bá» qua chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i láº·p.", lockedOrder.getId(), targetStatus);
            return lockedOrder;
        }

        // 2. Kiá»ƒm tra chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i há»£p lá»‡
        if (!canTransition(currentStatus, targetStatus)) {
            log.warn("Chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ cho Order #{}: {} -> {}", lockedOrder.getId(), currentStatus, targetStatus);
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_STATUS_INVALID_TRANSITION,
                    String.format("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng tá»« %s sang %s", currentStatus, targetStatus));
        }

        log.info("Order #{} chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i: {} -> {} bá»Ÿi {}", lockedOrder.getId(), currentStatus, targetStatus, updatedBy);

        // 3. HoÃ n kho idempotent Ä‘Ãºng 1 láº§n khi Ä‘Æ¡n bá»‹ há»§y (CANCELLED) hoáº·c háº¿t háº¡n (EXPIRED)
        if (targetStatus == OrderStatus.CANCELLED || targetStatus == OrderStatus.EXPIRED) {
            restoreStockIdempotent(lockedOrder);
        }

        // 4. Cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Order
        lockedOrder.setOrderStatus(targetStatus);
        Order saved = orderRepository.save(lockedOrder);

        // 5. Ghi nháº­t kÃ½ audit
        statusHistoryRepository.save(OrderStatusHistory.builder()
                .order(saved)
                .fromStatus(currentStatus)
                .toStatus(targetStatus)
                .updatedBy(updatedBy)
                .note(note)
                .build());

        return saved;
    }

    /**
     * [Sá»¬A] HoÃ n kho an toÃ n: kiá»ƒm tra cá» stockRestored Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng bao giá» hoÃ n kho 2 láº§n cho cÃ¹ng 1 Ä‘Æ¡n.
     * TUYá»†T Äá»I KHÃ”NG nuá»‘t exception. Náº¿u cÃ³ lá»—i hoÃ n kho thÃ¬ pháº£i throw Ä‘á»ƒ rollback toÃ n bá»™ transaction.
     */
    private void restoreStockIdempotent(Order order) {
        if (order.isStockRestored()) {
            log.info("Order #{} Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c hoÃ n kho trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³, bá» qua hoÃ n kho láº·p.", order.getId());
            return;
        }

        List<OrderItem> items = order.getItems();
        if (items == null || items.isEmpty()) {
            items = orderItemRepository.findByOrderId(order.getId());
        }

        if (items != null) {
            for (OrderItem item : items) {
                // KhÃ´ng bá»c try-catch nuá»‘t lá»—i: náº¿u lá»—i DB thÃ¬ rollback Ä‘á»ƒ báº£o toÃ n tÃ­nh toÃ n váº¹n
                inventoryService.restoreStock(item.getVariant().getId(), item.getQuantity());
                log.info("HoÃ n tá»“n kho Order #{}: Variant ID={}, Qty={}",
                        order.getId(), item.getVariant().getId(), item.getQuantity());
            }
        }
        order.setStockRestored(true);
    }
}





=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\payment\controller\PaymentController.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.payment.controller;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.dto.VnpayIpnResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.dto.VnpayReturnResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.service.PaymentService;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/payment/vnpay")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class PaymentController {

    PaymentService paymentService;

    /**
     * IPN URL nháº­n callback ngáº§m tá»« VNPay (Server-to-Server).
     * Báº¯t buá»™c luÃ´n tráº£ vá» HTTP 200 kÃ¨m JSON chuáº©n Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng: {"RspCode":"00","Message":"Confirm Success"}.
     */
    @GetMapping("/ipn")
    public ResponseEntity<VnpayIpnResponse> handleIpnGet(@RequestParam Map<String, String> params) {
        VnpayIpnResponse response = paymentService.processIpn(params);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    @PostMapping("/ipn")
    public ResponseEntity<VnpayIpnResponse> handleIpnPost(@RequestParam Map<String, String> params) {
        VnpayIpnResponse response = paymentService.processIpn(params);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    /**
     * Return URL sau khi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng thá»±c hiá»‡n thanh toÃ¡n xong trÃªn cá»•ng VNPay.
     * TUYá»†T Äá»I khÃ´ng cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y, chá»‰ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ káº¿t quáº£ cho khÃ¡ch.
     */
    @GetMapping("/return")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<VnpayReturnResponse>> handleReturn(
            @RequestParam Map<String, String> params) {
        VnpayReturnResponse response = paymentService.processReturn(params);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<VnpayReturnResponse>builder()
                .result(response)
                .message("Káº¿t quáº£ thanh toÃ¡n tá»« VNPay")
                .build());
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.payment.dto;

import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.Data;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;

/**
 * [Má»šI] Äá»‹nh dáº¡ng JSON tráº£ vá» cho VNPay khi nháº­n IPN callback.
 * Báº¯t buá»™c Ä‘Ãºng 2 trÆ°á»ng: RspCode vÃ  Message.
 */
@Data
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
public class VnpayIpnResponse {

    private String RspCode;
    private String Message;

    public static VnpayIpnResponse success() {
        return new VnpayIpnResponse("00", "Confirm Success");
    }

    public static VnpayIpnResponse orderNotFound() {
        return new VnpayIpnResponse("01", "Order not Found");
    }

    public static VnpayIpnResponse orderAlreadyConfirmed() {
        return new VnpayIpnResponse("02", "Order already confirmed");
    }

    public static VnpayIpnResponse invalidAmount() {
        return new VnpayIpnResponse("04", "Invalid Amount");
    }

    public static VnpayIpnResponse invalidSignature() {
        return new VnpayIpnResponse("97", "Invalid Checksum");
    }

    public static VnpayIpnResponse systemError() {
        return new VnpayIpnResponse("99", "Unknown error");
    }
}

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.EnumType;
import jakarta.persistence.Enumerated;
import jakarta.persistence.FetchType;
import jakarta.persistence.JoinColumn;
import jakarta.persistence.Lob;
import jakarta.persistence.ManyToOne;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;
import lombok.ToString;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.IpnStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.persistence.BaseEntity;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.Order;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@ToString(callSuper = true, exclude = {"order"})
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
@Entity
@Table(name = "payment_transaction")
public class PaymentTransaction extends BaseEntity {

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "order_id", nullable = false)
    Order order;
    @Column(name = "provider", nullable = false)
    private String provider;

    @Column(name = "txn_ref", nullable = false, unique = true)
    private String txnRef;

    @Column(name = "amount", nullable = false, precision = 15, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal amount;

    @Column(name = "status", nullable = false)
    private String status;

    @Column(name = "response_code", length = 10)
    private String responseCode;

    @Column(name = "transaction_status", length = 10)
    private String transactionStatus;

    @Column(name = "transaction_no", length = 20)
    private String transactionNo;

    @Column(name = "bank_code", length = 20)
    private String bankCode;

    @Column(name = "pay_date")
    private LocalDateTime payDate;

    @Column(name = "expire_date")
    private LocalDateTime expireDate;

    @Column(name = "ip_address", length = 50)
    private String ipAddress;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "ipn_processed", nullable = false)
    private IpnStatus ipnProcessed;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.LONGVARCHAR)
    @Column(name = "raw_request_payload", columnDefinition = "LONGTEXT")
    private String rawRequestPayload;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.LONGVARCHAR)
    @Column(name = "raw_response_payload", columnDefinition = "LONGTEXT")
    private String rawResponsePayload;

    @Column(name = "failure_reason")
    private String failureReason;
}

@Repository
public interface PaymentTransactionRepository extends JpaRepository<PaymentTransaction, Long> {

    Optional<PaymentTransaction> findByTxnRef(String txnRef);

    @Lock(LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)
    @Query("SELECT p FROM PaymentTransaction p JOIN FETCH p.order o WHERE p.txnRef = :txnRef")
    Optional<PaymentTransaction> findByTxnRefForUpdate(@Param("txnRef") String txnRef);

package org.example.smashhub.payment.service.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.IpnStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.common.enums.OrderStatus;
import org.example.smashhub.config.VnpayConfig;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.Order;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.OrderStatusHistory;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderItemRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.order.repository.OrderStatusHistoryRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.order.service.OrderStateMachine;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.dto.VnpayIpnResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.dto.VnpayReturnResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.entity.PaymentTransaction;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.repository.PaymentTransactionRepository;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.service.PaymentService;
import org.example.smashhub.payment.util.VnpayUtils;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.service.ShippingService;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import tools.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

@Service
@Slf4j
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class PaymentServiceImpl implements PaymentService {

    static final ZoneId VN_ZONE = ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh");
    static final DateTimeFormatter VNP_DATE_FORMATTER = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMddHHmmss");

    VnpayConfig vnpayConfig;
    PaymentTransactionRepository transactionRepository;
    OrderRepository orderRepository;
    OrderStateMachine orderStateMachine;
    ShippingService shippingService;
    OrderStatusHistoryRepository statusHistoryRepository;

    ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public String createPaymentUrl(Order order, String ipAddress) {
        log.info("Khá»Ÿi táº¡o thanh toÃ¡n VNPay cho Order #{}", order.getId());

        // Táº¡o mÃ£ giao dá»‹ch duy nháº¥t cho má»—i láº§n thanh toÃ¡n
        String txnRef = order.getId() + "_" + System.currentTimeMillis();

        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now(VN_ZONE);
        LocalDateTime expireTime = now.plusMinutes(
                vnpayConfig.getExpireMinutes() != null ? vnpayConfig.getExpireMinutes() : 15);

        String vnpCreateDate = now.format(VNP_DATE_FORMATTER);
        String vnpExpireDate = expireTime.format(VNP_DATE_FORMATTER);

        // VNPay yÃªu cáº§u sá»‘ tiá»n nhÃ¢n 100, kiá»ƒu sá»‘ nguyÃªn khÃ´ng cÃ³ pháº§n tháº­p phÃ¢n
        BigDecimal amountInVnd = order.getTotal().setScale(0, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        String vnpAmount = amountInVnd.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).toPlainString();

        String clientIp = (ipAddress != null && !ipAddress.isBlank()) ? ipAddress : "127.0.0.1";
        if (clientIp.contains(",")) {
            clientIp = clientIp.split(",")[0].trim();
        }

        String orderInfo = "Thanh toan don hang #" + order.getId();
        orderInfo = VnpayUtils.removeAccents(orderInfo);

        Map<String, String> vnpParams = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        vnpParams.put("vnp_Version", "2.1.0");
        vnpParams.put("vnp_Command", "pay");
        vnpParams.put("vnp_TmnCode", vnpayConfig.getTmnCode());
        vnpParams.put("vnp_Amount", vnpAmount);
        vnpParams.put("vnp_CurrCode", "VND");
        vnpParams.put("vnp_TxnRef", txnRef);
        vnpParams.put("vnp_OrderInfo", orderInfo);
        vnpParams.put("vnp_OrderType", "other");
        vnpParams.put("vnp_Locale", "vn");
        vnpParams.put("vnp_ReturnUrl", vnpayConfig.getReturnUrl());
        vnpParams.put("vnp_IpAddr", clientIp);
        vnpParams.put("vnp_CreateDate", vnpCreateDate);
        vnpParams.put("vnp_ExpireDate", vnpExpireDate);

        // KÃ½ dá»¯ liá»‡u
        String hashData = VnpayUtils.buildHashData(vnpParams);
        String secureHash = VnpayUtils.hmacSHA512(vnpayConfig.getHashSecret(), hashData);

        String paymentUrl = vnpayConfig.getPayUrl() + "?" + hashData + "&vnp_SecureHash=" + secureHash;

        // LÆ°u báº£n ghi PaymentTransaction á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i PENDING
        PaymentTransaction transaction = PaymentTransaction.builder()
                .order(order)
                .provider("VNPAY")
                .txnRef(txnRef)
                .amount(order.getTotal())
                .status("PENDING")
                .ipnProcessed(IpnStatus.PENDING)
                .expireDate(expireTime)
                .ipAddress(clientIp)
                .rawRequestPayload(paymentUrl)
                .build();

        transactionRepository.save(transaction);
        log.info("Táº¡o payment URL thÃ nh cÃ´ng cho Order #{}, txnRef={}", order.getId(), txnRef);

        return paymentUrl;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public VnpayIpnResponse processIpn(Map<String, String> params) {
        log.info("Nháº­n callback VNPay IPN: {}", params);

        try {
            // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra chá»¯ kÃ½ báº£o máº­t (constant-time)
            String secureHash = params.get("vnp_SecureHash");
            String hashData = VnpayUtils.buildHashData(params);

            if (!VnpayUtils.verifySignature(hashData, secureHash, vnpayConfig.getHashSecret())) {
                log.warn("VNPay IPN: Sai chá»¯ kÃ½ báº£o máº­t!");
                return VnpayIpnResponse.invalidSignature(); // RspCode 97
            }

            // 2. TÃ¬m báº£n ghi giao dá»‹ch báº±ng txnRef vÃ  khÃ³a dÃ²ng (Pessimistic Write Lock)
            String txnRef = params.get("vnp_TxnRef");
            if (txnRef == null || txnRef.isBlank()) {
                return VnpayIpnResponse.orderNotFound(); // RspCode 01
            }

            Optional<PaymentTransaction> txOpt = transactionRepository.findByTxnRefForUpdate(txnRef);
            if (txOpt.isEmpty()) {
                log.warn("VNPay IPN: KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y giao dá»‹ch vá»›i txnRef={}", txnRef);
                return VnpayIpnResponse.orderNotFound(); // RspCode 01
            }

            PaymentTransaction transaction = txOpt.get();
            Order order = transaction.getOrder();

            // 3. Äá»‘i chiáº¿u sá»‘ tiá»n thanh toÃ¡n
            String amountStr = params.get("vnp_Amount");
            if (amountStr == null) {
                return VnpayIpnResponse.invalidAmount(); // RspCode 04
            }
            BigDecimal paidAmount = new BigDecimal(amountStr).divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
            if (paidAmount.compareTo(transaction.getAmount().setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)) != 0) {
                log.warn("VNPay IPN: Sai sá»‘ tiá»n! ÄÃ£ nháº­n: {}, Ká»³ vá»ng: {}", paidAmount, transaction.getAmount());
                return VnpayIpnResponse.invalidAmount(); // RspCode 04
            }

            // 4. Kiá»ƒm tra Idempotency: náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ xá»­ lÃ½ rá»“i thÃ¬ khÃ´ng xá»­ lÃ½ láº¡i
            if (transaction.getIpnProcessed() == IpnStatus.PROCESSED) {
                log.info("VNPay IPN: Giao dá»‹ch txnRef={} Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xá»­ lÃ½ trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³ (Idempotent).", txnRef);
                return VnpayIpnResponse.orderAlreadyConfirmed(); // RspCode 02
            }

            // 5. Cáº­p nháº­t káº¿t quáº£ giao dá»‹ch
            String responseCode = params.get("vnp_ResponseCode");
            String transactionStatus = params.get("vnp_TransactionStatus");
            String transactionNo = params.get("vnp_TransactionNo");
            String bankCode = params.get("vnp_BankCode");
            String payDateStr = params.get("vnp_PayDate");

            transaction.setResponseCode(responseCode);
            transaction.setTransactionStatus(transactionStatus);
            transaction.setTransactionNo(transactionNo);
            transaction.setBankCode(bankCode);
            try {
                transaction.setRawResponsePayload(objectMapper.writeValueAsString(params));
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}

            if (payDateStr != null && payDateStr.length() == 14) {
                try {
                    transaction.setPayDate(LocalDateTime.parse(payDateStr, VNP_DATE_FORMATTER));
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }

            // ThÃ nh cÃ´ng khi ResponseCode = 00 VÃ€ TransactionStatus = 00
            boolean isSuccess = "00".equals(responseCode) && "00".equals(transactionStatus);

            if (isSuccess) {
                // [Sá»¬A] 1. Kiá»ƒm tra ká»‹ch báº£n Late IPN: KhÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ tiá»n nhÆ°ng Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘Ã£ EXPIRED hoáº·c CANCELLED
                // TUYá»†T Äá»I KHÃ”NG nÃ©m lá»—i lÃ m rollback giao dá»‹ch khiáº¿n tiá»n bá»‹ máº¥t dáº¥u.
                // Pháº£i ghi nháº­n giao dá»‹ch thÃ nh cÃ´ng vÃ  Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u cáº§n Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t hoÃ n tiá»n!
                if (order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.EXPIRED || order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.CANCELLED) {
                    log.warn("Cáº¢NH BÃO: VNPay IPN bÃ¡o thÃ nh cÃ´ng nhÆ°ng Order #{} Ä‘Ã£ á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i {}! Cáº§n hoÃ n tiá»n cho khÃ¡ch.",
                            order.getId(), order.getOrderStatus());
                    transaction.setStatus("SUCCESS_LATE");
                    transaction.setFailureReason("PAID_AFTER_" + order.getOrderStatus() + "_NEEDS_REFUND");
                    transaction.setIpnProcessed(IpnStatus.PROCESSED);
                    transactionRepository.save(transaction);

                    statusHistoryRepository.save(OrderStatusHistory.builder()
                            .order(order)
                            .fromStatus(order.getOrderStatus())
                            .toStatus(order.getOrderStatus())
                            .updatedBy("VNPAY_IPN_LATE")
                            .note(String.format("KhÃ¡ch thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng sá»‘ tiá»n %s qua VNPay (MÃ£ GD: %s) nhÆ°ng Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘Ã£ %s. Cáº§n Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t hoÃ n tiá»n!",
                                    paidAmount, transactionNo, order.getOrderStatus()))
                            .build());

                    return VnpayIpnResponse.success(); // Tráº£ RspCode 00 Ä‘á»ƒ VNPay khÃ´ng gá»i láº¡i liÃªn tá»¥c
                }

                // [Sá»¬A] 2. Kiá»ƒm tra Double Payment: Náº¿u Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ 1 giao dá»‹ch khÃ¡c thÃ nh cÃ´ng trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³
                List<PaymentTransaction> existingSuccess = transactionRepository.findByOrderIdAndStatus(order.getId(), "SUCCESS");
                if (!existingSuccess.isEmpty()) {
                    log.warn("Cáº¢NH BÃO: ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng #{} Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ giao dá»‹ch {} thÃ nh cÃ´ng trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³! Giao dá»‹ch txnRef={} lÃ  thanh toÃ¡n trÃ¹ng, cáº§n hoÃ n tiá»n.",
                            order.getId(), existingSuccess.get(0).getTxnRef(), txnRef);
                    transaction.setStatus("DUPLICATE_PAYMENT");
                    transaction.setFailureReason("ORDER_ALREADY_PAID_NEEDS_REFUND");
                    transaction.setIpnProcessed(IpnStatus.PROCESSED);
                    transactionRepository.save(transaction);
                    return VnpayIpnResponse.orderAlreadyConfirmed(); // RspCode 02
                }

                transaction.setStatus("SUCCESS");
                transaction.setIpnProcessed(IpnStatus.PROCESSED);
                transactionRepository.save(transaction);

                // Chuyá»ƒn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Order sang PAID
                Order paidOrder = orderStateMachine.transition(order, OrderStatus.PAID, "VNPAY_IPN", "Thanh toÃ¡n VNPay thÃ nh cÃ´ng");

                // [Sá»¬A] 3. TÃ¡ch gá»i API GHN ra ngoÃ i Database Transaction báº±ng afterCommit
                // TrÃ¡nh giá»¯ connection DB, trÃ¡nh lock dÃ²ng orders kÃ©o dÃ i vÃ  Ä‘áº£m báº£o pháº£n há»“i IPN tá»©c thÃ¬ (<50ms)
                if (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
                    TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
                        @Override
                        public void afterCommit() {
                            try {
                                shippingService.createShipmentForOrder(paidOrder);
                            } catch (Exception e) {
                                log.error("Táº¡o váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN sau khi thanh toÃ¡n VNPay lá»—i (Order #{}): {}", paidOrder.getId(), e.getMessage());
                            }
                        }
                    });
                } else {
                    try {
                        shippingService.createShipmentForOrder(paidOrder);
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        log.error("Táº¡o váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN sau khi thanh toÃ¡n VNPay lá»—i (Order #{}): {}", paidOrder.getId(), e.getMessage());
                    }
                }

                log.info("VNPay IPN thÃ nh cÃ´ng cho Order #{}, txnRef={}", order.getId(), txnRef);
            } else {
                transaction.setStatus("FAILED");
                transaction.setFailureReason("VNPay response code: " + responseCode + ", transaction status: " + transactionStatus);
                transaction.setIpnProcessed(IpnStatus.PROCESSED);
                transactionRepository.save(transaction);
                log.warn("VNPay IPN tháº¥t báº¡i cho Order #{}, mÃ£ lá»—i: {}", order.getId(), responseCode);
            }

            return VnpayIpnResponse.success(); // RspCode 00

        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Lá»—i ngoáº¡i lá»‡ khi xá»­ lÃ½ VNPay IPN: ", e);
            return VnpayIpnResponse.systemError(); // RspCode 99
        }
    }

    @Override
    public VnpayReturnResponse processReturn(Map<String, String> params) {
        log.info("Nháº­n callback VNPay Return URL: {}", params);

        String secureHash = params.get("vnp_SecureHash");
        String hashData = VnpayUtils.buildHashData(params);

        if (!VnpayUtils.verifySignature(hashData, secureHash, vnpayConfig.getHashSecret())) {
            log.warn("VNPay Return: Chá»¯ kÃ½ khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡!");
            return VnpayReturnResponse.builder()
                    .status("FAILED")
                    .message("Chá»¯ kÃ½ pháº£n há»“i khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡")
                    .build();
        }

        String txnRef = params.get("vnp_TxnRef");
        String responseCode = params.get("vnp_ResponseCode");
        String amountStr = params.get("vnp_Amount");
        BigDecimal amount = (amountStr != null)
                ? new BigDecimal(amountStr).divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
                : BigDecimal.ZERO;

        Long orderId = null;
        if (txnRef != null && txnRef.contains("_")) {
            try {
                orderId = Long.parseLong(txnRef.split("_")[0]);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }

        boolean success = "00".equals(responseCode);
        return VnpayReturnResponse.builder()
                .orderId(orderId)
                .txnRef(txnRef)
                .amount(amount)
                .responseCode(responseCode)
                .transactionNo(params.get("vnp_TransactionNo"))
                .status(success ? "SUCCESS" : "FAILED")
                .message(success ? "Giao dá»‹ch thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng" : "Giao dá»‹ch khÃ´ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y")
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public boolean queryTransactionStatus(PaymentTransaction transaction) {
        if (vnpayConfig.getApiUrl() == null || vnpayConfig.getApiUrl().isBlank()) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            String requestId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
            String version = "2.1.0";
            String command = "querydr";
            String tmnCode = vnpayConfig.getTmnCode();
            String txnRef = transaction.getTxnRef();
            String orderInfo = "Truy van giao dich " + txnRef;
            String transDate = transaction.getCreateDate().format(VNP_DATE_FORMATTER);
            String createDate = LocalDateTime.now(VN_ZONE).format(VNP_DATE_FORMATTER);
            String ipAddr = "127.0.0.1";

            // Chuá»—i dá»¯ liá»‡u kÃ½ cho querydr theo tÃ i liá»‡u VNPay:
            // requestId|version|command|tmnCode|txnRef|transDate|createDate|ipAddr|orderInfo
            String rawHash = String.join("|", requestId, version, command, tmnCode, txnRef, transDate, createDate, ipAddr, orderInfo);
            String secureHash = VnpayUtils.hmacSHA512(vnpayConfig.getHashSecret(), rawHash);

            Map<String, Object> reqBody = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            reqBody.put("vnp_RequestId", requestId);
            reqBody.put("vnp_Version", version);
            reqBody.put("vnp_Command", command);
            reqBody.put("vnp_TmnCode", tmnCode);
            reqBody.put("vnp_TxnRef", txnRef);
            reqBody.put("vnp_OrderInfo", orderInfo);
            reqBody.put("vnp_TransactionDate", transDate);
            reqBody.put("vnp_CreateDate", createDate);
            reqBody.put("vnp_IpAddr", ipAddr);
            reqBody.put("vnp_SecureHash", secureHash);

            RestClient client = RestClient.builder()
                    .baseUrl(vnpayConfig.getApiUrl())
                    .build();

            String response = client.post()
                    .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                    .body(reqBody)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
            String responseCode = root.has("vnp_ResponseCode") ? root.get("vnp_ResponseCode").asText() : "";
            String transactionStatus = root.has("vnp_TransactionStatus") ? root.get("vnp_TransactionStatus").asText() : "";

            if ("00".equals(responseCode) && "00".equals(transactionStatus)) {
                log.info("VNPay querydr xÃ¡c nháº­n giao dá»‹ch {} Ä‘Ã£ thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng!", txnRef);
                Order order = transaction.getOrder();
                if (order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.EXPIRED || order.getOrderStatus() == OrderStatus.CANCELLED) {
                    transaction.setStatus("SUCCESS_LATE");
                    transaction.setFailureReason("PAID_AFTER_" + order.getOrderStatus() + "_NEEDS_REFUND");
                    transaction.setIpnProcessed(IpnStatus.PROCESSED);
                    transactionRepository.save(transaction);
                    return true;
                }

                transaction.setStatus("SUCCESS");
                transaction.setIpnProcessed(IpnStatus.PROCESSED);
                if (root.has("vnp_TransactionNo")) {
                    transaction.setTransactionNo(root.get("vnp_TransactionNo").asText());
                }
                transactionRepository.save(transaction);
                Order paidOrder = orderStateMachine.transition(order, OrderStatus.PAID, "VNPAY_QUERYDR", "Thanh toÃ¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng qua QueryDR");
                shippingService.createShipmentForOrder(paidOrder);
                return true;
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("Lá»—i khi gá»i VNPay querydr cho giao dá»‹ch {}: {}", transaction.getTxnRef(), e.getMessage());
        }
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public String repay(Long orderId, String username, String ipAddress) {
        Order order = orderRepository.findById(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND));

        if (!order.getUser().getUsername().equals(username)) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_ACCESS_DENIED);
        }

        if (order.getOrderStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT) {
            throw new AppException(ErrorCode.ORDER_ALREADY_PAID, "ÄÆ¡n hÃ ng khÃ´ng á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i chá» thanh toÃ¡n");
        }

        return createPaymentUrl(order, ipAddress);
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\thanh\Desktop\doAn\SmashHub\src\main\java\org\example\smashhub\payment\util\VnpayUtils.java ===
package org.example.smashhub.payment.util;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;

import javax.crypto.Mac;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * [Má»šI] Tiá»‡n Ã­ch mÃ£ hÃ³a, kÃ½ HMAC-SHA512 vÃ  chuáº©n hÃ³a dá»¯ liá»‡u cho cá»•ng thanh toÃ¡n VNPay.
 */
@Slf4j
public final class VnpayUtils {

    private VnpayUtils() {}

    /**
     * KÃ½ HMAC-SHA512 theo Ä‘Ãºng quy chuáº©n ká»¹ thuáº­t cá»§a VNPay.
     *
     * @param key  Chuá»—i bÃ­ máº­t (hash-secret)
     * @param data Dá»¯ liá»‡u cáº§n kÃ½
     * @return Chuá»—i Hex chá»¯ hoa / thÆ°á»ng (VNPay cháº¥p nháº­n cáº£ hai, chuáº©n hÃ³a tráº£ vá» chá»¯ thÆ°á»ng)
     */
    public static String hmacSHA512(String key, String data) {
        try {
            if (key == null || data == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Key vÃ  Data khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c null");
            }
            Mac hmac512 = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA512");
            SecretKeySpec secretKey = new SecretKeySpec(key.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), "HmacSHA512");
            hmac512.init(secretKey);
            byte[] result = hmac512.doFinal(data.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(2 * result.length);
            for (byte b : result) {
                sb.append(String.format("%02x", b & 0xff));
            }
            return sb.toString();
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            log.error("Lá»—i táº¡o HMAC-SHA512: ", ex);
            return "";
        }
    }

    /**
     * So sÃ¡nh chá»¯ kÃ½ an toÃ n chá»‘ng táº¥n cÃ´ng timing (constant-time comparison).
     */
    public static boolean verifySignature(String rawData, String secureHash, String secretKey) {
        if (secureHash == null || secretKey == null) {
            return false;
        }
        String calculated = hmacSHA512(secretKey, rawData);
        return MessageDigest.isEqual(
                calculated.toLowerCase().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
                secureHash.toLowerCase().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
        );
    }

    /**
     * XÃ¢y dá»±ng chuá»—i dá»¯ liá»‡u kÃ½ vÃ  chuá»—i query string tá»« map tham sá»‘.
     * CÃ¡c tham sá»‘ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sáº¯p xáº¿p theo báº£ng chá»¯ cÃ¡i tÄƒng dáº§n (A-Z).
     *
     * @param params Map tham sá»‘ vnp_*
     * @return Chuá»—i query string Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c URL-encode
     */
    public static String buildHashData(Map<String, String> params) {
        List<String> fieldNames = new ArrayList<>(params.keySet());
        Collections.sort(fieldNames);

        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        Iterator<String> itr = fieldNames.iterator();
        while (itr.hasNext()) {
            String fieldName = itr.next();
            String fieldValue = params.get(fieldName);
            if (fieldValue != null && !fieldValue.isBlank()
                    && !"vnp_SecureHash".equals(fieldName)
                    && !"vnp_SecureHashType".equals(fieldName)) {
                try {
                    sb.append(URLEncoder.encode(fieldName, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII.toString()));
                    sb.append('=');
                    sb.append(URLEncoder.encode(fieldValue, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII.toString()));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("URL encode error: ", e);
                }
                if (itr.hasNext()) {
                    sb.append('&');
                }
            }
        }
        // XÃ³a dáº¥u & thá»«a á»Ÿ cuá»‘i náº¿u cÃ³
        String result = sb.toString();
        if (result.endsWith("&")) {
            result = result.substring(0, result.length() - 1);
        }
        return result;
    }

    /**
     * Loáº¡i bá» dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t vÃ  kÃ½ tá»± Ä‘áº·c biá»‡t theo yÃªu cáº§u cá»§a vnp_OrderInfo.
     */
    public static String removeAccents(String text) {
        if (text == null) return "";
        String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(text, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile("\\p{InCombiningDiacriticalMarks}+");
        String noAccents = pattern.matcher(normalized).replaceAll("").replace('Ä‘', 'd').replace('Ä', 'D');
        // Loáº¡i bá» kÃ½ tá»± Ä‘áº·c biá»‡t, chá»‰ giá»¯ chá»¯, sá»‘ vÃ  khoáº£ng tráº¯ng
        return noAccents.replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]", "").trim();
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.shipping.client.impl;

import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.example.smashhub.config.GhnConfig;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.AppException;
import org.example.smashhub.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.client.GhnClient;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnCreateOrderRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnCreateOrderResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnFeeCalculationRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnServiceInfo;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.client.SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.client.HttpClientErrorException;
import org.springframework.web.client.HttpServerErrorException;
import org.springframework.web.client.ResourceAccessException;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import tools.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

@Component
@Slf4j
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class GhnClientImpl implements GhnClient {

    GhnConfig ghnConfig;
    RestClient restClient;
    ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

    public GhnClientImpl(GhnConfig ghnConfig) {
        this.ghnConfig = ghnConfig;

        SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory factory = new SimpleClientHttpRequestFactory();
        factory.setConnectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10));
        factory.setReadTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15));

        this.restClient = RestClient.builder()
                .baseUrl(ghnConfig.getBaseUrl())
                .requestFactory(factory)
                .defaultHeader("Token", ghnConfig.getToken())
                .defaultHeader("ShopId", String.valueOf(ghnConfig.getShopId()))
                .defaultHeader(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_TYPE, MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public List<GhnServiceInfo> getAvailableServices(int fromDistrict, int toDistrict) {
        return executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = Map.of(
                    "shop_id", ghnConfig.getShopId(),
                    "from_district", fromDistrict,
                    "to_district", toDistrict
            );

            String response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/v2/shipping-order/available-services")
                    .body(body)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            List<GhnServiceInfo> services = new ArrayList<>();
            try {
                JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
                JsonNode data = root.get("data");
                if (data != null && data.isArray()) {
                    for (JsonNode s : data) {
                        services.add(GhnServiceInfo.builder()
                                .serviceId(s.get("service_id").asInt())
                                .shortName(s.has("short_name") ? s.get("short_name").asText() : "")
                                .serviceTypeId(s.has("service_type_id") ? s.get("service_type_id").asInt() : 2)
                                .build());
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Lá»—i parse available-services tá»« GHN: ", e);
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_SERVICE_NOT_AVAILABLE, "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c danh sÃ¡ch dá»‹ch vá»¥ GHN");
            }
            return services;
        });
    }

    @Override
    public BigDecimal calculateFee(GhnFeeCalculationRequest request) {
        return executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            body.put("from_district_id", request.getFromDistrictId());
            if (request.getFromWardCode() != null) body.put("from_ward_code", request.getFromWardCode());
            body.put("to_district_id", request.getToDistrictId());
            body.put("to_ward_code", request.getToWardCode());
            if (request.getServiceId() != null) body.put("service_id", request.getServiceId());
            if (request.getServiceTypeId() != null) body.put("service_type_id", request.getServiceTypeId());
            body.put("weight", request.getWeight() != null ? request.getWeight() : ghnConfig.getDefaultWeightGram());
            body.put("length", request.getLength() != null ? request.getLength() : ghnConfig.getDefaultLengthCm());
            body.put("width", request.getWidth() != null ? request.getWidth() : ghnConfig.getDefaultWidthCm());
            body.put("height", request.getHeight() != null ? request.getHeight() : ghnConfig.getDefaultHeightCm());
            if (request.getInsuranceValue() != null && request.getInsuranceValue().compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0) {
                body.put("insurance_value", request.getInsuranceValue().intValue());
            }

            String response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/v2/shipping-order/fee")
                    .body(body)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            try {
                JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
                JsonNode data = root.get("data");
                if (data != null && data.has("total")) {
                    return BigDecimal.valueOf(data.get("total").asDouble());
                }
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_FEE_CALCULATION_FAILED, "Dá»¯ liá»‡u cÆ°á»›c GHN tráº£ vá» khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
            } catch (AppException e) {
                throw e;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Lá»—i parse fee tá»« GHN: ", e);
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_FEE_CALCULATION_FAILED, e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    @Override
    public LocalDateTime getLeadtime(int fromDistrict, String fromWard, int toDistrict, String toWard, int serviceId) {
        return executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            body.put("from_district_id", fromDistrict);
            body.put("from_ward_code", fromWard);
            body.put("to_district_id", toDistrict);
            body.put("to_ward_code", toWard);
            body.put("service_id", serviceId);

            String response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/v2/shipping-order/leadtime")
                    .body(body)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            try {
                JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
                JsonNode data = root.get("data");
                if (data != null && data.has("leadtime")) {
                    long epoch = data.get("leadtime").asLong();
                    return LocalDateTime.ofInstant(Instant.ofEpochSecond(epoch), ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh"));
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("KhÃ´ng láº¥y Ä‘Æ°á»£c leadtime tá»« GHN: {}", e.getMessage());
            }
            // Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh sau 3 ngÃ y náº¿u khÃ´ng láº¥y Ä‘Æ°á»£c leadtime
            return LocalDateTime.now().plusDays(3);
        });
    }

    @Override
    public GhnCreateOrderResponse createOrder(GhnCreateOrderRequest request) {
        return executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            body.put("payment_type_id", 1); // 1: Shop tráº£ cÆ°á»›c (vÃ¬ Shop Ä‘Ã£ thu phÃ­ váº­n chuyá»ƒn cá»§a khÃ¡ch táº¡i checkout)
            body.put("note", request.getNote() != null ? request.getNote() : "SmashHub Order");
            body.put("required_note", "CHOXEMHANGKHONGTHU");
            body.put("client_order_code", request.getClientOrderCode());

            body.put("to_name", request.getToName());
            body.put("to_phone", request.getToPhone());
            body.put("to_address", request.getToAddress());
            body.put("to_district_id", request.getToDistrictId());
            body.put("to_ward_code", request.getToWardCode());

            body.put("weight", request.getWeight() != null ? request.getWeight() : ghnConfig.getDefaultWeightGram());
            body.put("length", request.getLength() != null ? request.getLength() : ghnConfig.getDefaultLengthCm());
            body.put("width", request.getWidth() != null ? request.getWidth() : ghnConfig.getDefaultWidthCm());
            body.put("height", request.getHeight() != null ? request.getHeight() : ghnConfig.getDefaultHeightCm());

            if (request.getServiceId() != null) body.put("service_id", request.getServiceId());
            if (request.getServiceTypeId() != null) body.put("service_type_id", request.getServiceTypeId());

            // COD amount: ÄÆ¡n COD thÃ¬ thu tá»•ng tiá»n; ÄÆ¡n VNPay Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ thÃ¬ thu 0Ä‘
            int cod = request.getCodAmount() != null ? request.getCodAmount().intValue() : 0;
            body.put("cod_amount", cod);

            if (request.getInsuranceValue() != null) {
                body.put("insurance_value", request.getInsuranceValue().intValue());
            }

            if (request.getItems() != null && !request.getItems().isEmpty()) {
                body.put("items", request.getItems());
            }

            String response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/v2/shipping-order/create")
                    .body(body)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            try {
                JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
                JsonNode data = root.get("data");
                if (data != null && data.has("order_code")) {
                    String orderCode = data.get("order_code").asText();
                    BigDecimal totalFee = data.has("total_fee") ? BigDecimal.valueOf(data.get("total_fee").asDouble()) : BigDecimal.ZERO;
                    LocalDateTime expected = null;
                    if (data.has("expected_delivery_time")) {
                        try {
                            String expStr = data.get("expected_delivery_time").asText();
                            expected = LocalDateTime.parse(expStr.substring(0, 19));
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {
                            expected = LocalDateTime.now().plusDays(3);
                        }
                    }

                    return GhnCreateOrderResponse.builder()
                            .orderCode(orderCode)
                            .totalFee(totalFee)
                            .expectedDeliveryDate(expected)
                            .rawResponse(response)
                            .build();
                }
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_ORDER_CREATION_FAILED, "GHN khÃ´ng tráº£ vá» mÃ£ váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n");
            } catch (AppException e) {
                throw e;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Lá»—i parse káº¿t quáº£ táº¡o Ä‘Æ¡n GHN: ", e);
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_ORDER_CREATION_FAILED, e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void cancelOrder(String orderCode) {
        executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = Map.of("order_codes", List.of(orderCode));
            try {
                restClient.post()
                        .uri("/v2/switch-status/cancel")
                        .body(body)
                        .retrieve()
                        .toBodilessEntity();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Lá»—i khi há»§y Ä‘Æ¡n GHN {}: {}", orderCode, e.getMessage());
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_ORDER_CANCEL_FAILED, e.getMessage());
            }
            return null;
        });
    }

    @Override
    public String getPrintToken(List<String> orderCodes) {
        return executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = Map.of("order_codes", orderCodes);
            String response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/v2/a5/gen-token")
                    .body(body)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            try {
                JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
                JsonNode data = root.get("data");
                if (data != null && data.has("token")) {
                    return data.get("token").asText();
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("Lá»—i láº¥y print token GHN: {}", e.getMessage());
            }
            return null;
        });
    }

    @Override
    public JsonNode getOrderDetail(String orderCode) {
        return executeWithRetry(() -> {
            Map<String, Object> body = Map.of("order_code", orderCode);
            String response = restClient.post()
                    .uri("/v2/shipping-order/detail")
                    .body(body)
                    .retrieve()
                    .body(String.class);

            try {
                JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
                return root.get("data");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Lá»—i Ä‘á»c chi tiáº¿t váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN {}: ", orderCode, e);
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_TRACKING_FAILED, e.getMessage());
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------------------- Retry Logic Helper ----------------------

    private <T> T executeWithRetry(Supplier<T> action) {
        int maxAttempts = 3;
        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
            try {
                return action.get();
            } catch (HttpClientErrorException e) {
                // 4xx: lá»—i nghiá»‡p vá»¥ tá»« client -> KHÃ”NG retry
                log.error("GHN API 4xx error (attempt {}): status={}, body={}", attempt, e.getStatusCode(), e.getResponseBodyAsString());
                throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_FEE_CALCULATION_FAILED, "Lá»—i tá»« dá»‹ch vá»¥ váº­n chuyá»ƒn: " + e.getResponseBodyAsString());
            } catch (HttpServerErrorException | ResourceAccessException e) {
                // 5xx hoáº·c Timeout/Connection error -> retry
                log.warn("GHN API call failure (attempt {}/{}): {}", attempt, maxAttempts, e.getMessage());
                if (attempt == maxAttempts) {
                    throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_FEE_CALCULATION_FAILED, "Dá»‹ch vá»¥ váº­n chuyá»ƒn GHN táº¡m thá»i khÃ´ng pháº£n há»“i. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i sau.");
                }
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(attempt * 500L); // backoff
                } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    throw new RuntimeException(ie);
                }
            }
        }
        throw new AppException(ErrorCode.SHIPPING_FEE_CALCULATION_FAILED, "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i tá»›i GHN");
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.shipping.client;

import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnCreateOrderRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnCreateOrderResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnFeeCalculationRequest;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnServiceInfo;
import tools.jackson.databind.JsonNode;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

public interface GhnClient {

    List<GhnServiceInfo> getAvailableServices(int fromDistrict, int toDistrict);

    BigDecimal calculateFee(GhnFeeCalculationRequest request);

    LocalDateTime getLeadtime(int fromDistrict, String fromWard, int toDistrict, String toWard, int serviceId);

    GhnCreateOrderResponse createOrder(GhnCreateOrderRequest request);

    void cancelOrder(String orderCode);

    String getPrintToken(List<String> orderCodes);

    JsonNode getOrderDetail(String orderCode);
}

package org.example.smashhub.shipping.controller;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.response.ApiResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.GhnWebhookPayload;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.ShipmentTrackingResponse;
import org.example.smashhub.shipping.dto.ShippingFeeRequest;

import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE, makeFinal = true)
public class ShippingController {

    ShippingService shippingService;

    private String currentUsernameOrNull() {
        Authentication auth = SecurityContextHolder.getContext().getAuthentication();
        return (auth != null && auth.isAuthenticated() && !"anonymousUser".equals(auth.getName()))
                ? auth.getName() : null;
    }

    @PostMapping("/shipping/fee")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<ShippingFeeResponse>> calculateFee(
            @RequestBody @Valid ShippingFeeRequest request) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<ShippingFeeResponse>builder()
                .result(shippingService.calculateFee(currentUsernameOrNull(), request))
                .message("TÃ­nh phÃ­ váº­n chuyá»ƒn thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }

    @PostMapping("/shipping/ghn/webhook")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<String>> handleGhnWebhook(
            @RequestHeader(value = "Token", required = false) String token,
            @RequestBody GhnWebhookPayload payload) {
        shippingService.handleWebhook(token, payload);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<String>builder()
                .result("OK")
                .message("Webhook processed successfully")
                .build());
    }

    @GetMapping("/orders/{orderId}/tracking")
    public ResponseEntity<ApiResponse<ShipmentTrackingResponse>> getTrackingInfo(
            @PathVariable Long orderId) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(ApiResponse.<ShipmentTrackingResponse>builder()
                .result(shippingService.getTrackingInfo(orderId, currentUsernameOrNull()))
                .message("Láº¥y thÃ´ng tin hÃ nh trÃ¬nh Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng thÃ nh cÃ´ng")
                .build());
    }
}

package org.example.smashhub.shipping.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.Column;
import jakarta.persistence.Entity;
import jakarta.persistence.FetchType;
import jakarta.persistence.JoinColumn;
import jakarta.persistence.Lob;
import jakarta.persistence.OneToOne;
import jakarta.persistence.Table;
import lombok.AccessLevel;
import lombok.AllArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Builder;
import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.NoArgsConstructor;
import lombok.Setter;
import lombok.ToString;
import lombok.experimental.FieldDefaults;
import org.example.smashhub.common.persistence.BaseEntity;
import org.example.smashhub.order.entity.Order;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * [Má»šI] Báº£ng lÆ°u váº¿t thÃ´ng tin váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c táº¡o qua dá»‹ch vá»¥ váº­n chuyá»ƒn (GHN).
 * Quan há»‡ 1-1 vá»›i Order.
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Builder
@NoArgsConstructor
@AllArgsConstructor
@ToString(callSuper = true, exclude = {"order"})
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
@Entity
@Table(name = "shipments")
@FieldDefaults(level = AccessLevel.PRIVATE)
public class Shipment extends BaseEntity {

    @OneToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "order_id", nullable = false, unique = true)
    Order order;

    @Column(name = "order_code", length = 50, unique = true)
    String orderCode; // MÃ£ váº­n Ä‘Æ¡n GHN tráº£ vá» (vd: GY45SD)

    @Column(name = "client_order_code", length = 100, nullable = false)
    String clientOrderCode; // MÃ£ Ä‘Æ¡n hÃ ng há»‡ thá»‘ng gá»­i lÃªn GHN Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t (vd: ORDER-1001)

    @Column(name = "service_id")
    Integer serviceId;

    @Column(name = "service_type_id")
    Integer serviceTypeId;

    @Column(name = "fee", precision = 15, scale = 2)
    BigDecimal fee; // CÆ°á»›c phÃ­ thá»±c táº¿ GHN bÃ¡o

    @Column(name = "cod_amount", precision = 15, scale = 2)
    BigDecimal codAmount; // Tiá»n thu há»™: COD = order.total, VNPAY = 0

    @Column(name = "expected_delivery_date")
    LocalDateTime expectedDeliveryDate;

    @Column(name = "status", length = 50)
    String status; // Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i GHN: ready_to_pick, picked, delivering, delivered, cancel...

    @Column(name = "status_name", length = 100)
    String statusName;

    @Column(name = "failure_reason")
    String failureReason;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.LONGVARCHAR)
    @Column(name = "raw_response", columnDefinition = "LONGTEXT")
    String rawResponse;
}
